📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Vaccine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-20  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.vaccine.2026.129244  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 94, 129244

人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 在年轻女性中引入后 17 年的交叉保护和类型替代

Cross-protection and type-replacement over the 17 years after human papillomavirus vaccine introduction in young women
作者： Jessica A. Kahn, Aaron C. Ermel, Lili Ding, Aislinn DeSieghardt, Sem Yao, Darron R. Brown, Casey L. Dagnall, Amrita M. Bhatia, Eduardo Luis Franco
摘要中文翻译背景:本研究的目的是调查美国 HPV 疫苗 引入年轻女性后 17 年内非 4 价和非 9 价 疫苗 型 HPV 检测的趋势。方法:我们从 2006 年至 2023 年从医院和社区诊所招募了 13-26 岁有性经验的女性 (N = 2335) 进行六项 监测 研究,并通过罗氏线性阵列或 TypeSeq2 检测 HPV DNA。结果变量为:1) 潜在致癌的 alpha-9 和 -7 非 疫苗 型 HPV 与 HPV16 或 18 遗传相关,但不是 4 价或 9 价 疫苗 的目标(以评估交叉保护)和 2) 潜在致癌的 alpha-5、-6 和 - 11 非 疫苗 型 HPV 不紧密与 4 价或 9 价 疫苗 所针对的类型在遗传上相关(以评估类型替换)。我们使用 治疗 加权的逆概率和倾向得分来平衡 患病率 估计中参与者特征的波间差异以及使用 监测 研究作为连续变量的线性趋势测试。结果:在所有接种疫苗和未接种疫苗的参与者中,至少 1 种与 HPV16 和/或 HPV18 遗传相关但不是 4 价或 9 价 疫苗 靶向的非 疫苗 型 HPV 的 患病率 没有显着趋势。与 4 价或 9 价 疫苗 所针对的类型遗传无关的非 疫苗 型 HPV 的阳性率从 2006 年到 2015 年保持稳定,但从 2016 年到 2023 年有所增加;这一趋势对所有群体都很重要。结论:我们没有找到针对 4 价或 9 价 疫苗 不针对的类型的交叉保护的证据,但确实发现了可能出现的类型替代的信号。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查年轻女性中引入 HPV 疫苗 后最初 17 年内非 4 价和非 9 价 疫苗 型 HPV 检测的趋势,以评估交叉保护和类型替代。
设计与对象
该研究从医院和社区诊所招募了 13-26 岁有性经历的女性 (N = 2335),进行 6 项 监测 研究,研究时间为 2006 年至 2023 年,使用罗氏线性阵列或 TypeSeq2 检测 HPV DNA。
核心方法
结果变量包括与 HPV16 或 18 遗传相关但不被 4 价或 9 价 疫苗 靶向的潜在致癌 α-9 和-7 非 疫苗 型 HPV,以及与 疫苗 靶向类型不密切相关的潜在致癌性 α-5、-6 和 -11 非 疫苗 型 HPV。使用倾向得分的 治疗 加权的逆概率来平衡参与者特征的波间差异。
主要结果
在所有接种疫苗和未接种疫苗的参与者中,与 HPV16 和/或 HPV18 遗传相关的非 疫苗 型 HPV 的 患病率 没有显着趋势。从 2006 年到 2015 年,与 疫苗 目标类型无关的非 疫苗 型 HPV 的阳性率保持稳定,但从 2016 年到 2023 年有所增加,在所有组中都观察到了显着的趋势。
解释与创新
作者没有找到针对非目标 HPV 类型的交叉保护的证据,但观察到了新兴类型替代的可能信号。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,4 价和 9 价 HPV 疫苗 可能无法针对非靶向高危类型提供交叉保护,但可能与与 疫苗 靶向类型无关的非 疫苗 型 HPV 感染增加相关,这可能表明类型替代。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到其观察设计和潜在混杂因素的限制,但证据是强有力的,因为它基于大型纵向数据集,并且在所有组中观察到了清晰的趋势。

Cross-protection and type-replacement over the 17 years after human papillomavirus vaccine introduction in young women

Authors: Jessica A. Kahn, Aaron C. Ermel, Lili Ding, Aislinn DeSieghardt, Sem Yao, Darron R. Brown, Casey L. Dagnall, Amrita M. Bhatia, Eduardo Luis Franco
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: The study aim was to examine trends in non-4-valent and non-9-valent vaccine-type HPV detection over the first 17 years after U.S. HPV vaccine introduction among young women. METHODS: We recruited 13-26 year-old sexually experienced women (N = 2335) from hospital and community clinics for six surveillance studies from 2006 to 2023, with testing for HPV DNA by Roche Linear Array or TypeSeq2. Outcome variables were: 1) potentially carcinogenic alpha-9 and -7 non-vaccine-type HPV genetically related to HPV16 or 18 but not targeted by the 4- or 9-valent vaccines (to assess for cross-protection) and 2) potentially carcinogenic alpha-5, -6, and - 11 non-vaccine-type HPV not closely genetically related to types targeted by the 4- or 9-valent vaccines (to assess for type replacement). We used inverse probability of treatment weighting with propensity score to balance between-wave differences in participant characteristics in prevalence estimates and in tests of linear trend using surveillance study as a continuous variable. RESULTS: Among all, vaccinated, and unvaccinated participants, there were no significant trends in the prevalence of at least 1 non-vaccine-type HPV genetically related to HPV16 and/or HPV18 but not targeted by the 4- or 9-valent vaccines. Positivity for non-vaccine-type HPV genetically unrelated to types targeted by the 4- or 9-valent vaccines remained stable from 2006 to 2015 but increased from 2016 to 2023; the trend was significant for all groups. CONCLUSIONS: We did not find evidence for cross-protection against types not targeted by the 4-valent or 9-valent vaccines, but did find a signal for possible emerging type replacement.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to examine trends in non-4-valent and non-9-valent vaccine-type HPV detection over the first 17 years after HPV vaccine introduction among young women, to assess cross-protection and type replacement.
Design and population
The study recruited 13-26 year-old sexually experienced women (N = 2335) from hospital and community clinics for six surveillance studies conducted from 2006 to 2023, with testing for HPV DNA using Roche Linear Array or TypeSeq2.
Core methods
Outcome variables included potentially carcinogenic alpha-9 and -7 non-vaccine-type HPV genetically related to HPV16 or 18 but not targeted by the 4- or 9-valent vaccines, and potentially carcinogenic alpha-5, -6, and -11 non-vaccine-type HPV not closely related to vaccine-targeted types. Inverse probability of treatment weighting with propensity score was used to balance between-wave differences in participant characteristics.
Main results
Among all, vaccinated, and unvaccinated participants, there were no significant trends in the prevalence of non-vaccine-type HPV genetically related to HPV16 and/or HPV18. Positivity for non-vaccine-type HPV genetically unrelated to vaccine-targeted types remained stable from 2006 to 2015 but increased from 2016 to 2023, with a significant trend observed across all groups.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors did not find evidence for cross-protection against non-targeted HPV types, but observed a possible signal for emerging type replacement.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the 4- and 9-valent HPV vaccines may not provide cross-protection against non-targeted high-risk types, but may be associated with an increase in non-vaccine-type HPV infections unrelated to vaccine-targeted types, potentially indicating type replacement.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and potential for confounding factors, but the evidence is strong as it is based on a large, longitudinal dataset with a clear trend observed across all groups.

智利全国范围内高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 和宫颈细胞学结果的 患病率:一项基于登记的研究(2019-2024 年)

Nationwide prevalence of high-risk human papillomavirus and cervical cytology findings in Chile: a registry-based study (2019–2024)
作者： Haylyn Sarria, Giuliano Bernal
摘要中文翻译宫颈癌 主要由高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 引起,仍然是全球女性癌症相关死亡的主要原因之一。目前还没有全国性的分析使用基于登记的数据将分子 HPV 检测和宫颈细胞学结合起来。本研究的目的是在智利国家 宫颈癌 筛查计划中提供第一个基于全国登记的 HR-HPV 阳性率和年龄特异性宫颈细胞学异常的估计。使用智利卫生部 (MINSAL) 的全国汇总数据进行回顾性描述性分析,涵盖全国 宫颈癌 筛查网络。总共分析了 506 712 份宫颈样本进行 HR-HPV 检测(2020-2024 年)和 4 055 738 份充分巴氏(PAP)检测(2019-2024 年)。2020年至2024年HR-HPV阳性率为13.02%(95%置信区间:12.93-13.11%),2020年、2021年、2022年、2023年和2023年的年值为12.34、12.73、13.27、13.47和12.85%。分别为2024年。HPV 患病率 最高出现在 G1(30-44 岁),范围为 15.39-16.26%,而 G2(45-64 岁)为 8.95-10.55%,G3(≥65 岁)为 5.46-13.30%。在所有 HPV 阳性女性中,HPV-16 占感染的 19.57%,HPV-18 占 6.39%,汇总 HR-HPV 类型占 74.03%。关于 PAP 结果,95.63% 的适当检查被分类为上皮内病变或恶性肿瘤阴性,而低度鳞状上皮内病变 (LSIL) (NIE I) 占 0.77%,高度鳞状上皮内病变 (HSIL) (NIE II) 占 0.33%,重度 HSIL (NIE III) 占 0.26%。这项全国性的登记研究在智利建立了第一个 HR-HPV 阳性和宫颈细胞学综合基线。非 16/18 h-HPV 感染的主导地位强化了广谱扩展 HPV 基因分型 监测 的重要性,并为评估 HPV 监测 的长期影响和实施初级 HPV 筛查提供了基准。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 主要由高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 引起,仍然是全球女性癌症相关死亡的主要原因之一。目前还没有全国性的分析使用基于登记的数据将分子 HPV 检测和宫颈细胞学结合起来。
设计与对象
使用智利卫生部 (MINSAL) 的全国汇总数据进行回顾性描述性分析,涵盖全国 宫颈癌 筛查网络。总共分析了 506 712 份宫颈样本进行 HR-HPV 检测(2020-2024 年)和 4 055 738 份充分巴氏(PAP)检测(2019-2024 年)。
核心方法
本研究的目的是在智利国家 宫颈癌 筛查计划中提供第一个基于全国登记的 HR-HPV 阳性率和年龄特异性宫颈细胞学异常的估计。
主要结果
2020年至2024年HR-HPV阳性率为13.02%(95%置信区间:12.93-13.11%),2020年、2021年、2022年、2023年和2023年的年值为12.34、12.73、13.27、13.47和12.85%。分别为2024年。HPV 患病率 最高出现在 G1(30-44 岁),范围为 15.39-16.26%,而 G2(45-64 岁)为 8.95-10.55%,G3(≥65 岁)为 5.46-13.30%。在所有 HPV 阳性女性中,HPV-16 占感染的 19.57%,HPV-18 占 6.39%,汇总 HR-HPV 类型占 74.03%。
解释与创新
非 16/18 h-HPV 感染的主导地位强化了广谱扩展 HPV 基因分型 监测 的重要性,并为评估 HPV 监测 的长期影响和实施初级 HPV 筛查提供了基准。
科研与公卫意义
这项全国性的登记研究在智利建立了第一个 HR-HPV 阳性和宫颈细胞学综合基线。研究结果可为针对性 HPV 监测 策略的制定以及 疫苗接种 计划和筛查方案等公共卫生干预措施的评估提供信息。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。证据强度中等,因为该研究基于登记数据,不包括详细的统计分析或控制。

Nationwide prevalence of high-risk human papillomavirus and cervical cytology findings in Chile: a registry-based study (2019–2024)

Authors: Haylyn Sarria, Giuliano Bernal
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer, primarily caused by high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV), remains one of the leading causes of cancer-related death in women globally. No nationwide analysis has integrated molecular HPV testing and cervical cytology using registry-based data. The aim of the present study was to provide the first nationwide registry-based estimate of HR-HPV positivity and age-specific cervical cytological abnormalities within the Chilean National Cervical Cancer Screening Program. A retrospective descriptive analysis was conducted using nationwide aggregate data from the Chilean Ministry of Health (MINSAL), covering the nationwide cervical cancer screening network. A total of 506 712 cervical samples tested for HR-HPV (2020–2024) and 4 055 738 adequate Papanicolaou (PAP) tests (2019–2024) were analyzed. The HR-HPV positivity between 2020 and 2024 was 13.02% (95% confidence interval: 12.93–13.11%), with annual values of 12.34, 12.73, 13.27, 13.47, and 12.85% in 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, and 2024, respectively. The highest HPV prevalence was observed in G1 (30–44 years), ranging from 15.39 to 16.26%, compared with 8.95–10.55% in G2 (45–64 years) and 5.46–13.30% in G3 (≥65 years). Among all HPV-positive women, HPV-16 accounted for 19.57% of infections, HPV-18 for 6.39%, and pooled HR-HPV types for 74.03%. Regarding PAP results, 95.63% of adequate tests were classified as negative for intraepithelial lesion or malignancy, while low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (LSIL) (NIE I) was found in 0.77%, high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion (HSIL) (NIE II) in 0.33%, and severe HSIL (NIE III) in 0.26% of cases. This nationwide registry-based study establishes the first comprehensive baseline of HR-HPV positivity and cervical cytology in Chile. The predominance of non-16/18 h-HPV infections reinforces the importance of broad-spectrum extended HPV genotyping surveillance and provides a benchmark for evaluating the long-term impact of HPV vaccination and implementation of primary HPV screening.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer, primarily caused by high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV), remains one of the leading causes of cancer-related death in women globally. No nationwide analysis has integrated molecular HPV testing and cervical cytology using registry-based data.
Design and population
A retrospective descriptive analysis was conducted using nationwide aggregate data from the Chilean Ministry of Health (MINSAL), covering the nationwide cervical cancer screening network. A total of 506 712 cervical samples tested for HR-HPV (2020–2024) and 4 055 738 adequate Papanicolaou (PAP) tests (2019–2024) were analyzed.
Core methods
The aim of the present study was to provide the first nationwide registry-based estimate of HR-HPV positivity and age-specific cervical cytological abnormalities within the Chilean National Cervical Cancer Screening Program.
Main results
The HR-HPV positivity between 2020 and 2024 was 13.02% (95% confidence interval: 12.93–13.11%), with annual values of 12.34, 12.73, 13.27, 13.47, and 12.85% in 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, and 2024, respectively. The highest HPV prevalence was observed in G1 (30–44 years), ranging from 15.39 to 16.26%, compared with 8.95–10.55% in G2 (45–64 years) and 5.46–13.30% in G3 (≥65 years). Among all HPV-positive women, HPV-16 accounted for 19.57% of infections, HPV-18 for 6.39%, and pooled HR-HPV types for 74.03%.
Interpretation and novelty
The predominance of non-16/18 h-HPV infections reinforces the importance of broad-spectrum extended HPV genotyping surveillance and provides a benchmark for evaluating the long-term impact of HPV vaccination and implementation of primary HPV screening.
Scientific and public-health significance
This nationwide registry-based study establishes the first comprehensive baseline of HR-HPV positivity and cervical cytology in Chile. The findings can inform the development of targeted HPV surveillance strategies and the evaluation of public health interventions such as vaccination programs and screening protocols.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design. The evidence strength is moderate, as the study is based on registry data and does not include detailed statistical analyses or controls.

人乳头瘤病毒 归因的头颈癌的时间趋势:BROADEN 研究

Time trends of human papillomavirus-attributable head and neck cancers: the BROADEN study
作者： Tim Waterboer, Fang-Hui Zhao, Anna R. Giuliano, Miquel Àngel Pavón, You-Lin Qiao, Haïtham Mirghani, Hisham Mohamed Mehanna, Ken-Ichi Nibu, Nobuhiko Oridate, Shao-Kai Zhang
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 发病率 或 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 驱动的口咽癌 (OPC) 在几个西方国家正在增加,但这种趋势在其他地理区域和其他头颈癌 (HNC) 中尚不清楚。BROADEN(HPV 相关头颈的绝对负担)研究评估了法国、德国、意大利、葡萄牙、西班牙、中国和日本的 HNC(包括 OPC)的 HPV 归因分数 (HPV-AF),历时 10 年。方法 这项回顾性研究纳入了 2008-2009 年和 2018-2019 年两个时期诊断为 HNC(口咽癌、口腔癌、鼻咽癌、下咽癌和喉癌)的患者。福尔马林固定的组织样本集中检测 HPV-DNA、HPV-E6*I mRNA 和 p16INK4a 免疫组织化学。HPV 归因要求 OPC 的三项测试中有两项呈阳性结果,而其他 HNC 的 HPV-DNA 和 HPV-E6*I mRNA 均呈阳性。结果 在来自 88 家医院的 6043 名患者中,OPC 中的 HPV-AF 在所有国家均呈上升趋势,2008-2009 年葡萄牙的 HPV-AF 为 0%,日本为 44.2%,2018-2019 年分别为 20.0% 和 51.7%。除中国和日本外,所有国家的增长趋势均达到0.05的统计显着性。2008-2009 年,日本的 HPV-AF 最高。其他 HNC 显示较低的 HPV-AF (≤5%),无时间趋势。结论 BROADEN 是迄今为止第一项也是规模最大的研究,旨在调查 7 个国家的 5 个 HNC 中 HPV 因果关系及其时间趋势。这些结果支持了解 HPV-AF 的时间趋势和地理变异,对于全球 HNC 预防和 治疗 至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解西方国家 HPV 驱动的口咽癌 发病率 的增加及其在其他地理区域和其他头颈癌的趋势。
设计与对象
BROADEN 研究使用来自 88 家医院 6043 名患者的组织样本,评估了 10 年来法国、德国、意大利、葡萄牙、西班牙、中国和日本的头颈癌 (HNC) 包括口咽癌 (OPC) 中 HPV 所致的部分。
核心方法
这项回顾性研究纳入了 2008-2009 年和 2018-2019 年两个时期诊断为 HNC 的患者,并使用福尔马林固定的组织样本集中检测 HPV-DNA、HPV-E6*I mRNA 和 p16INK4a 免疫组织化学。
主要结果
在 6043 名患者中,所有国家 OPC 中的 HPV-AF 均有所增加,从 2008-2009 年葡萄牙的 0% 到日本的 44.2%,以及 2018-2019 年的 20.0% 到 51.7%。其他 HNC 显示较低的 HPV-AF (≤5%),无时间趋势。
解释与创新
该研究重点介绍了迄今为止关于 7 个国家的 5 个 HNC 中 HPV 因果关系及其时间趋势的首次也是规模最大的调查,为 HPV-AF 的地理变异提供了重要见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果对于了解 HPV 归因分数的时间趋势和地理变化至关重要,为头颈癌的预防和 治疗 的全球策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
在除中国和日本之外的所有国家中,OPC 的 HPV-AF 的增加趋势均达到统计显着性(0.05),表明对研究结果有一定的信心。

Time trends of human papillomavirus-attributable head and neck cancers: the BROADEN study

Authors: Tim Waterboer, Fang-Hui Zhao, Anna R. Giuliano, Miquel Àngel Pavón, You-Lin Qiao, Haïtham Mirghani, Hisham Mohamed Mehanna, Ken-Ichi Nibu, Nobuhiko Oridate, Shao-Kai Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background The incidence of human papillomavirus (HPV)-driven oropharyngeal cancer (OPC) is increasing in several Western countries, but this trend is less well understood in other geographical regions and for other head and neck cancers (HNC). The BROADEN (absolute BuRden Of HPV relAteD hEad and Neck) study assessed HPV-attributable fractions (HPV-AFs) of HNC including OPC in France, Germany, Italy, Portugal, Spain, China, and Japan over a 10-year period. Methods This retrospective study included patients diagnosed with HNC (oropharynx, oral cavity, nasopharynx, hypopharynx, and larynx cancer) in two time periods, 2008–2009 and 2018–2019. Formalin-fixed tissue samples were centrally tested for HPV-DNA, HPV-E6*I mRNA, and p16INK4a immunohistochemistry. HPV attributability required positive results in two of the three tests for OPC, and both HPV-DNA and HPV-E6*I mRNA positivity for other HNC. Results Among 6043 patients from 88 hospitals, the HPV-AF in OPC shown an increasing trend across all countries, with an HPV-AF ranging between 0% in Portugal and 44.2% in Japan in 2008-2009, and 20.0% and 51.7%, respectively, in 2018-2019. The increasing trend reached the statistical significance at 0.05 in all countries except China and Japan. Japan had the highest HPV-AF in 2008-2009. Other HNC showed lower HPV-AF (≤5%) with no time trends. Conclusion BROADEN is the first and largest study to date to investigate HPV causality and its time trends across five HNCs in seven countries. These results support the understanding of time trends and geographic variation in HPV-AF and are essential for global HNC prevention and treatment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the increasing incidence of HPV-driven oropharyngeal cancer in Western countries and its trends in other geographical regions and for other head and neck cancers.
Design and population
The BROADEN study assessed HPV-attributable fractions of head and neck cancers (HNC) including oropharyngeal cancer (OPC) in France, Germany, Italy, Portugal, Spain, China, and Japan over a 10-year period, using tissue samples from 6043 patients across 88 hospitals.
Core methods
This retrospective study included patients diagnosed with HNC in two time periods, 2008–2009 and 2018–2019, and used formalin-fixed tissue samples tested centrally for HPV-DNA, HPV-E6*I mRNA, and p16INK4a immunohistochemistry.
Main results
Among 6043 patients, the HPV-AF in OPC increased across all countries, ranging from 0% in Portugal to 44.2% in Japan in 2008–2009, and from 20.0% to 51.7% in 2018–2019. Other HNC showed lower HPV-AF (≤5%) with no time trends.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the first and largest investigation to date on HPV causality and its time trends across five HNCs in seven countries, providing critical insights into geographic variation in HPV-AF.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results are essential for understanding time trends and geographic variation in HPV-attributable fractions, informing global strategies for the prevention and treatment of head and neck cancers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The increasing trend in HPV-AF for OPC reached statistical significance at 0.05 in all countries except China and Japan, indicating moderate confidence in the findings.

含有 HPV16 E7 的外泌体可能有助于 宫颈癌 肿瘤浸润淋巴细胞中 PD-L1 的表达

HPV16 E7-Containing Exosomes May Contribute to PD-L1 Expression in Tumor-Infiltrating Lymphocytes of Cervical Cancer
作者： Ya-Jing Zhang, Guqun Shen, Li Luo, Yang-Chun Feng, Sen-Yu Wang
摘要中文翻译背景 人乳头瘤病毒 16 (HPV16) 在 宫颈癌 (CC) 的发展中发挥着重要作用。肿瘤浸润淋巴细胞 (TIL) 中的程序性细胞死亡配体 1 (PD-L1) 表达是肿瘤免疫微环境的重要特征,可能影响对免疫检查点阻断的反应。然而,TIL 中 HPV16 E7 癌蛋白与 PD-L1 表达之间的关系仍不清楚。目的 研究来自 CC 组织和血浆的外泌体中 HPV16 E7 蛋白的存在,确定外泌体 E7 与 TIL 中 PD-L1 表达之间的关联,并探讨富含 E7 的肿瘤源性外泌体是否有助于 TIL 中 PD-L1 表达增加。研究设计本研究结合了临床观察分析和体外实验。分析了三个 CC 单细胞 RNA 测序数据集、30 例 HPV16 阳性 队列研究 患者和 104 例患者组织微阵列 队列研究。进行体外实验以进一步检查 TIL 中外泌体 E7 和 PD-L1 表达之间的潜在关系。方法收集30例HPV16阳性CC患者的血液和病理样本,以及C33A和SiHa CC细胞系的培养液。分离外泌体,并评估 E7 蛋白表达。对 CC 单细胞 RNA 测序数据集进行生物信息学分析,以评估 TIL 中 CD274 mRNA 的表达。使用免疫组织化学 (IHC) 评估 30 名患者 队列研究 中 TIL 中的 PD-L1 蛋白表达,同时使用多重免疫荧光评估 104 名患者组织微阵列中 TIL 中 E7 和 PD-L1 的表达。使用共聚焦显微镜评估 TIL 对外泌体的摄取。将 TIL 与外泌体共培养,通过定量逆转录聚合酶链反应(qRT-PCR)和蛋白质印迹法评估 TIL 空白对照、共培养空白对照、共培养空 媒介 和共培养 E7 过表达组中 HPV16 E7 和 PD-L1 的表达情况。结果 30 例患者中有 28 例 (93.3%) 的外泌体和 SiHa 细胞培养物的外泌体中检测到 E7 蛋白。PD-L1在TILs中表达,阳性率为43.3%(13/30)。在组织微阵列 队列研究(n = 103;28 个事件)中,较高的 CD3 + PD-L1 + /CD3 + 表达与较差的生存率相关(HR = 2.97,p = 0.009)。在二分分析中,CD3 + E7 + /CD3 + 表达与较差的生存率呈边缘相关(HR = 2.15,p = 0.052),但当建模为连续变量时,与生存率显着相关(每 10%:HR = 1.28,p = 0.002)。在 30 名患者的 队列研究 中,外泌体 E7 蛋白含量与 TIL 中的 PD-L1 表达显着相关(Cramér V = 0.728,p = 0.0001)。qRT-PCR 和蛋白质印迹证明,暴露于来自 E7 过表达细胞的外泌体的 TIL 中 PD-L1 mRNA 和蛋白表达增加。结论 HPV16 E7蛋白存在于来自CC患者和HPV16阳性CC细胞的外泌体中。较高的外泌体 E7 水平与 TIL 中 PD-L1 表达增加相关,而暴露于富含 E7 的外泌体与 TIL 中 PD-L1 表达上调相关。这些发现表明,外泌体 E7 可能有助于 TIL 中的 PD-L1 调节,并可能与 CC 患者较差的预后相关。需要进一步的研究来确定 E7 是否直接介导这种作用并阐明其功能和临床意义。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景 人乳头瘤病毒 16 (HPV16) 在 宫颈癌 (CC) 的发展中发挥着重要作用。HPV16 E7 癌蛋白与肿瘤浸润淋巴细胞 (TIL) 中 PD-L1 表达之间的关系仍不清楚。
设计与对象
本研究结合了临床观察分析和体外实验。对三个 宫颈癌 单细胞 RNA 测序数据集、30 例 HPV16 阳性 队列研究 和 104 例患者组织微阵列 队列研究 进行了分析。
核心方法
收集了 30 名 HPV16 阳性 宫颈癌 患者的血液和病理样本,以及 C33A 和 SiHa 宫颈癌 细胞系的培养液。分离外泌体,并评估 E7 蛋白表达。使用免疫组织化学 (IHC) 评估 30 名患者 队列研究 中 TIL 中的 PD-L1 蛋白表达,同时使用多重免疫荧光评估 104 名患者组织微阵列中 TIL 中 E7 和 PD-L1 的表达。使用共聚焦显微镜评估 TIL 对外泌体的摄取。
主要结果
在 30 名患者中 28 名 (93.3%) 的外泌体和 SiHa 细胞培养物的外泌体中检测到 E7 蛋白。在组织微阵列 队列研究(n = 103;28 个事件)中,较高的 CD3 + PD-L1 + /CD3 + 表达与较差的生存率相关(HR = 2.97,p = 0.009)。在二分分析中,CD3 + E7 + /CD3 + 表达与较差的生存率呈边缘相关(HR = 2.15,p = 0.052),但当建模为连续变量时,与生存率显着相关(每 10%:HR = 1.28,p = 0.002)。在 30 名患者的 队列研究 中,外泌体 E7 蛋白含量与 TIL 中的 PD-L1 表达显着相关(Cramér V = 0.728,p = 0.0001)。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,外泌体 E7 可能有助于 TIL 中的 PD-L1 调节,并可能与 宫颈癌 患者较差的预后相关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为 HPV16 阳性 宫颈癌 的靶向治疗的开发提供信息,并提高对这种情况下免疫检查点阻断反应的理解。
局限与证据强度
需要进一步的研究来确定 E7 是否直接介导这种作用并阐明其功能和临床意义。由于该研究的观察性质以及需要额外的机制验证,证据强度适中。

HPV16 E7-Containing Exosomes May Contribute to PD-L1 Expression in Tumor-Infiltrating Lymphocytes of Cervical Cancer

Authors: Ya-Jing Zhang, Guqun Shen, Li Luo, Yang-Chun Feng, Sen-Yu Wang
Original abstract (English)
Background Human papillomavirus 16 (HPV16) plays a major role in the development of cervical cancer (CC). Programmed cell death ligand 1 (PD-L1) expression in tumor-infiltrating lymphocytes (TILs) is an important feature of the tumor immune microenvironment and may affect responses to immune checkpoint blockade. However, the relationship between the HPV16 E7 oncoprotein and PD-L1 expression in TILs remains unclear. Aim To investigate the presence of HPV16 E7 protein in exosomes from CC tissues and plasma, determine the association between exosomal E7 and PD-L1 expression in TILs, and explore whether E7-rich tumor-derived exosomes contribute to increased PD-L1 expression in TILs. Study design This study combined clinical observational analyses and in vitro experiments. Three CC single-cell RNA sequencing datasets, a 30-patient HPV16-positive cohort, and a 104-patient tissue microarray cohort were analyzed. In vitro experiments were conducted to further examine the potential relationship between exosomal E7 and PD-L1 expression in TILs. Methods Blood and pathological samples from 30 patients with HPV16-positive CC, as well as culture fluid from the C33A and SiHa CC cell lines, were collected. Exosomes were isolated, and E7 protein expression was assessed. Bioinformatics analyses of CC single-cell RNA sequencing datasets were performed to evaluate CD274 mRNA expression in TILs. Immunohistochemistry (IHC) was used to assess PD-L1 protein expression in TILs in the 30-patient cohort, while multiplex immunofluorescence was used to evaluate E7 and PD-L1 expression in TILs in a 104-patient tissue microarray. Exosome uptake by TILs was assessed using confocal microscopy. TILs were co-cultured with exosomes, and HPV16 E7 and PD-L1 expression was evaluated by quantitative reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (qRT-PCR) and western blotting in the TIL blank control, co-culture blank control, co-culture empty vector, and co-culture E7-overexpression groups. Results E7 protein was detected in exosomes from the peripheral blood of 28 of 30 patients (93.3%) and in exosomes from SiHa cell culture. PD-L1 was expressed in TILs, with a positive rate of 43.3% (13/30). In the tissue microarray cohort (n = 103; 28 events), higher CD3 + PD-L1 + /CD3 + expression was associated with poorer survival (HR = 2.97, p = 0.009). CD3 + E7 + /CD3 + expression showed a borderline association with poorer survival in the dichotomized analysis (HR = 2.15, p = 0.052) but was significantly associated with survival when modeled as a continuous variable (per 10%: HR = 1.28, p = 0.002). In the 30-patient cohort, exosomal E7 protein content was significantly associated with PD-L1 expression in TILs (Cramér's V = 0.728, p = 0.0001). qRT-PCR and western blotting demonstrated increased PD-L1 mRNA and protein expression in TILs exposed to exosomes from E7-overexpressing cells. Conclusion HPV16 E7 protein is present in exosomes from patients with CC and HPV16-positive CC cells. Higher exosomal E7 levels are associated with increased PD-L1 expression in TILs, and exposure to E7-rich exosomes is associated with upregulated PD-L1 expression in TILs. These findings suggest that exosomal E7 may contribute to PD-L1 regulation in TILs and may be associated with poorer prognosis in patients with CC. Further studies are needed to determine whether E7 directly mediates this effect and to clarify its functional and clinical significance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Background Human papillomavirus 16 (HPV16) plays a major role in the development of cervical cancer (CC). The relationship between the HPV16 E7 oncoprotein and PD-L1 expression in tumor-infiltrating lymphocytes (TILs) remains unclear.
Design and population
This study combined clinical observational analyses and in vitro experiments. Three cervical cancer single-cell RNA sequencing datasets, a 30-patient HPV16-positive cohort, and a 104-patient tissue microarray cohort were analyzed.
Core methods
Blood and pathological samples from 30 patients with HPV16-positive cervical cancer, as well as culture fluid from the C33A and SiHa cervical cancer cell lines, were collected. Exosomes were isolated, and E7 protein expression was assessed. Immunohistochemistry (IHC) was used to assess PD-L1 protein expression in TILs in the 30-patient cohort, while multiplex immunofluorescence was used to evaluate E7 and PD-L1 expression in TILs in a 104-patient tissue microarray. Exosome uptake by TILs was assessed using confocal microscopy.
Main results
E7 protein was detected in exosomes from the peripheral blood of 28 of 30 patients (93.3%) and in exosomes from SiHa cell culture. In the tissue microarray cohort (n = 103; 28 events), higher CD3 + PD-L1 + /CD3 + expression was associated with poorer survival (HR = 2.97, p = 0.009). CD3 + E7 + /CD3 + expression showed a borderline association with poorer survival in the dichotomized analysis (HR = 2.15, p = 0.052) but was significantly associated with survival when modeled as a continuous variable (per 10%: HR = 1.28, p = 0.002). In the 30-patient cohort, exosomal E7 protein content was significantly associated with PD-L1 expression in TILs (Cramér's V = 0.728, p = 0.0001).
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest that exosomal E7 may contribute to PD-L1 regulation in TILs and may be associated with poorer prognosis in patients with cervical cancer.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of targeted therapies for HPV16-positive cervical cancer and improve understanding of immune checkpoint blockade responses in this context.
Limitations and evidence strength
Further studies are needed to determine whether E7 directly mediates this effect and to clarify its functional and clinical significance. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study and the need for additional mechanistic validation.

巴林初级保健医生的知识、态度和 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 推荐实践:一项横断面研究

Knowledge, attitudes and human papillomavirus vaccine recommendation practices among primary care physicians in Bahrain: a cross-sectional study
作者： Fatema A. Yusuf, Maryam Aftab Ahmed, Zahraa A. Alali, Zakeya S. Khalaf, Riham Algarf, Muna A. Almohri, Basheer Makarem, Sadok Chlif, Afif Ben Salah, Fatema Habbash
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
医生推荐是 HPV 疫苗 吸收的关键决定因素,但人们对巴林初级保健医生推荐 疫苗 的频率了解有限。
设计与对象
2025 年,对巴林所有四个省的初级卫生保健中心工作的 205 名初级保健医生进行了一项横断面研究。
核心方法
使用自填问卷来评估过去六个月的知识(24 项)、态度(6 项)、信心和 疫苗 推荐频率。定期推荐(至少每月一次)被定义为结果,并采用多变量逻辑回归来识别相关因素。
主要结果
平均知识得分为 19.1(满分 24 分)(SD 2.8)。大多数医生认为 疫苗 安全(81.0%)和有效(88.8%),77.6% 支持将其纳入常规治疗方案。在过去六个月中,35.6%的人从未推荐过疫苗,28.3%的人推荐过一两次,36.1%的人经常推荐。
解释与创新
定期推荐与四年以上的经验以及较高的家长和社区接受度相关,但与知识水平无关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要对早期职业医生进行有针对性的培训,并反馈家长的实际接受程度,以改进 HPV 疫苗 推荐实践。
局限与证据强度
该研究是横断面的,限制了因果推断,并且证据基于自我报告的数据,这可能会引入偏差。研究结果的可信度为中等。

Knowledge, attitudes and human papillomavirus vaccine recommendation practices among primary care physicians in Bahrain: a cross-sectional study

Authors: Fatema A. Yusuf, Maryam Aftab Ahmed, Zahraa A. Alali, Zakeya S. Khalaf, Riham Algarf, Muna A. Almohri, Basheer Makarem, Sadok Chlif, Afif Ben Salah, Fatema Habbash
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Physician recommendation is one of the main determinants of HPV vaccine uptake, yet little is known about how often primary care physicians in Bahrain recommend the vaccine. We assessed their knowledge, attitudes and recommendation practices and looked for factors associated with regular recommendation. Methods We surveyed 205 physicians working in primary health care centres in all four governorates of Bahrain in 2025. A self-administered questionnaire covered knowledge (24 items), attitudes (6 items), confidence, and how often the physician had recommended the vaccine in the previous six months. Regular recommendation (about once a month or more) was the outcome, and multivariable logistic regression was used to identify associated factors. Results The mean knowledge score was 19.1 of 24 (SD 2.8). Most physicians considered the vaccine safe (81.0%) and effective (88.8%), and 77.6% supported its place in the routine schedule. Far fewer perceived that parents will accept it (28.8%) or that the community would (36.1%). In the previous six months, 35.6% had never recommended the vaccine, 28.3% had done so once or twice, and 36.1% recommended it regularly. Regular recommendation was associated with more than four years of experience (5–15 years: aOR 5.34, 95% CI 2.39–11.93; over 15 years: aOR 5.15, 95% CI 2.15–12.35) and with higher perceived parental and community acceptance (aOR 1.33 per point, 95% CI 1.07–1.65). Knowledge was not associated with recommendation (aOR 1.04, 95% CI 0.92–1.18). Conclusions Primary care physicians in Bahrain have good knowledge of HPV vaccination and view the vaccine favourably. Because the vaccine is delivered through school campaigns, physicians have few routine opportunities to recommend it, and their role is mainly counselling hesitant parents. Recommendation was linked to experience and to what physicians expect parents to accept, not to knowledge. Training for early-career physicians and feedback on actual parental acceptance may help.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Physician recommendation is a key determinant of HPV vaccine uptake, yet there is limited understanding of how frequently primary care physicians in Bahrain recommend the vaccine.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted in 2025 among 205 primary care physicians working in primary health care centres across all four governorates of Bahrain.
Core methods
A self-administered questionnaire was used to assess knowledge (24 items), attitudes (6 items), confidence, and frequency of vaccine recommendation over the previous six months. Regular recommendation (at least once a month) was defined as the outcome, and multivariable logistic regression was employed to identify associated factors.
Main results
The mean knowledge score was 19.1 out of 24 (SD 2.8). Most physicians considered the vaccine safe (81.0%) and effective (88.8%), and 77.6% supported its inclusion in the routine schedule. In the previous six months, 35.6% had never recommended the vaccine, 28.3% had done so once or twice, and 36.1% recommended it regularly.
Interpretation and novelty
Regular recommendation was associated with more than four years of experience and higher perceived parental and community acceptance, but not with knowledge levels.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for targeted training for early-career physicians and feedback on actual parental acceptance to improve HPV vaccine recommendation practices.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is cross-sectional, limiting causal inference, and the evidence is based on self-reported data, which may introduce bias. The confidence in the findings is moderate.

巴林家长对 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 的知识、态度和接受度:一项横断面研究

Knowledge, attitudes and acceptance of human papillomavirus vaccination among parents in Bahrain: a cross-sectional study
作者： Mohamed Hany Shehata, Jaafar Al Mulla, Fatema Al Aali, Masooma Alawi, Asma Abdulnoor, ZAHRA HABIB, Afif Ben Salah, Muna A. Almohri, Amer Jebril Almarabheh, Sadok Chlif
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解巴林家长对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 的了解、态度和接受程度,该国于 2023 年 10 月通过学校活动向青少年引入了 HPV 疫苗接种。
设计与对象
2025 年 7 月至 9 月期间,在四个政府初级卫生保健中心(每个省随机抽取一个)进行了一项横断面调查。巴林6至14岁儿童的家长在咨询后通过方便抽样的方式招募。
核心方法
该研究使用了一份涵盖社会人口特征的调查问卷、20个项目的知识得分、形成疫苗-信心和疫苗-关注分量表的八种态度陈述、提供时的接受度以及提供时的意愿。卡方检验和多变量逻辑回归用于统计分析。
主要结果
368名家长中,74.0%为母亲,62.9%受过大学教育。大多数人知道 HPV 影响女性 (83.1%) 并导致 宫颈癌 (72.1%),但只有 46.2% 听说过 疫苗。只有 22.0% 的人曾为孩子提供过这种服务,其中 70.0% (56/80) 接受了。在尚未提供的285名家长中,有60.4%表示愿意。卫生部门工作人员的积极态度更为常见(74.5% vs 45.1–48.1%;p = 0.002)。
解释与创新
作者解释了愿意接种疫苗的杰出父母的态度,而不是知识。疫苗 信心和较少的 疫苗 担忧独立预测了接种疫苗的意愿。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,改善态度并提供有关 疫苗 安全性的明确信息可以提高巴林家长对 HPV 疫苗接种 的接受度。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据基于来自便利样本的自我报告数据,这可能会引入偏差。由于这些限制,研究结果的可信度较低。

Knowledge, attitudes and acceptance of human papillomavirus vaccination among parents in Bahrain: a cross-sectional study

Authors: Mohamed Hany Shehata, Jaafar Al Mulla, Fatema Al Aali, Masooma Alawi, Asma Abdulnoor, ZAHRA HABIB, Afif Ben Salah, Muna A. Almohri, Amer Jebril Almarabheh, Sadok Chlif
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Bahrain introduced human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination for adolescent girls and boys through school campaigns in October 2023. Parents' decisions are central to uptake, yet their views have not been studied in Bahrain. We assessed parents' knowledge, attitudes and acceptance of the vaccine and the factors associated with willingness to vaccinate. Methods We conducted a cross-sectional, interviewer-administered survey between July and September 2025 in four governmental primary health care centres, one randomly selected from each governorate. Bahraini parents of children aged 6 to 14 years were recruited by convenience sampling after their consultation. The questionnaire covered sociodemographic characteristics, a 20-item knowledge score (for parents aware of the vaccine), eight attitude statements forming vaccine-confidence and vaccine-concern subscales, acceptance when offered, and willingness if offered. We used chi-square tests and multivariable logistic regression. Results Of 368 parents, 74.0% were mothers and 62.9% had a university education. Most knew that HPV affects women (83.1%) and causes cervical cancer (72.1%), but only 46.2% had heard of the vaccine. Just 22.0% had been offered it for a child, and 70.0% of those (56/80) accepted. Among the 285 parents not yet offered it, 60.4% were willing. In all, 80.6% said a recommendation from doctors and nurses would encourage them, and 54.6% said fear of side effects discouraged them. Willingness was 84.8% with positive and 37.9% with negative attitudes; vaccine confidence (adjusted OR 5.8 per SD, 95% CI 3.5–9.8) and fewer vaccine concerns (2.4, 1.7–3.6) independently predicted willingness. Among parents aware of the vaccine, mean knowledge was 14.0/20 (SD 3.9); willingness was 83.7% with good knowledge versus 57.1–58.1% otherwise (p = 0.017), but knowledge was not independently associated once attitude was included (adjusted OR 1.12 per point, 95% CI 0.94–1.33). Positive attitudes were more common among health-sector workers (74.5% vs 45.1–48.1%; p = 0.002). Conclusion Most parents in Bahrain are open to HPV vaccination, but awareness of the vaccine is low and concerns about side effects are common. Attitude, more than knowledge, distinguished parents who were willing to vaccinate. Recommendation by doctors and nurses, together with clear information about vaccine safety, is a practical way to turn openness into acceptance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand parents' knowledge, attitudes, and acceptance of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination in Bahrain, as the country introduced HPV vaccination for adolescents through school campaigns in October 2023.
Design and population
A cross-sectional survey was conducted between July and September 2025 in four governmental primary health care centres, one randomly selected from each governorate. Bahraini parents of children aged 6 to 14 years were recruited by convenience sampling after their consultation.
Core methods
The study used a questionnaire covering sociodemographic characteristics, a 20-item knowledge score, eight attitude statements forming vaccine-confidence and vaccine-concern subscales, acceptance when offered, and willingness if offered. Chi-square tests and multivariable logistic regression were used for statistical analysis.
Main results
Of 368 parents, 74.0% were mothers and 62.9% had a university education. Most knew that HPV affects women (83.1%) and causes cervical cancer (72.1%), but only 46.2% had heard of the vaccine. Just 22.0% had been offered it for a child, and 70.0% of those (56/80) accepted. Among the 285 parents not yet offered it, 60.4% were willing. Positive attitudes were more common among health-sector workers (74.5% vs 45.1–48.1%; p = 0.002).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that attitude, more than knowledge, distinguished parents who were willing to vaccinate. Vaccine confidence and fewer vaccine concerns independently predicted willingness to vaccinate.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that improving attitude and providing clear information about vaccine safety could enhance HPV vaccination acceptance among parents in Bahrain.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is based on self-reported data from a convenience sample, which may introduce bias. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to these limitations.

HPV 相关扁桃体上皮内瘤变和口服 高危型HPV mRNA 作为候选生物标志物

HPV-Associated Tonsillar Intraepithelial Neoplasia and Oral High-Risk HPV mRNA as a Candidate Biomarker
作者： Masashi Mori, Hidenori Tanaka, Motoyuki Suzuki, Kansuke Kido, Kazuya Ogawa, Noah Sasa, Toshihiro Kishikawa, Nagayasu Egawa, Daichi Maeda, Masafumi Horie
摘要中文翻译摘要 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 相关口咽癌的早期检测尚未得到满足,因为其癌前病变尚未确定。我们假设这种病变存在于口腔 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) mRNA 的个体中。我们术前筛查了 1,180 名因良性病症而接受扁桃体切除术的个体,并检测到 5 名患者口腔 HR-HPV mRNA。对整个切除的扁桃体进行连续切片,在这 5 个人中的 3 人中发现了 HPV 相关的上皮内病变。在另一名口腔 HPV16 感染且口腔 HR-HPV mRNA 状态未知的个体中发现了类似的病变。两个个体的上皮内病变与 HPV16 相关,并表现出中度至重度异型性,而另外两个个体的上皮内病变与其他 HR-HPV 相关,并表现出轻微至轻度的异型性。根据HPV生命周期,前者被归类为转化性病变,后者被归类为生产性至中期病变。空间转录组分析显示,分子谱介于正常上皮和 HPV 相关口咽癌之间。这些发现为 HPV 相关扁桃体癌前病变提供了第一个证据,并建议口服 HR-HPV mRNA 作为候选生物标志物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在通过调查口腔 高危型HPV mRNA 个体是否存在癌前病变,解决 HPV 相关口咽癌早期检测的未满足需求。
设计与对象
对 1,180 名因良性疾病接受扁桃体切除术的个体进行了一项术前筛查研究,确定了其中 5 名具有口腔 高危型HPV mRNA 的个体,并进一步分析了切除的扁桃体。
核心方法
该研究涉及术前筛查口腔 高危型HPV mRNA,然后对整个切除的扁桃体进行连续切片,以识别 HPV 相关的上皮内病变。
主要结果
对切​​除的扁桃体进行连续切片,在 5 名口腔 高危型HPV mRNA 个体中的 3 名中发现了 HPV 相关的上皮内病变。此外,一名口腔 HPV16 感染者也有类似病变,但其口腔 HR-HPV mRNA 状态未知。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,口腔 HR-HPV mRNA 可作为识别与 HPV 相关的癌前病变的候选生物标志物,标志着扁桃体区域此类病变的第一个证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为开发用于早期检测 HPV 相关口咽癌的新生物标志物奠定了基础,并可能为未来有关 HPV 相关癌前病变的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于术前筛查以及口服 HR-HPV mRNA 的个体数量较少,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

HPV-Associated Tonsillar Intraepithelial Neoplasia and Oral High-Risk HPV mRNA as a Candidate Biomarker

Authors: Masashi Mori, Hidenori Tanaka, Motoyuki Suzuki, Kansuke Kido, Kazuya Ogawa, Noah Sasa, Toshihiro Kishikawa, Nagayasu Egawa, Daichi Maeda, Masafumi Horie
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Early detection of human papillomavirus (HPV)-associated oropharyngeal cancer is an unmet need, as its premalignant lesions remain unestablished. We hypothesized that such lesions exist in individuals with oral high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) mRNA. We preoperatively screened 1,180 individuals undergoing tonsillectomy due to benign conditions and detected five with oral HR-HPV mRNA. Serial sectioning of the entire excised tonsils identified HPV-associated intraepithelial lesions in three of these five individuals. A similar lesion was identified in an additional individual with oral HPV16 infection with unknown oral HR-HPV mRNA status. Intraepithelial lesions in two individuals were HPV16-associated and showed moderate-to-severe atypia, whereas those in the other two individuals were associated with other HR-HPV and showed minimal-to-mild atypia. Based on HPV life cycle, the former were classified as transforming lesions and the latter as productive-to-intermediate lesions. Spatial transcriptomic analysis revealed molecular profiles intermediate between those of normal epithelium and HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer. These findings establish the first evidence for HPV-associated tonsillar premalignant lesions and suggest oral HR-HPV mRNA as a candidate biomarker.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the unmet need for early detection of HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer by investigating the existence of premalignant lesions in individuals with oral high-risk HPV mRNA.
Design and population
A preoperative screening study was conducted on 1,180 individuals undergoing tonsillectomy for benign conditions, identifying five with oral high-risk HPV mRNA and further analyzing the excised tonsils.
Core methods
The study involved preoperative screening for oral high-risk HPV mRNA, followed by serial sectioning of the entire excised tonsils to identify HPV-associated intraepithelial lesions.
Main results
Serial sectioning of the excised tonsils identified HPV-associated intraepithelial lesions in three of the five individuals with oral high-risk HPV mRNA. Additionally, one individual with oral HPV16 infection had a similar lesion, though their oral HR-HPV mRNA status was unknown.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that oral HR-HPV mRNA may serve as a candidate biomarker for identifying premalignant lesions associated with HPV, marking the first evidence of such lesions in the tonsillar region.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide a foundation for developing new biomarkers for early detection of HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer and may inform future research on HPV-related premalignant conditions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on preoperative screening and the small number of individuals with oral HR-HPV mRNA, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

人乳头瘤病毒 L1 衣壳蛋白的组装途径以静电相互作用编码。

The assembly pathway for human papillomavirus L1 capsid protein is encoded in electrostatic interactions.
作者： Yuan X, K N Nand, J C Patel, Jin A, Bystroff C
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 L1 蛋白自发折叠并组装成 60 nm 病毒样颗粒 (VLP),在高盐浓度下具有 T = 7d 二十面体对称性,但在低盐浓度下则不然。在组装过程中从低盐到高盐的快速增加会产生质量较差的 VLP,而在长达 48 小时内缓慢增加盐会产生高质量的 VLP。对这种行为的深入了解是通过基于 人乳头瘤病毒 16 型 VLP 低温电子显微镜 (cryo-EM) 结构的计算模型得出的。使用线性化泊松-玻尔兹曼方程计算静电势能垒。在单体最初快速寡聚成对称五聚体(壳粒)后,这些单元以能量顺序形成成对相互作用,最低势垒首先。但在高盐浓度下,无论是在模拟还是在实验中,能量势垒或多或少都是相等的,并且粒子形式不规则。相互作用界面由 突变 修改,以支持更小的 30 nm T = 1 二十面体对称性,而不支持 T = 7d 对称性。结果是更小的颗粒。通过仔细控制离子强度和/或通过设计突变界面残基,可以直接从未纯化的细菌裂解物中制备高质量、高免疫原性的VLP,而不需要使用拆卸/重新组装步骤。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 人乳头瘤病毒 L1 衣壳蛋白的组装途径以及盐浓度如何影响具有特定二十面体对称性的病毒样颗粒 (VLP) 的形成。
设计与对象
该研究使用基于 人乳头瘤病毒 16 型 VLP 低温电子显微镜 (cryo-EM) 结构的计算模型来研究组装过程,重点关注 L1 蛋白在不同盐浓度下的行为。
核心方法
研究人员使用线性化泊松-玻尔兹曼方程计算静电势能垒,并通过 突变 修改相互作用界面以支持特定的对称性。
主要结果
在高盐浓度下,能量势垒或多或少相等,导致在模拟和实验中形成不规则颗粒。结果表明,当相互作用界面被修改时,会产生具有 T = 1 对称性的较小颗粒。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,HPV L1 蛋白的组装途径受静电相互作用控制,控制离子强度或突变界面残基可以影响形成的 VLP 的对称性和质量。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为高质量、免疫原性 VLP 的生产提供了见解,这可能对 疫苗 的开发以及在生产中直接使用细菌裂解物而无需拆卸/重新组装步骤产生影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于计算模型和实验验证,在完全复制体内条件方面可能存在局限性,但证据得到模拟和实验数据的支持,使其具有中等的置信度。

The assembly pathway for human papillomavirus L1 capsid protein is encoded in electrostatic interactions.

Authors: Yuan X, K N Nand, J C Patel, Jin A, Bystroff C
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus L1 protein spontaneously folds and assembles into 60 nm virus-like particles (VLP), with T = 7d icosahedral symmetry at high salt concentrations but not in low salt. Rapid increase from low to high salt during the assembly process gives poor-quality VLPs, while slowly increasing salt, over as many as 48 h, gives rise to high-quality VLPs. Insight into this behavior is drawn from a computational model based on the cryogenic electron microscopy (cryo-EM) structure of the human papillomavirus Type 16 VLP. Electrostatic potential energy barriers were calculated using the linearized Poisson-Boltzmann equation. After initial, rapid oligomerization of monomers into symmetrical pentamers (capsomeres), these units form pairwise interactions in energetic order, lowest barrier first. But at high salt concentrations, the energetic barriers are more or less equal and irregular particles form, both in simulation and in experiments. The interaction interfaces were modified by mutation to favor the smaller, 30 nm T = 1 icosahedral symmetry and disfavor T = 7d symmetry. The results were smaller particles. By carefully controlling ionic strength, and/or by mutating interface residues by design, it is possible to make high quality, highly immunogenic VLPs directly from unpurified bacterial lysate, without requiring the use of a disassembly/reassembly step.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the assembly pathway of human papillomavirus L1 capsid protein and how salt concentration affects the formation of virus-like particles (VLPs) with specific icosahedral symmetries.
Design and population
The study used a computational model based on the cryogenic electron microscopy (cryo-EM) structure of the human papillomavirus Type 16 VLP to investigate the assembly process, focusing on the behavior of L1 protein under varying salt concentrations.
Core methods
The researchers calculated electrostatic potential energy barriers using the linearized Poisson-Boltzmann equation and modified interaction interfaces through mutation to favor specific symmetries.
Main results
At high salt concentrations, the energetic barriers were more or less equal, leading to the formation of irregular particles, both in simulation and experiments. The results showed that smaller particles with T = 1 symmetry were produced when the interaction interfaces were modified.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the assembly pathway of HPV L1 protein is governed by electrostatic interactions, and that controlling ionic strength or mutating interface residues can influence the symmetry and quality of VLPs formed.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the production of high-quality, immunogenic VLPs, which could have implications for vaccine development and the direct use of bacterial lysates in manufacturing without the need for disassembly/reassembly steps.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on computational modeling and experimental validation, which may have limitations in fully replicating in vivo conditions, but the evidence is supported by both simulation and experimental data, giving it a moderate confidence level.

利用生殖道微生态和病原体特征预测 高危型HPV 感染和基因型合并感染:机器学习研究

Predicting high-risk HPV infection and genotype co-infection using genital tract microecological and pathogen characteristics: a machine learning study
作者： Jiaoling Wen, Huisan Xiao, Guoxuan Li, Zhe Lu, Xiangyue Zeng, Ying Zheng
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 高危型人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)持续感染是宫颈癌及其前驱病变的主要原因。阴道微生态失调和生殖道合并感染可能会影响 HR-HPV 的获得、清除和持续存在。然而,整合阴道微生态指标、生殖道病原体和HR-HPV基因型共感染模式的定量预测模型仍然有限。方法回顾性研究2022年1月至2025年12月期间在海南省妇女儿童医疗中心妇科门诊同时接受HPV基因分型、阴道微生态评估和生殖道病原体检测的3,403例患者。其中HR-HPV阳性765例,HR-HPV阴性2,638例。使用单变量分析、基于方差膨胀因子 (VIF ≤ 4) 的多重共线性评估和 Boruta 算法来选择候选预测变量。开发了七个机器学习模型,并使用网格搜索和训练集中的 10 倍交叉验证来优化它们的超参数。分类器链(C≤C)和集成分类器链(ECC)被应用于HR-HPV基因型的多标签预测。使用 SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) 解释模型预测。结果 在多变量逻辑回归中,解脲支原体(UU;aOR = 2.051)、沙眼衣原体(CT;aOR = 1.982)、严重细菌性阴道病评分(aOR = 1.614)、阴道 pH 值异常(aOR = 1.375)和唾液酸酶阳性(aOR = 1.747)与HR-HPV 阳性(所有 P
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)持续感染是宫颈癌及其前驱病变的主要原因。本研究旨在开发整合阴道微生态指标、生殖道病原体和 HR-HPV 基因型共感染模式的定量预测模型。
设计与对象
本回顾性研究纳入了2022年1月至2025年12月期间在海南省妇女儿童医疗中心妇科门诊同时接受HPV基因分型、阴道微生态评估和生殖道病原体检测的3,403例患者。其中,765例HR-HPV阳性,2,638例HR-HPV阴性。
核心方法
使用单变量分析、基于方差膨胀因子 (VIF ≤ 4) 的多重共线性评估和 Boruta 算法来选择候选预测变量。开发了七个机器学习模型,并使用网格搜索和训练集中的 10 倍交叉验证来优化它们的超参数。分类器链(C≤C)和集成分类器链(ECC)被应用于HR-HPV基因型的多标签预测。
主要结果
在多变量 Logistic 回归中,解脲支原体 (UU;aOR = 2.051)、沙眼衣原体 (CT;aOR = 1.982)、严重细菌性阴道病评分 (aOR = 1.614)、阴道 pH 值异常 (aOR = 1.375) 和唾液酸酶阳性 (aOR = 1.747) 与 HR-HPV 独立相关积极性(所有P.
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为特定生殖道微生态和病原体特征与 HR-HPV 阳性独立相关的证据,强调了将这些因素整合到 HR-HPV 感染预测模型中的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更准确的 HR-HPV 感染 监测 和诊断工具提供信息,从而有可能改善临床环境中的风险评估和预防策略。
局限与证据强度
尽管使用多变量逻辑回归和机器学习模型为所报告的关联提供了中等程度的置信度,但该研究仍因其回顾性设计和潜在的混杂因素而受到限制。

Predicting high-risk HPV infection and genotype co-infection using genital tract microecological and pathogen characteristics: a machine learning study

Authors: Jiaoling Wen, Huisan Xiao, Guoxuan Li, Zhe Lu, Xiangyue Zeng, Ying Zheng
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) is the principal cause of cervical cancer and its precursor lesions. Vaginal microecological dysbiosis and genital tract co-infections may influence HR-HPV acquisition, clearance, and persistence. However, quantitative prediction models integrating vaginal microecological indicators, genital tract pathogens, and HR-HPV genotype co-infection patterns remain limited. Of these, 765 were HR-HPV-positive and 2,638 were HR-HPV-negative. Candidate predictors were selected using univariable analysis, multicollinearity assessment based on the variance inflation factor (VIF ≦ 4), and the Boruta algorithm. Seven machine learning models were developed, and their hyperparameters were optimized using grid search with 10-fold cross-validation within the training set. Question and background
Persistent infection with high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) is the principal cause of cervical cancer and its precursor lesions. This study aimed to develop quantitative prediction models that integrate vaginal microecological indicators, genital tract pathogens, and HR-HPV genotype co-infection patterns.
Design and population
This retrospective study included 3,403 patients who underwent concurrent HPV genotyping, vaginal microecological assessment, and genital tract pathogen testing at the gynecology outpatient clinic of Hainan Women and Children’s Medical Center between January 2022 and December 2025. Of these, 765 were HR-HPV-positive and 2,638 were HR-HPV-negative.
Core methods
Candidate predictors were selected using univariable analysis, multicollinearity assessment based on the variance inflation factor (VIF ≦ 4), and the Boruta algorithm. Seven machine learning models were developed, and their hyperparameters were optimized using grid search with 10-fold cross-validation within the training set. Classifier chains (C ≦ C) and ensemble classifier chains (ECC) were applied to the multi-label prediction of HR-HPV genotypes.
Main results
In multivariable logistic regression, Ureaplasma urealyticum (UU; aOR = 2.051), Chlamydia trachomatis (CT; aOR = 1.982), severe bacterial vaginosis score (aOR = 1.614), abnormal vaginal pH (aOR = 1.375), and sialidase positivity (aOR = 1.747) were independently associated with HR-HPV positivity (all P.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence that specific genital tract microecological and pathogen characteristics are independently associated with HR-HPV positivity, highlighting the potential of integrating these factors into predictive models for HR-HPV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform the development of more accurate surveillance and diagnostic tools for HR-HPV infection, potentially improving risk assessment and prevention strategies in clinical settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for confounding factors, although the use of multivariable logistic regression and machine learning models provides a moderate level of confidence in the associations reported.

在喀麦隆对高风险致癌 人乳头瘤病毒 进行更广泛的筛查,需要优化 疫苗 分支覆盖范围

A Wider Screening of High-Risk Oncogenic Human Papillomaviruses in Cameroon Calls for An Optimized Generation of Vaccine-Clade Coverage
作者： Michel Carlos Tommo Tchouaket, Bernadette Fokou Bomgning, Larissa Gaëlle Moko Fotso, Valentine Marie Ferré, Kambou Kountchou Diane Keriane, Ezéchiel Ngoufack Jagni Semengue, Collins Ambe Chenwi, Durelle Tsopmo Ymele, Alex Durand Nka, Aude Christelle Ka’e
摘要中文翻译摘要背景:高危致癌人乳头瘤病毒(HR-HPV)在全球范围内分布不均,这可能会影响当前预防性药物疫苗的有效性。我们评估了喀麦隆女性中 HR-HPV 的分子 流行病学 及其与现有 疫苗 效价的充分性。方法:2020 年 6 月至 2022 年 5 月期间,在喀麦隆雅温得和杜阿拉的两家参考医院招募了 18 岁或以上性活跃、未接种疫苗的女性。通过实时 PCR 对宫颈样本进行 HR-HPV 筛查,使用第二个实时 PCR 平台进行基因分型,并通过第三个多重实时 PCR 检测确认不一致或无法解释的结果。Logistic 回归确定了与 HR-HPV 阳性相关的因素。结果:在 413 名入组女性(中位年龄 40 岁,四分位数范围 33 至 49)中,96 名(23.2%,95% 置信区间 17.2 至 25.6)HR-HPV 检测呈阳性。鉴定出 13 种基因型,最常见的是 HPV18 (19.8%)、HPV16 (16.7%)、HPV58 (16.7%)、HPV39 (15.6%)、HPV66 (13.5%) 和 HPV59 (12.5%)。37.5% 的阳性样本中检测到多基因型感染,每个样本有 2 至 5 种基因型。70 名女性 (17%) 观察到细胞学异常,大部分为低度病变。15 岁之前第一次性交、超过 3 个终生性伴侣以及宫颈病变的存在与 HR-HPV 阳性显着相关。结论:HR-HPV感染在喀麦隆女性中常见,基因型多样性广泛,多重感染频繁。非 疫苗 基因型(例如 HPV39、HPV59 和 HPV66)是最常见的,这种模式可能需要在这种情况下考虑更广价的 HPV 疫苗,同时继续投资 宫颈癌 筛查计划。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危致癌人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV)在全球范围内分布不均,这可能会影响当前预防性药物疫苗的有效性。
设计与对象
我们评估了喀麦隆女性中 HR-HPV 的分子 流行病学 及其与现有 疫苗 效价的充分性。
核心方法
2020 年 6 月至 2022 年 5 月期间,喀麦隆雅温得和杜阿拉的两家参考医院招募了性活跃、年龄为 18 岁或以上、未接种疫苗的女性。通过实时 PCR 对宫颈样本进行 HR-HPV 筛查,使用第二个实时 PCR 平台进行基因分型,并通过第三个多重实时 PCR 检测确认不一致或无法解释的结果。Logistic 回归确定了与 HR-HPV 阳性相关的因素。
主要结果
在 413 名登记女性(中位年龄 40 岁,四分位数范围 33 至 49)中,96 名(23.2%,95% 置信区间 17.2 至 25.6)HR-HPV 检测呈阳性。鉴定出 13 种基因型,最常见的是 HPV18 (19.8%)、HPV16 (16.7%)、HPV58 (16.7%)、HPV39 (15.6%)、HPV66 (13.5%) 和 HPV59 (12.5%)。37.5% 的阳性样本中检测到多基因型感染,每个样本有 2 至 5 种基因型。
解释与创新
HR-HPV感染在喀麦隆女性中很常见,具有广泛的基因型多样性和频繁的多重感染。非 疫苗 基因型(例如 HPV39、HPV59 和 HPV66)是最常见的,这种模式可能需要在这种情况下考虑更广价的 HPV 疫苗。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了喀麦隆对广价 HPV 疫苗 的需求,并强调了继续投资 宫颈癌 筛查计划的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其重点关注未接种疫苗的女性以及依赖宫颈样本,而宫颈样本可能无法完全代表人群。由于该研究的观察性质,证据的强度中等。

A Wider Screening of High-Risk Oncogenic Human Papillomaviruses in Cameroon Calls for An Optimized Generation of Vaccine-Clade Coverage

Authors: Michel Carlos Tommo Tchouaket, Bernadette Fokou Bomgning, Larissa Gaëlle Moko Fotso, Valentine Marie Ferré, Kambou Kountchou Diane Keriane, Ezéchiel Ngoufack Jagni Semengue, Collins Ambe Chenwi, Durelle Tsopmo Ymele, Alex Durand Nka, Aude Christelle Ka’e
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background: High-risk oncogenic human papillomaviruses (HR-HPV) are unevenly distributed worldwide, which may affect the effectiveness of current prophylactic vaccines. We assessed the molecular epidemiology of HR-HPV among Cameroonian women and its adequacy with existing vaccine valence. Methods: Sexually active, unvaccinated women aged 18 years or older were enrolled between June 2020 and May 2022 in two reference hospitals in Yaoundé and Douala, Cameroon. Cervical samples were screened for HR-HPV by real-time PCR, genotyped using a second real-time PCR platform, and discordant or uninterpretable results were confirmed with a third multiplex real-time PCR assay. Logistic regression identified factors associated with HR-HPV positivity. Results: Of 413 women enrolled (median age 40 years, interquartile range 33 to 49), 96 (23.2%, 95% confidence interval 17.2 to 25.6) tested positive for HR-HPV. Thirteen genotypes were identified, most frequently HPV18 (19.8%), HPV16 (16.7%), HPV58 (16.7%), HPV39 (15.6%), HPV66 (13.5%), and HPV59 (12.5%). Multiple-genotype infections were detected in 37.5% of positive samples, ranging from two to five genotypes per specimen. Cytological abnormalities were observed in 70 women (17%), mostly low-grade lesions. First sexual intercourse before age 15, more than three lifetime sexual partners, and the presence of cervical lesions were significantly associated with HR-HPV positivity. Conclusion: HR-HPV infection is common among Cameroonian women, with wide genotypic diversity and frequent multiple infections. Non-vaccine genotypes such as HPV39, HPV59 and HPV66 were among the most frequent, a pattern that may warrant consideration of broader-valence HPV vaccines in this setting, alongside continued investment in cervical cancer screening programmes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
High-risk oncogenic human papillomaviruses (HR-HPV) are unevenly distributed worldwide, which may affect the effectiveness of current prophylactic vaccines.
Design and population
We assessed the molecular epidemiology of HR-HPV among Cameroonian women and its adequacy with existing vaccine valence.
Core methods
Sexually active, unvaccinated women aged 18 years or older were enrolled between June 2020 and May 2022 in two reference hospitals in Yaoundé and Douala, Cameroon. Cervical samples were screened for HR-HPV by real-time PCR, genotyped using a second real-time PCR platform, and discordant or uninterpretable results were confirmed with a third multiplex real-time PCR assay. Logistic regression identified factors associated with HR-HPV positivity.
Main results
Of 413 women enrolled (median age 40 years, interquartile range 33 to 49), 96 (23.2%, 95% confidence interval 17.2 to 25.6) tested positive for HR-HPV. Thirteen genotypes were identified, most frequently HPV18 (19.8%), HPV16 (16.7%), HPV58 (16.7%), HPV39 (15.6%), HPV66 (13.5%), and HPV59 (12.5%). Multiple-genotype infections were detected in 37.5% of positive samples, ranging from two to five genotypes per specimen.
Interpretation and novelty
HR-HPV infection is common among Cameroonian women, with wide genotypic diversity and frequent multiple infections. Non-vaccine genotypes such as HPV39, HPV59 and HPV66 were among the most frequent, a pattern that may warrant consideration of broader-valence HPV vaccines in this setting.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for broader-valence HPV vaccines in Cameroon and emphasize the importance of continued investment in cervical cancer screening programmes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on unvaccinated women and its reliance on cervical samples, which may not fully represent the population. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study.

孕妇高风险 患病率 感染及其与不良妊娠结局的关联:横断面研究

Prevalence of High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Infection in Pregnant Women and Its Association with Adverse Pregnancy Outcomes: A Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Thanapornphan Pokchainun, Natacha Phoolcharoen, Natkrita Pohthipornthawat, Pinyada Panyavaranant
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染在育龄妇女中很常见,但东南亚妊娠期 HPV 感染的数据仍然有限。本研究旨在确定泰国孕妇中高危型HPV感染和宫颈细胞学异常的患病率,确定与高危型HPV感染相关的因素,并探讨其与不良妊娠结局的关系。方法 这项横断面研究纳入了 2024 年 6 月至 2025 年 5 月期间在泰国曼谷一家三级转诊医院接受产前护理的年龄≥ 18 岁的孕妇。使用实时聚合酶链反应检测宫颈样本的 14 种 高危型HPV 基因型,并通过液基细胞学进行评估。使用问卷和电子病历收集人口统计、生殖和流行病学特征。分娩后记录妊娠结局。使用逻辑回归来识别与 高危型HPV 感染相关的因素。结果 在 255 名参与者中,40 名 高危型HPV 检测呈阳性,患病率 为 15.7% (95% CI 11.5–20.7)。其他 高危型HPV 基因型最常见 (12.2%),其次是 HPV 16 (3.9%) 和 HPV 18 (1.6%)。2.0% 的参与者(4 名 ASC-US 和 1 名 HSIL)检测到宫颈细胞学异常。年龄较小和一生拥有三个或以上性伴侣与 高危型HPV 感染独立相关。216 名参与者的妊娠结果数据可用。高危型HPV 感染与流产、早产、胎膜早破、胎儿生长受限、低出生体重、妊娠期高血压或先兆子痫之间没有观察到统计学上显着的关联。结论 15.7%的孕妇中发现高危型HPV感染,而宫颈细胞学异常并不常见,大多数高危型HPV感染发生在细胞学正常的女性中。产前护理可能提供提高 宫颈癌 筛查覆盖率的机会。尽管该研究的主要目的不是评估这些结果,但没有观察到 高危型HPV 感染与不良妊娠结局之间存在显着关联。

问题与背景

在 255 名参与者中,有 40 人的 高危型HPV 检测呈阳性,患病率 为 15.7% (95% CI 11.5–20.7)。其他 高危型HPV 基因型最常见 (12.2%),其次是 HPV 16 (3.9%) 和 HPV 18 (1.6%)。2.0% 的参与者(4 名 ASC-US 和 1 名 HSIL)检测到宫颈细胞学异常。

Prevalence of High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Infection in Pregnant Women and Its Association with Adverse Pregnancy Outcomes: A Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Thanapornphan Pokchainun, Natacha Phoolcharoen, Natkrita Pohthipornthawat, Pinyada Panyavaranant
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background High-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is common among women of reproductive age, but data on HPV infection during pregnancy in South-East Asia remain limited. This study aimed to determine the prevalence of high-risk HPV infection and abnormal cervical cytology among pregnant women in Thailand, identify factors associated with high-risk HPV infection, and explore its association with adverse pregnancy outcomes. Methods This cross-sectional study included pregnant women aged ≥ 18 years attending antenatal care at a tertiary referral hospital in Bangkok, Thailand, between June 2024 and May 2025. Cervical samples were tested for 14 high-risk HPV genotypes using real-time polymerase chain reaction and assessed by liquid-based cytology. Demographic, reproductive, and epidemiological characteristics were collected using questionnaires and electronic medical records. Pregnancy outcomes were recorded after delivery. Logistic regression was used to identify factors associated with high-risk HPV infection. Results Among 255 participants, 40 tested positive for high-risk HPV, giving a prevalence of 15.7% (95% CI 11.5–20.7). Other high-risk HPV genotypes were most common (12.2%), followed by HPV 16 (3.9%) and HPV 18 (1.6%). Abnormal cervical cytology was detected in 2.0% of participants (four ASC-US and one HSIL). Younger age and having three or more lifetime sexual partners were independently associated with high-risk HPV infection. Pregnancy outcome data were available for 216 participants. No statistically significant associations were observed between high-risk HPV infection and abortion, preterm delivery, preterm premature rupture of membranes, fetal growth restriction, low birth weight, gestational hypertension, or pre-eclampsia. Conclusions High-risk HPV infection was identified in 15.7% of pregnant women, while abnormal cervical cytology was uncommon and most high-risk HPV infections occurred in women with normal cytology. Antenatal care may provide an opportunity to improve cervical cancer screening coverage. No significant association between high-risk HPV infection and adverse pregnancy outcomes was observed, although the study was not powered primarily to assess these outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
High-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is common among women of reproductive age, but data on HPV infection during pregnancy in South-East Asia remain limited.
Design and population

Main results

9 价 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 后精氨酸加压素缺乏:病例报告

Arginine vasopressin deficiency following 9-valent human papillomavirus vaccination: a case report
作者： Jianping Yao, Yujia Qiu, Yani Zhou, Pengtao Song, Guoping Chen, Jing Chen
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染及相关疾病是全球主要的健康负担,9 价 HPV 疫苗 被广泛用于减少其 发病率 和 死亡率。本研究报告了首例有记录的 9 价 HPV 疫苗接种 后精氨酸加压素 (AVP) 缺乏的病例,以强调临床对 疫苗接种 后潜在不良事件保持警惕的必要性。病例介绍 一名 30 岁的中国女性于 2023 年 7 月首次接种 9 价 HPV 疫苗 后出现症状。本报告详细介绍了她的临床数据、实验室结果、MRI 结果、治疗 和随访结果。疫苗接种后一天,患者出现多尿和严重口渴,既往无头部外伤、围产期事件或内分泌紊乱病史。实验室检查证实 AVP 缺乏,脑 MRI 显示垂体柄增厚且缺乏垂体后亮点,与漏斗部神经垂体炎一致。治疗服用去氨加压素后,她的多尿和多饮得到了很好的控制。在 30 个月的随访中,去氨加压素剂量已减少至 0.025 mg,每天两次,没有症状复发,重复 MRI 显示垂体形态正常化。结论 这是首例报道的 AVP 缺陷可能与 9 价 HPV 疫苗接种 相关。虽然不能从单一病例中确定因果关系,但临床医生应注意 疫苗接种 后新发的多尿和多饮,并在鉴别 诊断 时考虑 AVP 缺乏。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
摘要 背景 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染及相关疾病是全球主要的健康负担,9 价 HPV 疫苗 被广泛用于减少其 发病率 和 死亡率。
设计与对象
本研究报告了首例有记录的 9 价 HPV 疫苗接种 后精氨酸加压素 (AVP) 缺乏的病例,以强调临床对 疫苗接种 后潜在不良事件保持警惕的必要性。
核心方法
病例介绍 一名 30 岁的中国女性于 2023 年 7 月首次接种 9 价 HPV 疫苗 后出现症状。
主要结果
实验室检查证实 AVP 缺乏,脑 MRI 显示垂体柄增厚且缺乏垂体后亮点,与漏斗部神经垂体炎一致。
解释与创新
这是首例报道的 AVP 缺乏症可能与 9 价 HPV 疫苗接种 相关。
科研与公卫意义
虽然不能从单一病例中确定因果关系,但临床医生应注意 疫苗接种 后新发的多尿和多饮,并在鉴别 诊断 时考虑 AVP 缺乏。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告详细的研究设计或统计分析,但 病例报告 提供了独特的临床观察。

Arginine vasopressin deficiency following 9-valent human papillomavirus vaccination: a case report

Authors: Jianping Yao, Yujia Qiu, Yani Zhou, Pengtao Song, Guoping Chen, Jing Chen
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection and associated diseases are a major global health burden, and the 9-valent HPV vaccine is widely used to reduce their incidence and mortality. This study reports the first documented case of arginine vasopressin (AVP) deficiency following 9-valent HPV vaccination, to highlight the need for clinical vigilance for potential post-vaccination adverse events. Case presentation A 30-year-old Chinese woman developed symptoms after her first dose of the 9-valent HPV vaccine in July 2023. This report details her clinical data, laboratory results, MRI findings, treatment, and follow-up outcomes. One day after vaccination, the patient developed polyuria and severe thirst, with no prior history of head trauma, peripartum events, or endocrine disorders. Laboratory tests confirmed AVP deficiency, and brain MRI showed pituitary stalk thickening and absence of the posterior pituitary bright spot, consistent with infundibulo-neurohypophysitis. Following treatment with desmopressin, her polyuria and polydipsia were well controlled. At 30 months of follow-up, the desmopressin dose had been reduced to 0.025 mg twice daily without symptom recurrence, and repeat MRI showed normalized pituitary morphology. Conclusion This is the first reported case of AVP deficiency possibly associated with 9-valent HPV vaccination. Although causality cannot be established from a single case, clinicians should be aware of new-onset polyuria and polydipsia after vaccination and consider AVP deficiency in the differential diagnosis.

人乳头瘤病毒 传播 和 宫颈癌 级数与 疫苗接种 和 治疗 的数学建模和稳定性分析

Mathematical Modeling and Stability Analysis of Human Papillomavirus Transmission and Cervical Cancer Progression with Vaccination and Treatment
作者： Bashir Sule, Dauda Gulibur Yakubu, Magaji Yunbunga Adamu, Abubakar Muhammad Kwami
摘要中文翻译在本研究中,我们开发并分析了确定性数学模型,即 人乳头瘤病毒–宫颈癌 传播 和控制模型 (HPV-CCT-CM),以研究在 疫苗接种 和 治疗 干预措施的联合作用下,HPV 的 传播 动态导致 宫颈癌。该模型扩展了现有框架,将易感、疫苗接种、感染、癌前、癌变、治疗和康复的女性以及易感、感染和康复的男性纳入隔室,并明确表示了临床干预措施。我们建立了无病和地方性平衡状态的存在并分析了它们的稳定性。检查基本再生数 R0 和相关的稳定性条件。无病平衡对于 R0 1 来说是局部渐近稳定的。数值结果表明,即使有效接触率高于 0.7,地方性平衡也保持稳定,而当接触率超过 1.0 时,地方性平衡就会变得不稳定。这些发现表明,控制 HPV 传播 和 宫颈癌 需要降低接触率,同时提高 疫苗接种 覆盖率、疫苗 功效和 治疗 的可及性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨在疫苗接种和治疗干预措施的联合作用下,人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)导致宫颈癌的传播动态。这项研究的动机是需要了解这些干预措施如何影响 HPV 的传播和 宫颈癌 的进展。
设计与对象
开发并分析了确定性数学模型 HPV-CCT-CM,以研究 HPV 的 传播 动态和 宫颈癌 进展。该模型包含易感、疫苗接种、感染、癌前、癌变、治疗和康复女性以及易感、感染和康复男性的隔间。
核心方法
分析该模型以确定无病和地方性平衡状态的存在并检查其稳定性。还分析了基本再生数 R0 和相关的稳定性条件。
主要结果
当 R0 < 1 时,无病平衡是局部渐近稳定的。数值结果表明,即使有效接触率高于 0.7,地方性平衡也保持稳定,而当接触率超过 1.0 时,地方性平衡就会变得不稳定。
解释与创新
作者解释说,控制 HPV 传播 和 宫颈癌 需要降低接触率,同时提高 疫苗接种 覆盖率、疫苗 功效和 治疗 的可及性。这项研究为不同接触率和干预策略下平衡状态的稳定性提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过强调降低接触率和改进 疫苗接种 和 治疗 计划的重要性,为 HPV 监测、预防和控制的公共卫生策略提供信息。它们还有助于理解如何使用数学模型来预测和管理 HPV 相关疾病。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于确定性数学模型,并未考虑现实世界的变异性或临床试验的数据。证据来自理论分析和数值模拟,这在捕获复杂的生物相互作用方面可能存在局限性。由于证据仅具有抽象性质以及对基于模型的结果的依赖,因此研究结果的可信度为中等。

Mathematical Modeling and Stability Analysis of Human Papillomavirus Transmission and Cervical Cancer Progression with Vaccination and Treatment

Authors: Bashir Sule, Dauda Gulibur Yakubu, Magaji Yunbunga Adamu, Abubakar Muhammad Kwami
Original abstract (English)
In this study, we develop and analyze a deterministic mathematical model, the Human Papillomavirus–Cervical Cancer Transmission and Control Model (HPV-CCT-CM), to investigate the transmission dynamics of HPV leading to cervical cancer under the combined effects of vaccination and treatment interventions. The model extends existing frameworks by incorporating compartments for susceptible, vaccinated, infected, precancerous, cancerous, treated, and recovered females, as well as susceptible, infected, and recovered males, with clinical interventions explicitly represented. We establish the existence of disease-free and endemic equilibrium states and analyze their stability properties. The basic reproduction number, R0, and the associated stability conditions are examined. The disease-free equilibrium is shown to be locally asymptotically stable for R0 1. Numerical results demonstrate that the endemic equilibrium remains stable even when the effective contact rate is above 0.7 and becomes unstable when the contact rate exceeds 1.0. These findings indicate that controlling HPV transmission and cervical cancer requires reducing contact rates while enhancing vaccination coverage, vaccine efficacy, and access to treatment.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the transmission dynamics of human papillomavirus (HPV) leading to cervical cancer under the combined effects of vaccination and treatment interventions. The research is motivated by the need to understand how these interventions can influence the spread of HPV and the progression to cervical cancer.
Design and population
A deterministic mathematical model, the HPV-CCT-CM, was developed and analyzed to study the transmission dynamics of HPV and cervical cancer progression. The model incorporates compartments for susceptible, vaccinated, infected, precancerous, cancerous, treated, and recovered females, as well as susceptible, infected, and recovered males.
Core methods
The model was analyzed to establish the existence of disease-free and endemic equilibrium states and to examine their stability properties. The basic reproduction number, R0, and the associated stability conditions were also analyzed.
Main results
The disease-free equilibrium is shown to be locally asymptotically stable for R0 < 1. Numerical results demonstrate that the endemic equilibrium remains stable even when the effective contact rate is above 0.7 and becomes unstable when the contact rate exceeds 1.0.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that controlling HPV transmission and cervical cancer requires reducing contact rates while enhancing vaccination coverage, vaccine efficacy, and access to treatment. This study provides novel insights into the stability of equilibrium states under varying contact rates and intervention strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies for HPV surveillance, prevention, and control by highlighting the importance of reducing contact rates and improving vaccination and treatment programs. They also contribute to the understanding of how mathematical models can be used to predict and manage HPV-related diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a deterministic mathematical model and does not account for real-world variability or data from clinical trials. The evidence is derived from theoretical analysis and numerical simulations, which may have limitations in capturing complex biological interactions. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to the abstract-only nature of the evidence and the reliance on model-based results.

在患有口腔扁平苔藓的年轻成年男性的口腔和生殖器区域同时检测到 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV):病例报告

Coincident Detection of Human Papillomavirus (HPV) in the Oral and Genital Regions in a Young Adult Male with Oral Lichen Planus: A Case Report
作者： Andjelija Petrović, Željko D. Popović, Biljana Pokimica, Vesna Vučić, Miloš Čanković, Ž. Popović, Milos Cankovic, Andjelija Petrovic
摘要中文翻译口腔扁平苔藓 (OLP) 是年轻男性的一种非典型表现,其与 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的共存引起了临床对恶性转化的担忧。病例报告 描述了一名性活跃的 27 岁男性,患有 OLP,并可能持续、同步口腔和多基因型生殖器 HPV 合并感染。一名 27 岁男性在手术切除具有苔藓样特征且经组织病理学证实的 OLP 的口腔乳头状瘤后 19 个月进行随访。通过 CFX96 实时 PCR 使用口腔和生殖器拭子进行分子检测和基因分型。结果显示,高危型HPV 31 和低风险 HPV 44 可能存在持续性口腔双重感染。生殖器筛查检测到这些相同的口腔基因型以及另外四种菌株,包括 高危型HPV 18、35 和 73(根据 IARC 标准分类)和低风险 HPV 81,具有临床明显的尖锐湿疣。在 OLP 存在的情况下,在口腔拭子中长期检测到 高危型HPV 31 突显了一种假设的“双重打击”致癌框架,其中炎症诱导的氧化应激可能与病毒 E6 和 E7 癌蛋白协同作用,破坏细胞周期调节并提高恶性转化风险,尽管在缺乏机械生物标志物的情况下,这仍然完全是推测性的。年轻 OLP 患者同时口腔和肛门生殖器 HPV 合并感染需要综合的多学科诊断方案和谨慎的肿瘤学 监测 建议,以防止恶性进展和进一步 传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查患有口腔扁平苔藓 (OLP) 的年轻成年男性口腔和生殖器区域 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的共存情况,解决潜在恶变的临床担忧。
设计与对象
病例报告 描述了一名性活跃的 27 岁男性,患有 OLP,并可能持续、同步口腔和多基因型生殖器 HPV 合并感染。
核心方法
通过 CFX96 实时 PCR 使用口腔和生殖器拭子进行分子检测和基因分型。
主要结果
结果显示,高危型HPV 31 和低危 HPV 44 可能存在持续口腔双重感染,同时生殖器区域还存在四种其他毒株,包括 高危型HPV 18、35 和 73 以及低危 HPV 81。
解释与创新
在 OLP 存在的情况下,在口腔拭子中长期检测到 高危型HPV 31 突显了一种假设的“双重打击”致癌框架,其中炎症诱导的氧化应激可能与病毒 E6 和 E7 癌蛋白协同作用,破坏细胞周期调节并提高恶性转化风险,尽管在缺乏机械生物标志物的情况下,这仍然完全是推测性的。
科研与公卫意义
年轻 OLP 患者同时口腔和肛门生殖器 HPV 合并感染需要综合的多学科诊断方案和谨慎的肿瘤学 监测 建议,以防止恶性进展和进一步 传播。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到 病例报告 设计的限制,无法对研究结果进行概括。

Coincident Detection of Human Papillomavirus (HPV) in the Oral and Genital Regions in a Young Adult Male with Oral Lichen Planus: A Case Report

Authors: Andjelija Petrović, Željko D. Popović, Biljana Pokimica, Vesna Vučić, Miloš Čanković, Ž. Popović, Milos Cankovic, Andjelija Petrovic
Original abstract (English)
Oral lichen planus (OLP) is an atypical presentation in young males, and its co-occurrence with human papillomavirus (HPV) raises clinical concerns regarding malignant transformation. This case report describes a 27-year-old sexually active male with OLP and potentially persistent, synchronous oral and multigenotypic genital HPV co-infections. A 27-year-old male presented for follow-up 19 months after surgical excision of an oral papilloma with lichenoid features and histopathologically confirmed OLP. Molecular detection and genotyping were performed using oral and genital swabs via CFX96 real-time PCR. Results revealed potentially persistent oral co-infection with high-risk HPV 31 and low-risk HPV 44. Genital screening detected these same oral genotypes alongside four additional strains, consisting of high-risk HPV 18, 35, and 73 (classified per IARC standards), and low-risk HPV 81, with clinically evident condylomata acuminata. The long-term detection of high-risk HPV 31 in the oral swab in the presence of OLP highlights a hypothetical “dual-hit” oncogenic framework, where inflammation-induced oxidative stress may synergize with viral E6 and E7 oncoproteins to disrupt cell cycle regulation and elevate malignant transformation risks, though this remains entirely speculative in the absence of mechanistic biomarkers. Synchronous oral and anogenital HPV co-infections in young patients with OLP warrant a comprehensive multidisciplinary diagnostic protocol and cautious oncologic surveillance recommendations to prevent malignant progression and further transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the co-occurrence of human papillomavirus (HPV) in the oral and genital regions in a young adult male with oral lichen planus (OLP), addressing the clinical concern of potential malignant transformation.
Design and population
This case report described a 27-year-old sexually active male with OLP and potentially persistent, synchronous oral and multigenotypic genital HPV co-infections.
Core methods
Molecular detection and genotyping were performed using oral and genital swabs via CFX96 real-time PCR.
Main results
Results revealed potentially persistent oral co-infection with high-risk HPV 31 and low-risk HPV 44, alongside four additional strains in the genital region, including high-risk HPV 18, 35, and 73, and low-risk HPV 81.
Interpretation and novelty
The long-term detection of high-risk HPV 31 in the oral swab in the presence of OLP highlights a hypothetical “dual-hit” oncogenic framework, where inflammation-induced oxidative stress may synergize with viral E6 and E7 oncoproteins to disrupt cell cycle regulation and elevate malignant transformation risks, though this remains entirely speculative in the absence of mechanistic biomarkers.
Scientific and public-health significance
Synchronous oral and anogenital HPV co-infections in young patients with OLP warrant a comprehensive multidisciplinary diagnostic protocol and cautious oncologic surveillance recommendations to prevent malignant progression and further transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its case report design, which does not allow for generalization of findings.
