全身炎症标志物不能独立预测 3 级宫颈上皮内瘤变患者行环形电切术后切缘阳性:一项单中心回顾性研究

Systemic inflammatory markers do not independently predict positive surgical margins after loop electrosurgical excision procedure in patients with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 3: a single-center retrospective study
作者： LIU J, Fengying Wu, Qiang Deng, Tingzhu Meng, Qianhui Ke, Xiyan Ouyang, Han Gao, Yanli Li, Jie Shi, Jing LIU
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨术前临床病理因素和全身炎症标志物对宫颈上皮内瘤变3级(CIN3)患者宫颈环形电切术(LEEP)术后切缘阳性的预测价值,并建立列线图预测模型。
设计与对象
湖北省妇幼保健院开展了一项单中心回顾性研究,纳入 2021 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间接受 LEEP 的 1,373 例 CIN3 患者。患者按 7:3 的比例随机分配到训练组(n=961)和验证组(n=412)。
核心方法
收集术前人口统计学特征、细胞学结果(TCT分级)、HPV基因型、阴道镜活检病理学和血液学参数。使用 LASSO 回归在训练集中选择预测变量,然后进行多变量逻辑回归分析。模型辨别力通过受试者工作特征曲线 (AUC) 下面积、校准图和 Hosmer-Lemeshow 检验进行校准以及决策曲线分析 (DCA) 的临床效用进行评估。根据最终模型构建列线图。
主要结果
总共纳入 1,373 例 CIN3 患者,其中 129 例(9.4%)手术切缘呈阳性。全身炎症标志物不能独立预测切缘状态。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,基于术前 TCT 分级、年龄、BMI、多重 HPV 感染和产次的列线图可以准确预测 CIN3 患者 LEEP 后切缘阳性的风险,验证 AUC 为 0.80。
科研与公卫意义
该模型可能有助于CIN3患者的术前风险评估和个体化手术决策。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项单中心回顾性研究,可能限制普遍性。证据仅基于抽象数据,研究结果的可信度中等。

Systemic inflammatory markers do not independently predict positive surgical margins after loop electrosurgical excision procedure in patients with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 3: a single-center retrospective study

Authors: LIU J, Fengying Wu, Qiang Deng, Tingzhu Meng, Qianhui Ke, Xiyan Ouyang, Han Gao, Yanli Li, Jie Shi, Jing LIU
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Objective : To investigate the predictive value of preoperative clinicopathological factors and systemic inflammatory markers for positive surgical margin after loop electrosurgical excision procedure (LEEP) in patients with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 3 (CIN3), and to develop a nomogram prediction model. Methods : A total of 1,373 patients with CIN3 who underwent LEEP at Hubei Provincial Maternal and Child Health Hospital between January 2021 and December 2025 were retrospectively included. Patients were randomly assigned at a 7:3 ratio to a training set "n=961" and a validation set "n=412" . Preoperative demographic characteristics, cytology results (TCT grade), HPV genotypes, colposcopic biopsy pathology, and hematological parameters (including complete blood count, coagulation, and lipid profiles) were collected. LASSO regression was used to select predictors in the training set, followed by multivariable logistic regression analysis. Model discrimination was evaluated by the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC), calibration by calibration plots and the Hosmer-Lemeshow test, and clinical utility by decision curve analysis (DCA). A nomogram was constructed based on the final model. Results : A total of 1,373 CIN3 patients were included, among whom 129 (9.4%) had positive surgical margins. LASSO regression identified five predictors: TCT grade, age, BMI, multiple HPV infection, and parity. Multivariable logistic regression showed that TCT grade (aOR = 1.27, 95% CI: 1.10–1.48, P = 0.002) and age "aOR=1.05,P Conclusion : A nomogram based on preoperative TCT grade, age, BMI, multiple HPV infection, and parity can accurately predict the risk of positive margin after LEEP in CIN3 patients, with a validation AUC of 0.80. Systemic inflammatory markers do not independently predict margin status. This model may assist in preoperative risk assessment and individualized surgical decision-making.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the predictive value of preoperative clinicopathological factors and systemic inflammatory markers for positive surgical margins after loop electrosurgical excision procedure (LEEP) in patients with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 3 (CIN3), and to develop a nomogram prediction model.
Design and population
A single-center retrospective study was conducted at Hubei Provincial Maternal and Child Health Hospital, including 1,373 patients with CIN3 who underwent LEEP between January 2021 and December 2025. Patients were randomly assigned at a 7:3 ratio to a training set (n=961) and a validation set (n=412).
Core methods
Preoperative demographic characteristics, cytology results (TCT grade), HPV genotypes, colposcopic biopsy pathology, and hematological parameters were collected. LASSO regression was used to select predictors in the training set, followed by multivariable logistic regression analysis. Model discrimination was evaluated by the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC), calibration by calibration plots and the Hosmer-Lemeshow test, and clinical utility by decision curve analysis (DCA). A nomogram was constructed based on the final model.
Main results
A total of 1,373 CIN3 patients were included, among whom 129 (9.4%) had positive surgical margins. Systemic inflammatory markers do not independently predict margin status.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that a nomogram based on preoperative TCT grade, age, BMI, multiple HPV infection, and parity can accurately predict the risk of positive margin after LEEP in CIN3 patients, with a validation AUC of 0.80.
Scientific and public-health significance
This model may assist in preoperative risk assessment and individualized surgical decision-making for patients with CIN3.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a single-center retrospective study, which may limit generalizability. The evidence is based on abstract-only data, and the confidence in the findings is moderate.

委内瑞拉三个土著社区中人乳头瘤病毒基因型谱的差异:对疫苗选择和宫颈筛查的启示

Divergent Human Papillomavirus Genotype Spectra in Three Indigenous Communities of Venezuela: Implications for Vaccine Choice and Cervical Screening
作者： María Fátima Garcés, Jefferson Guzmán Silva, Daniel Aranguren, Lina M Figueira, Ricardo Blanch, Marwan Said Aguilar Mejía, Yacelli Bustamante Siberio, Juan Bautista De Sanctis, Alexis Hipólito García, Marwan Said Aguilar-Mejía
摘要中文翻译背景:委内瑞拉土著妇女几乎没有 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 的基因型解析数据。方法:我们研究了来自 La Guajira (Wayuu,n = 94)、Maniapure (Eñepa,n = 141) 和 Delta Amacuro (Warao,n = 25) 的 260 名女性,通过单一实时 PCR 检测处理所有自行收集的样本,可解析 21 种基因型;144 例可进行细胞学检查。结果:HPV 患病率 为 55.4% (95% CI 49.3–61.3),高危 患病率 为 51.2% (95% CI 45.1–57.2),从拉瓜希拉的 42.6% 上升到马尼亚普雷的 59.6% 和三角洲的 80.0%阿马库罗(p = 0.001)。检测到十九种基因型。HPV-31 最常见(15.8%),其次是 HPV-16(11.5%),每个社区都有自己的概况,其中 HPV-68 和 HPV-56 在阿马库罗三角洲领先。一半的受感染女性携带不止一种类型。细胞学和 HPV 检测几乎没有重叠:78 名 HPV 阳性涂片女性中,有 20 名 (25.6%) 结果异常 (κ = 0.056)。69.4% 的受感染女性中至少存在一种非价 疫苗 类型,而二价 疫苗 的比例为 30.6%。结论:HPV 疫苗接种 不属于委内瑞拉国家计划的一部分;这些数据支持非有效配方,并且基于 HPV 的筛查需要为许多检测呈阳性的女性提供可行的分类途径。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决委内瑞拉土著妇女中缺乏有关 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 基因型解析数据的问题,强调需要了解区域 HPV 多样性,以便为 疫苗 和筛查策略提供信息。
设计与对象
对来自委内瑞拉三个土著社区的 260 名妇女进行了一项横断面研究:La Guajira(Wayuu,n = 94)、Maniapure(Eñepa,n = 141)和 Delta Amacuro(Warao,n = 25)。所有自行收集的样本均通过能够解析 21 种基因型的单一实时 PCR 检测进行处理。
核心方法
该研究使用单一实时 PCR 检测来检测 21 种 HPV 基因型,并对 144 个样本进行了细胞学检查。该分析包括 患病率 计算和跨社区的比较。
主要结果
HPV 患病率 为 55.4% (95% CI 49.3–61.3),高危 患病率 范围从拉瓜希拉的 42.6% 到阿马库罗三角洲的 80.0%。检测到 19 种基因型,其中 HPV-31 最为常见(15.8%),其次是 HPV-16(11.5%)。一半的受感染女性携带不止一种类型。
解释与创新
研究结果揭示了 HPV 基因型分布的显着区域差异,表明一刀切的 疫苗接种 和筛查方法可能不是最佳的。该研究强调了基因型特异性策略在 HPV 预防中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果对委内瑞拉的 HPV 疫苗 选择和宫颈筛查计划有直接影响,强调需要根据当地基因型 患病率 制定定制方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计和使用单一 PCR 检测的限制,可能无法捕获所有 HPV 类型。证据是中等的,因为它基于没有纵向随访的 患病率 数据。

Divergent Human Papillomavirus Genotype Spectra in Three Indigenous Communities of Venezuela: Implications for Vaccine Choice and Cervical Screening

Authors: María Fátima Garcés, Jefferson Guzmán Silva, Daniel Aranguren, Lina M Figueira, Ricardo Blanch, Marwan Said Aguilar Mejía, Yacelli Bustamante Siberio, Juan Bautista De Sanctis, Alexis Hipólito García, Marwan Said Aguilar-Mejía
Original abstract (English)
Background: Genotype-resolved data on human papillomavirus (HPV) are almost absent for Venezuelan indigenous women. Methods: We studied 260 women from La Guajira (Wayuu, n = 94), Maniapure (Eñepa, n = 141), and Delta Amacuro (Warao, n = 25), processing all self-collected samples with a single real-time PCR assay that resolves 21 genotypes; cytology was available for 144. Results: HPV prevalence was 55.4% (95% CI 49.3–61.3) and high-risk prevalence was 51.2% (95% CI 45.1–57.2), rising from 42.6% in La Guajira to 59.6% in Maniapure and 80.0% in Delta Amacuro (p = 0.001). Nineteen genotypes were detected. HPV-31 was most frequent (15.8%), followed by HPV-16 (11.5%), and each community had its own profile, with HPV-68 and HPV-56 leading in Delta Amacuro. Half of the infected women carried more than one type. Cytology and HPV testing overlapped little: 20 of 78 HPV-positive women with a smear (25.6%) had an abnormal result (κ = 0.056). At least one nonavalent vaccine type was present in 69.4% of infected women, compared with 30.6% for the bivalent vaccine. Conclusions: HPV vaccination is not part of the Venezuelan national program; these data favor the nonavalent formulation, and HPV-based screening would need a feasible triage pathway for the many women who test positive.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of genotype-resolved data on human papillomavirus (HPV) in Venezuelan indigenous women, highlighting the need for understanding regional HPV diversity to inform vaccine and screening strategies.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted among 260 women from three indigenous communities in Venezuela: La Guajira (Wayuu, n = 94), Maniapure (Eñepa, n = 141), and Delta Amacuro (Warao, n = 25). All self-collected samples were processed with a single real-time PCR assay capable of resolving 21 genotypes.
Core methods
The study used a single real-time PCR assay to detect 21 HPV genotypes and performed cytology on 144 samples. The analysis included prevalence calculations and comparisons across communities.
Main results
HPV prevalence was 55.4% (95% CI 49.3–61.3), with high-risk prevalence ranging from 42.6% in La Guajira to 80.0% in Delta Amacuro. Nineteen genotypes were detected, and HPV-31 was the most frequent (15.8%), followed by HPV-16 (11.5%). Half of the infected women carried more than one type.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal significant regional variation in HPV genotype distribution, suggesting that a one-size-fits-all approach to vaccination and screening may not be optimal. The study highlights the importance of genotype-specific strategies in HPV prevention.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results have direct implications for HPV vaccine selection and cervical screening programs in Venezuela, emphasizing the need for tailored approaches based on local genotype prevalence.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and the use of a single PCR assay, which may not capture all HPV types. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on prevalence data without longitudinal follow-up.

中东地区的健康信念和家长对 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 的反应:以健康信念模型为指导的基于理论的叙事综合

Health beliefs and parental responses to human papillomavirus vaccination in the Middle East: a theory-informed narrative synthesis guided by the health belief model
作者： Ahmad Abuzoor, Md. Shafiqur Rahman Jabin, Cor Jonker
摘要中文翻译背景 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是女性中最常见的性传播感染,也是 宫颈癌 (CC) 的主要原因。HPV 疫苗接种 和筛查计划已在全球范围内显着减少了 CC 发病率 和 死亡率,预防了高达 70% 的 HPV 相关 CC 和 90% 的生殖器疣。因此,制定有效的 疫苗接种 策略至关重要。目的 本研究系统地综合了中东 (ME) 父母对 HPV 疫苗接种 的态度和健康信念,检查父母 疫苗 的吸收情况,并评估其子女的 疫苗 吸收情况。方法 本系统评价遵循 PRISMA 指南。对 PubMed/MEDLINE、Web of Science、CINAHL 和 Google Scholar 搜索了 2006 年 1 月至 2025 年 5 月期间发表的研究。纳入了 33 项研究。对主要定量证据进行了基于理论的叙述综合,并将研究结果演绎映射到健康信念模型(HBM)构造。审查方案已在 PROSPERO 中注册(CRD42025645551)。结果 该评价包括 32 项定量横断面研究和一项定性研究,涉及 18,170 名参与者。研究结果根据 HBM 结构进行组织,包括感知的易感性、严重性、障碍、益处、自我效能和行动线索。家长普遍表现出对 HPV 和 疫苗接种 的了解有限。主要障碍包括安全问题、成本、访问受限、缺乏医疗保健提供者建议以及信息不足。文化和系统因素也与 疫苗 的吸收相关。结论 中东地区国家的 HPV 相关知识仍然不足,并且经常与纳入研究中的 疫苗接种 态度和接受程度相关。提高吸收率需要有针对性的教育干预、医疗保健提供者的参与、公共卫生运动、财政支持和政策层面的举措。加强意识和解决结构性障碍对于提高 疫苗接种 的接受度至关重要。直接报告的自我效能似乎与更有利的 疫苗 态度相关;然而,在纳入的研究中,父母自我效能感作为一种独特的 HBM 结构的直接证据有限,不应被夸大。系统审核注册 https://www.crd.york.ac.uk/PROSPERO/myprospero/CRD42025645551。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是女性中最常见的性传播感染,也是 宫颈癌 (CC) 的主要原因。因此,制定有效的 疫苗接种 策略至关重要。
设计与对象
本研究系统地综合了中东 (ME) 父母对 HPV 疫苗接种 的态度和健康信念,检查了父母 疫苗 的吸收情况,并评估了其子女的吸收情况。包括三十三项研究。
核心方法
本次系统评价遵循 PRISMA 指南。对主要定量证据进行了基于理论的叙述综合,并将研究结果演绎映射到健康信念模型(HBM)构造。
主要结果
该审查包括 32 项定量横断面研究和一项定性研究,涉及 18,170 名参与者。研究结果根据 HBM 结构进行组织,包括感知的易感性、严重性、障碍、益处、自我效能和行动线索。家长普遍表现出对 HPV 和 疫苗接种 的了解有限。
解释与创新
该综述强调了解决文化和系统因素对提高 HPV 疫苗接种 接受度的重要性。直接报告的自我效能似乎与更有利的 疫苗 态度相关;然而,在纳入的研究中,父母自我效能感作为一种独特的 HBM 结构的直接证据有限,不应被夸大。
科研与公卫意义
提高吸收率需要有针对性的教育干预、医疗保健提供者的参与、公共卫生运动、财政支持和政策层面的举措。加强意识和解决结构性障碍对于提高 疫苗接种 的接受度至关重要。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据并未报告研究设计的具体局限性。证据强度中等,因为研究结果基于现有研究的叙述综合,而不是原始数据。

Health beliefs and parental responses to human papillomavirus vaccination in the Middle East: a theory-informed narrative synthesis guided by the health belief model

Authors: Ahmad Abuzoor, Md. Shafiqur Rahman Jabin, Cor Jonker
Original abstract (English)
Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the most common sexually transmitted infection in women and the primary cause of cervical cancer (CC). HPV vaccination and screening programs have significantly reduced CC incidence and mortality worldwide, preventing up to 70% of HPV-related CC and 90% of genital warts. Developing effective vaccination strategies is therefore essential. Objectives This study systematically synthesizes the attitudes and health beliefs of Middle Eastern (ME) parents regarding HPV vaccination, examines parental vaccine uptake, and assesses uptake among their children. Methods This systematic review followed PRISMA guidelines. PubMed/MEDLINE, Web of Science, CINAHL, and Google Scholar were searched for studies published between January 2006 and May 2025. Thirty-three studies were included. A theory-informed narrative synthesis of predominantly quantitative evidence was conducted, with deductive mapping of findings to the health belief model (HBM) constructs. The review protocol was registered in PROSPERO (CRD42025645551). Results The review included 32 quantitative cross-sectional studies and one qualitative study, involving 18,170 participants. Findings were organized according to HBM constructs, including perceived susceptibility, severity, barriers, benefits, self-efficacy, and cues to action. Parents generally demonstrated limited knowledge of HPV and vaccination. Major barriers included safety concerns, cost, limited access, lack of healthcare provider recommendations, and insufficient information. Cultural and systemic factors were also associated with vaccine uptake. Conclusion HPV-related knowledge in ME countries remains insufficient and is frequently associated with vaccination attitudes and acceptance across the included studies. Improving uptake requires targeted educational interventions, healthcare provider engagement, public health campaigns, financial support, and policy-level initiatives. Strengthening awareness and addressing structural barriers are critical for enhancing vaccination acceptance. Self-efficacy, where directly reported, appeared to be associated with more favorable vaccine attitudes; however, direct evidence for parental self-efficacy as a distinct HBM construct was limited across the included studies and should not be overstated. Systematic review registration https://www.crd.york.ac.uk/PROSPERO/myprospero/CRD42025645551 .
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the most common sexually transmitted infection in women and the primary cause of cervical cancer (CC). Developing effective vaccination strategies is therefore essential.
Design and population
This study systematically synthesizes the attitudes and health beliefs of Middle Eastern (ME) parents regarding HPV vaccination, examines parental vaccine uptake, and assesses uptake among their children. Thirty-three studies were included.
Core methods
This systematic review followed PRISMA guidelines. A theory-informed narrative synthesis of predominantly quantitative evidence was conducted, with deductive mapping of findings to the health belief model (HBM) constructs.
Main results
The review included 32 quantitative cross-sectional studies and one qualitative study, involving 18,170 participants. Findings were organized according to HBM constructs, including perceived susceptibility, severity, barriers, benefits, self-efficacy, and cues to action. Parents generally demonstrated limited knowledge of HPV and vaccination.
Interpretation and novelty
The review highlights the importance of addressing cultural and systemic factors to improve HPV vaccination acceptance. Self-efficacy, where directly reported, appeared to be associated with more favorable vaccine attitudes; however, direct evidence for parental self-efficacy as a distinct HBM construct was limited across the included studies and should not be overstated.
Scientific and public-health significance
Improving uptake requires targeted educational interventions, healthcare provider engagement, public health campaigns, financial support, and policy-level initiatives. Strengthening awareness and addressing structural barriers are critical for enhancing vaccination acceptance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design. The evidence strength is moderate, as the findings are based on a narrative synthesis of existing studies and not on original data.

宫颈癌 的基于血清游离脂肪酸的诊断指数(包括 高危型HPV 阴性病例):一项独立验证研究

Serum Free Fatty Acid-Based Diagnostic Index for Cervical Cancer Including High-Risk HPV-Negative Cases: An Independent Validation Study
作者： Yuki Katoh, Yosuke Fujii, Nagi Yamazaki, Akiko Kubo, Saki Kamata, Yuki Okuma, Ting Yang, Ayase Futatsugi, Masaki Sakamoto, Hirotsugu Hino
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:宫颈癌 (CC) 通常由高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (hrHPV) 引起;然而,目前的筛查计划无法检测到一些 hrHPV 阴性病例。我们在独立的 队列研究 中评估了先前建立的基于血清游离脂肪酸 (FFA) 的诊断模型,包括 hrHPV 阴性 CC。方法:我们分析了 101 名 CC 患者(16 hrHPV 阴性)、60 名健康捐献者和 16 名良性妇科疾病患者(均未纳入既往研究)的血清 FFA,以及 64 份手术 CC 和 5 份正常宫颈样本中脂肪酸代谢相关酶的基因表达水平。结果:9 个 FFA 水平在 CC 和非癌症组之间存在差异,但在 hrHPV 阴性和 hrHPV 阳性 CC 之间没有差异。与正常组织相比,CC中乙酰辅酶A羧化酶1、脂肪酸合酶、硬脂酰辅酶A去饱和酶1和单酰甘油脂肪酶的基因表达水平升高,而hrHPV阴性和hrHPV阳性CC之间没有显着差异。之前的模型(诊断指数 = −5.631 + 0.030 ×(硬脂酸)+ 9.188 ×(二高-γ-亚麻酸))将 CC 与非癌症组区分开来(曲线下面积,0.969)。在预先指定的临界值下,健康供体的敏感性为 97.0%(hrHPV 阴性 CC,93.8%),特异性为 58.3%;按照此 队列研究 中得出的截止值,这些值分别为 82.2% (93.8%) 和 98.3%。hrHPV阴性CC中,阳性率超过鳞状细胞癌抗原、癌胚抗原、CA125。结论:基于血清 FFA 的诊断模型在独立的 队列研究 中检测到 CC,包括 hrHPV 阴性病例。通过校准截止值,该方法可以补充现有的基于 HPV 的检测。

基于血清 FFA 的诊断模型在独立的 队列研究 中检测到 CC,包括 hrHPV 阴性病例。
科研与公卫意义
通过校准截止值,该方法可以补充现有的基于 HPV 的检测。
局限与证据强度
在预先指定的临界值下,健康供体的敏感性为 97.0%(hrHPV 阴性 CC,93.8%),特异性为 58.3%;按照此 队列研究 中得出的截止值,这些值分别为 82.2% (93.8%) 和 98.3%。

Serum Free Fatty Acid-Based Diagnostic Index for Cervical Cancer Including High-Risk HPV-Negative Cases: An Independent Validation Study

Authors: Yuki Katoh, Yosuke Fujii, Nagi Yamazaki, Akiko Kubo, Saki Kamata, Yuki Okuma, Ting Yang, Ayase Futatsugi, Masaki Sakamoto, Hirotsugu Hino
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Cervical cancer (CC) is usually caused by high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV); however, some hrHPV-negative cases are undetectable by current screening programs. We evaluated a previously established diagnostic model based on serum free fatty acids (FFAs) in an independent cohort including hrHPV-negative CC. Methods: We analyzed serum FFAs from 101 patients with CC (16 hrHPV-negative), 60 healthy donors, and 16 patients with benign gynecological diseases, none of whom were included in the previous study, and gene expression levels of fatty acid metabolism-related enzymes in 64 surgical CC and five normal cervix samples. Results: Levels of nine FFAs differed between CC and the non-cancer groups but not between hrHPV-negative and hrHPV-positive CC. Gene expression levels of acetyl-CoA carboxylase 1, fatty acid synthase, stearoyl-CoA desaturase 1, and monoacylglycerol lipase were increased in CC compared with normal tissues, with no significant differences between hrHPV-negative and hrHPV-positive CC. The previous model (diagnostic index = −5.631 + 0.030 × (stearic acid) + 9.188 × (dihomo-γ-linolenic acid)) distinguished CC from the non-cancer groups (area under the curve, 0.969). At the pre-specified cut-off value, sensitivity was 97.0% (hrHPV-negative CC, 93.8%) and specificity in healthy donors was 58.3%; at the cut-off value derived in this cohort, these were 82.2% (93.8%) and 98.3%, respectively. In hrHPV-negative CC, the positivity rate exceeded those of squamous cell carcinoma antigen, carcinoembryonic antigen, and CA125. Conclusions: The serum-FFA-based diagnostic model detected CC, including hrHPV-negative cases, in an independent cohort. With calibration of the cut-off value, this approach may complement existing HPV-based testing.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer (CC) is usually caused by high-risk human papillomavirus (hrHPV); however, some hrHPV-negative cases are undetectable by current screening programs.
Design and population
We evaluated a previously established diagnostic model based on serum free fatty acids (FFAs) in an independent cohort including hrHPV-negative CC.
Core methods
We analyzed serum FFAs from 101 patients with CC (16 hrHPV-negative), 60 healthy donors, and 16 patients with benign gynecological diseases, none of whom were included in the previous study, and gene expression levels of fatty acid metabolism-related enzymes in 64 surgical CC and five normal cervix samples.
Main results
Levels of nine FFAs differed between CC and the non-cancer groups but not between hrHPV-negative and hrHPV-positive CC. Gene expression levels of acetyl-CoA carboxylase 1, fatty acid synthase, stearoyl-CoA desaturase 1, and monoacylglycerol lipase were increased in CC compared with normal tissues, with no significant differences between hrHPV-negative and hrHPV-positive CC. The previous model (diagnostic index = −5.631 + 0.030 × (stearic acid) + 9.188 × (dihomo-γ-linolenic acid)) distinguished CC from the non-cancer groups (area under the curve, 0.969).
Interpretation and novelty
The serum-FFA-based diagnostic model detected CC, including hrHPV-negative cases, in an independent cohort.
Scientific and public-health significance
With calibration of the cut-off value, this approach may complement existing HPV-based testing.
Limitations and evidence strength
At the pre-specified cut-off value, sensitivity was 97.0% (hrHPV-negative CC, 93.8%) and specificity in healthy donors was 58.3%; at the cut-off value derived in this cohort, these were 82.2% (93.8%) and 98.3%, respectively.

日本大学生 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种 的性别差异及相关因素:一项检验知识和互动中介效应的横断面研究

Sex differences in human papillomavirus vaccination among Japanese university students and associated factors: a cross-sectional study examining the mediating effect of knowledge and interactions
作者： Chieko Yodawara, Yoko Iio, Harumi Ejiri, Yukihiro Mori, Manato Seguchi, Yuka Aoyama, Morihiro Ito
摘要中文翻译背景 日本大学生,尤其是男性学生中,人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 率仍然较低。自 2013 年暂停主动推荐以来,疫苗接种 费率自 2022 年恢复以来一直没有改善;男性疫苗接种尚未建立为常规。目的 描述性别特异性 HPV 疫苗接种 状态和相关因素,检查相关模式中的性别差异,并检查 HPV 相关知识的潜在中介作用。方法 这项基于网络的横断面调查于 2025 年 4 月在日本一所大学的本科生中进行。对 3203 名参与者(1760 名男性,1443 名女性)的数据进行了分析。自我报告的 疫苗接种 病史是主要结果。进行了多变量逻辑回归、基于引导的中介分析和交互分析。结果 疫苗接种 率为 30.1%(男性为 3.2%,女性为 62.9%;P < 0.001)。男性与 疫苗接种 呈强烈负相关(OR = 0.024;95% 置信区间 [CI]:0.017–0.034)。对免费 疫苗接种 (OR = 3.477) 和 HPV 相关疾病 (OR = 1.758) 的了解呈正相关。在女性中,只有知识变量是显着的,而在男性中,生活方式变量也相关。性别通过HPV相关知识对疫苗接种的间接影响具有统计学意义(间接影响β=-0.263;95% CI:-0.383至-0.141),表明部分中介作用。在未接种疫苗的男性中,81.1% 的人表示不需要 疫苗;50.4% 未接种疫苗的女性表示担心不良反应。结论 需要立法强制执行男性常规 疫苗接种、补贴费用、家长教育以及基于性别的大学课程。

Sex differences in human papillomavirus vaccination among Japanese university students and associated factors: a cross-sectional study examining the mediating effect of knowledge and interactions

Authors: Chieko Yodawara, Yoko Iio, Harumi Ejiri, Yukihiro Mori, Manato Seguchi, Yuka Aoyama, Morihiro Ito
Original abstract (English)
Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination rates among Japanese university students, especially among males, remain low. Following the suspension of active recommendations in 2013, vaccination rates have not improved since their resumption in 2022; male vaccination has not been established as a routine. Objectives To describe sex-specific HPV vaccination status and associated factors, examine sex differences in associated patterns, and examine the potential mediating role of HPV-related knowledge. Methods This web-based cross-sectional survey was conducted among undergraduates at a Japanese university in April 2025. The data of 3203 participants (1760 males, 1443 females) were analyzed. Self-reported vaccination history was the primary outcome. Multivariable logistic regression, bootstrap-based mediation analysis, and interaction analysis were performed. Results The vaccination rate was 30.1% (3.2% among males vs. 62.9% among females; P < 0.001). Male sex was strongly negatively associated with vaccination (OR = 0.024; 95% confidence interval [CI]: 0.017–0.034). Knowledge of free vaccination (OR = 3.477) and HPV-related diseases (OR = 1.758) showed positive associations. In females, only knowledge variables were significant, whereas in males, lifestyle variables were also associated. The indirect effect of sex on vaccination via HPV-related knowledge was statistically significant (indirect effect β = −0.263; 95% CI: −0.383 to −0.141), indicating partial mediation. Among unvaccinated males, 81.1% reported not needing the vaccine; 50.4% of unvaccinated females cited concerns about adverse effects. Conclusions Legislation mandating routine vaccination for males, subsidized costs, parental education, and sex-specific university-based programs are needed.

Question and background
Background Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination rates among Japanese university students, especially among males, remain low.
Design and population
This web-based cross-sectional survey was conducted among undergraduates at a Japanese university in April 2025. The data of 3203 participants (1760 males, 1443 females) were analyzed. Following the suspension of active recommendations in 2013, vaccination rates have not improved since their resumption in 2022; male vaccination has not been established as a routine.
Core methods
Multivariable logistic regression, bootstrap-based mediation analysis, and interaction analysis were performed. The primary outcome was self-reported vaccination history.
Main results
The vaccination rate was 30.1% (3.2% among males vs 62.9% among females; P < 0.001). Male sex was strongly negatively associated with vaccination (OR = 0.024; 95% confidence interval [CI]: 0.017–0.034). Knowledge of free vaccination (OR = 3.477) and HPV-related diseases (OR = 1.758) showed positive associations. In females, only knowledge variables were significant, whereas in males, lifestyle variables were also associated.
Interpretation and novelty
The indirect effect of sex on vaccination via HPV-related knowledge was statistically significant (indirect effect β = −0.263; 95% CI: −0.383 to −0.141), indicating partial mediation.
Scientific and public-health significance
Legislation mandating routine vaccination for males, subsidized costs, parental education, and sex-specific university-based programs are needed.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the limitations of the study design or the strength of the evidence.

组织学证实的宫颈 HSIL 中 高危型HPV 基因型模式的转变:浸润性鳞状细胞癌的时间变化和差异分布

Shifting High-Risk HPV Genotype Patterns in Histologically Confirmed Cervical HSIL: Temporal Changes and Differential Distribution in Invasive Squamous Cell Carcinoma
作者： Cristina Díaz del Arco, Mar Ramírez Mena, Silvia Rojas Calles, Fernando Durán Botía, Mónica Bellón del Amo, Ricardo García Martínez, Luis Ortega Medina, Fernando Durán-Botía, Mónica Bellón-del-Amo, Ricardo García-Martínez
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查西班牙机会性筛查环境中经组织学证实的宫颈高级鳞状上皮内病变 (HSIL) 中高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 基因型模式的时间变化以及这些病变与浸润性鳞状细胞癌 (SCC) 之间的差异。
设计与对象
This retrospective study included 371 patients with HSIL from 2011 to 2016, 411 with HSIL from 2022 to 2025, and 65 with SCC from 2022 to 2025.
核心方法
该研究分析了宫颈 HSIL 和 SCC 病例中的 HR-HPV 基因型模式,调整年龄和细胞学 诊断 以评估时间和 队列研究 相关的变化。
主要结果
在 HR-HPV 阳性 HSIL 中,HPV16/18 频率从 65.7% 下降至 50.8% (p = 0.003),同时检测到的基因型平均数量较低。调整年龄和细胞学 诊断 后,2022-2025 年期间 HPV16(OR 0.59)和 HPV18(OR 0.36)检测率较低。SCC 显示 HPV16/18 频率较高(75.0% vs. 50.8%;p = 0.003),多重感染较少,其他 HR-HPV 基因型的代表性较低。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,宫颈 HSIL 中的 HR-HPV 基因型模式随着时间和出生 队列研究 的变化而发生变化,其中 HPV16/18 患病率 显着减少,而其他基因型增加,而 SCC 保留了较高的 HPV16/18 代表性和较低的 HPV 多样性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以通过强调变化的 HR-HPV 基因型分布及其对疾病进展和预防的影响,为 宫颈癌 监测 和筛查策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,基于机会性筛选,这可能会引入偏差并限制普遍性。由于这些限制,证据是中等的。

Shifting High-Risk HPV Genotype Patterns in Histologically Confirmed Cervical HSIL: Temporal Changes and Differential Distribution in Invasive Squamous Cell Carcinoma

Authors: Cristina Díaz del Arco, Mar Ramírez Mena, Silvia Rojas Calles, Fernando Durán Botía, Mónica Bellón del Amo, Ricardo García Martínez, Luis Ortega Medina, Fernando Durán-Botía, Mónica Bellón-del-Amo, Ricardo García-Martínez
Original abstract (English)
High-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) genotypes differ in their oncogenic potential and contribution to cervical precancer and invasive cancer. We investigated temporal changes in HR-HPV genotype patterns in histologically confirmed cervical high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions (HSIL) and differences between 2022–2025 HSIL and invasive squamous cell carcinoma (SCC) in an opportunistic screening setting in Spain. This retrospective study included 371 patients with HSIL from 2011 to 2016, 411 with HSIL from 2022 to 2025, and 65 with SCC from 2022 to 2025. Among HR-HPV-positive HSIL, HPV16/18 frequency decreased from 65.7% to 50.8% (p = 0.003), accompanied by a lower mean number of detected genotypes. After adjustment for age and cytological diagnosis, the 2022–2025 period was associated with lower odds of HPV16 (OR 0.59) and HPV18 (OR 0.36) detection. Compared with 2022–2025 HSIL, SCC showed higher HPV16/18 frequency (75.0% vs. 50.8%; p = 0.003), fewer multiple infections, and lower representation of other HR-HPV genotypes. Exploratory birth-cohort analysis showed lower HPV16/18 frequency in women born in or after 1994, with higher individual frequencies of HPV52 and HPV58. HR-HPV genotype patterns in histologically confirmed HSIL varied over time and between birth cohorts, while invasive SCC showed greater HPV16/18 representation and lower HPV diversity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate temporal changes in high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) genotype patterns in histologically confirmed cervical high-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions (HSIL) and differences between these lesions and invasive squamous cell carcinoma (SCC) in an opportunistic screening setting in Spain.
Design and population
This retrospective study included 371 patients with HSIL from 2011 to 2016, 411 with HSIL from 2022 to 2025, and 65 with SCC from 2022 to 2025.
Core methods
The study analyzed HR-HPV genotype patterns in cervical HSIL and SCC cases, adjusting for age and cytological diagnosis to assess temporal and cohort-related changes.
Main results
Among HR-HPV-positive HSIL, HPV16/18 frequency decreased from 65.7% to 50.8% (p = 0.003), accompanied by a lower mean number of detected genotypes. After adjustment for age and cytological diagnosis, the 2022–2025 period was associated with lower odds of HPV16 (OR 0.59) and HPV18 (OR 0.36) detection. SCC showed higher HPV16/18 frequency (75.0% vs. 50.8%; p = 0.003), fewer multiple infections, and lower representation of other HR-HPV genotypes.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that HR-HPV genotype patterns in cervical HSIL have shifted over time and between birth cohorts, with a notable decrease in HPV16/18 prevalence and an increase in other genotypes, while SCC retained higher HPV16/18 representation and lower HPV diversity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform cervical cancer surveillance and screening strategies by highlighting changing HR-HPV genotype distributions and their implications for disease progression and prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective and based on opportunistic screening, which may introduce bias and limit generalizability. The evidence is moderate due to these limitations.

高危型HPV NILM 细胞学检测的阳性结果:病例报告 中 HPV 基因分型的附加价值

High-Risk HPV Positivity With NILM Cytology: The Added Value of HPV Genotyping in a Case Report
作者： Mahmoud A. Aloriby, Retaj Alomami, Yousef Hasen, Ibtesam Elkharobi, Marah ALshikhy, Ahmed Elfargane, Ahmed Mikael, Ahmed Ahmeida, Amawado Asmaeil, Lubna Almaghur
摘要中文翻译背景:高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 阳性且上皮内病变或恶性肿瘤 (NILM) 细胞学阴性代表不一致的 宫颈癌 筛查结果,其中尽管没有上皮内病变或恶性肿瘤的细胞学证据,但仍检测到 HPV DNA。已经对 HPV 基因分型和分子分类方法进行了评估,以进一步描述 HPV 阳性女性的风险特征。病例介绍:在 宫颈癌 筛查期间获得的宫颈细胞学标本使用 ThinPrep® 系统和巴氏染色的液基细胞学进行处理。显微镜检查显示标本令人满意,具有足够的鳞状上皮细胞代表性,并且没有上皮内病变或恶性肿瘤的细胞学证据。高危型HPV 测试是在 GeneXpert® Dx 系统上使用聚合酶链反应和 Xpert® HPV HR 测定进行的。HPV16 和 HPV18/45 以及 HPV31/35/33/52/58 和 HPV51/59 基因型组均为阴性。在 HPV39/68/56/66 基因型组中检测到 HPV DNA。样品充足性和探针检查控制已通过,测试成功完成,没有出现错误。综合解读:该病例表现出 HR-HPV 阳性/NILM 细胞学模式。阳性分子结果表明检测到 高危型HPV DNA,但并不能确定宫颈上皮内瘤变或癌前病变的存在。由于 Xpert® HPV HR 检测将阳性结果报告为基因型组,因此无法仅通过该检测确定导致阳性结果的特定基因型。结论:本病例说明了 HR-HPV 阳性/NILM 筛查结果中 HPV 基因型组分析提供的附加信息。研究结果支持根据适用的 宫颈癌 筛查指南而不是癌前疾病的 诊断 进行基于风险的临床随访。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 阳性且上皮内病变或恶性肿瘤 (NILM) 细胞学阴性代表不一致的 宫颈癌 筛查结果,其中尽管没有上皮内病变或恶性肿瘤的细胞学证据,但仍检测到 HPV DNA。已经对 HPV 基因分型和分子分类方法进行了评估,以进一步描述 HPV 阳性女性的风险特征。
设计与对象
在 宫颈癌 筛查过程中获得的宫颈细胞学标本采用液基细胞学方法、ThinPrep® 系统和巴氏染色进行处理。该病例显示 HR-HPV 阳性/NILM 细胞学模式。
核心方法
高危型HPV 测试是在 GeneXpert® Dx 系统上使用聚合酶链反应和 Xpert® HPV HR 测定进行的。由于 Xpert® HPV HR 检测将阳性结果报告为基因型组,因此无法仅通过该检测确定导致阳性结果的特定基因型。
主要结果
显微镜检查显示标本令人满意,具有足够的鳞状上皮细胞代表性,并且没有上皮内病变或恶性肿瘤的细胞学证据。在 HPV39/68/56/66 基因型组中检测到 HPV DNA。
解释与创新
阳性分子结果表明检测到 高危型HPV DNA,但并不能确定宫颈上皮内瘤变或癌前病变的存在。该病例说明了 HR-HPV 阳性/NILM 筛查结果中 HPV 基因型组分析提供的附加信息。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持根据适用的 宫颈癌 筛查指南而不是癌前疾病的 诊断 进行基于风险的临床随访。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。该研究为 病例报告,不提供统计或比较数据。

High-Risk HPV Positivity With NILM Cytology: The Added Value of HPV Genotyping in a Case Report

Authors: Mahmoud A. Aloriby, Retaj Alomami, Yousef Hasen, Ibtesam Elkharobi, Marah ALshikhy, Ahmed Elfargane, Ahmed Mikael, Ahmed Ahmeida, Amawado Asmaeil, Lubna Almaghur
Original abstract (English)
Background: High-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) positivity with negative for intraepithelial lesion or malignancy (NILM) cytology represents a discordant cervical cancer screening result in which HPV DNA is detected despite the absence of cytological evidence of an intraepithelial lesion or malignancy. HPV genotyping and molecular triage approaches have been evaluated to further characterise risk among HPV-positive women. Case Presentation: A cervical cytology specimen obtained during cervical cancer screening was processed using liquid-based cytology with the ThinPrep® system and Papanicolaou staining. Microscopic examination demonstrated a satisfactory specimen with adequate representation of squamous epithelial cells and no cytological evidence of intraepithelial lesion or malignancy. High-risk HPV testing was performed using polymerase chain reaction with the Xpert® HPV HR assay on the GeneXpert® Dx System. HPV16 and HPV18/45 were negative, as were the HPV31/35/33/52/58 and HPV51/59 genotype groups. HPV DNA was detected within the HPV39/68/56/66 genotype group. Sample adequacy and probe check controls passed, and the test was completed successfully without an error. Integrated Interpretation: The case demonstrates an HR-HPV-positive/NILM cytological pattern. The positive molecular result indicates detection of high-risk HPV DNA but does not establish the presence of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia or a precancerous lesion. Because the Xpert® HPV HR assay reports the positive result as a genotype group, the specific genotype responsible for the positive result cannot be determined from this test alone. Conclusion: This case illustrates the added information provided by HPV genotype-group analysis in an HR-HPV-positive/NILM screening result. The findings support risk-based clinical follow-up according to applicable cervical cancer screening guidelines rather than a diagnosis of precancerous disease.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
High-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) positivity with negative for intraepithelial lesion or malignancy (NILM) cytology represents a discordant cervical cancer screening result in which HPV DNA is detected despite the absence of cytological evidence of an intraepithelial lesion or malignancy. HPV genotyping and molecular triage approaches have been evaluated to further characterise risk among HPV-positive women.
Design and population
A cervical cytology specimen obtained during cervical cancer screening was processed using liquid-based cytology with the ThinPrep® system and Papanicolaou staining. The case demonstrates an HR-HPV-positive/NILM cytological pattern.
Core methods
High-risk HPV testing was performed using polymerase chain reaction with the Xpert® HPV HR assay on the GeneXpert® Dx System. Because the Xpert® HPV HR assay reports the positive result as a genotype group, the specific genotype responsible for the positive result cannot be determined from this test alone.
Main results
Microscopic examination demonstrated a satisfactory specimen with adequate representation of squamous epithelial cells and no cytological evidence of intraepithelial lesion or malignancy. HPV DNA was detected within the HPV39/68/56/66 genotype group.
Interpretation and novelty
The positive molecular result indicates detection of high-risk HPV DNA but does not establish the presence of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia or a precancerous lesion. This case illustrates the added information provided by HPV genotype-group analysis in an HR-HPV-positive/NILM screening result.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support risk-based clinical follow-up according to applicable cervical cancer screening guidelines rather than a diagnosis of precancerous disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength. The study is a case report and does not provide statistical or comparative data.

人乳头瘤病毒 相关 HPV 阳性口咽癌颈部淋巴结切除活检

Excisional biopsy of neck lymph node in HPV positive oropharyngeal cancer associated with human papillomavirus
作者： A. V. Karpenko, S. M. Alekseyev, R. R. Sibgatullin, A. A. Boyko, O. M. Nikolayeva, A. S. Kucherenko
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在分析颈部淋巴结初次切除活检后 HPV 阳性口咽癌的事后诊断率和预后影响,解决这种情况下的漏诊问题。
设计与对象
该研究纳入了 53 名 HPV 阳性 T1-2 口咽癌且临床上有明显颈部转移的患者,根据诊断方法分为两组。
核心方法
该研究使用扁桃体病变、T2 肿瘤和经口入路作为关键参数,分析了局部、区域复发率和 Kaplan-Meyer 总生存率。
主要结果
局部、区域复发率、局部控制率和Kaplan-Meyer总生存率分别为3.8%、5.7%、92.4%和87%,两组之间无统计学意义。
解释与创新
研究发现,偏离基本肿瘤学治疗原则不会损害结果,这表明诊断方法不会显着影响结果。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,诊断方法不会影响 治疗 结果,这可以为临床实践提供信息并提高门诊医生和普通外科医生的认识。
局限与证据强度
该研究的设计限制,例如样本量小和缺乏随机性,可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。

Excisional biopsy of neck lymph node in HPV positive oropharyngeal cancer associated with human papillomavirus

Authors: A. V. Karpenko, S. M. Alekseyev, R. R. Sibgatullin, A. A. Boyko, O. M. Nikolayeva, A. S. Kucherenko
Original abstract (English)
The course of HPV positive oropharyngeal cancer is often characterized by minimal symptoms caused by small primary tumor coupled with clinically apparent neck metastases. In those cases, the diagnosis of oropharyngeal cancer is frequently made post factum after initial excisional biopsy of neck lymph node. The aim of the study was to analyze the rate and the prognostic influence of that diagnostics variant. METHODS AND MATERIALS. Patients with HPV positive T1-2 oropharyngeal cancer with clinically apparent neck metastases were included into the study. The diagnosis was made in a standard manner before treatment in patients of Group 1. Patients of Group 2 previously underwent excisional biopsy of neck lymph node and only then referred to specialized clinic. Oncologic treatment results were assessed with the rate of local, regional recurrence and Kaplan – Meyer overall survival. After applying the inclusion criteria, 53 patients aged between 34 and 74 years were identified with 34 and 19 of them in Group 1 and 2. In both Groups, tonsil lesions (68 % and 89 %) and T2 tumors (76 % and 58 %) were more prevalent. Neck lump was the only symptom in 50 % and 68 % of cases. Transoral approach was used most often (62 % and 63 %). Neck dissection was performed in all patients. Adjuvant radiation was employed in 82 % and 63 % of cases. The rate of local, regional recurrence, locoregional control and Kaplan–Meyer overall survival for all subjects were 3.8 %, 5.7 %, 92.4 % and 87 % without statistical significance between the Group 1 and 2. Hypodiagnostics of oropharyngeal cancer is a widespread phenomenon and, to a great extent, can be explained by subjective reasons. Increasing awareness of the disease course by ambulatory physicians and general surgeons may raise the chance of establishing the prompt correct diagnosis. Deviation of basic oncologic principles of therapy is not accompanied by impairment of results.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to analyze the rate and prognostic influence of diagnosing HPV positive oropharyngeal cancer post factum after initial excisional biopsy of neck lymph node, addressing the issue of hypodiagnostics in this context.
Design and population
The study included 53 patients with HPV positive T1-2 oropharyngeal cancer and clinically apparent neck metastases, divided into two groups based on the diagnostic approach.
Core methods
The study analyzed the rate of local, regional recurrence and Kaplan–Meyer overall survival, using tonsil lesions, T2 tumors, and transoral approach as key parameters.
Main results
The rate of local, regional recurrence, locoregional control, and Kaplan–Meyer overall survival were 3.8%, 5.7%, 92.4%, and 87% respectively, without statistical significance between the two groups.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that deviation from basic oncologic principles of therapy did not impair results, suggesting that the diagnostic approach did not significantly affect outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the diagnostic approach does not impact treatment outcomes, which could inform clinical practice and raise awareness among ambulatory physicians and general surgeons.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design limitations, such as the small sample size and lack of randomization, may affect the generalizability of the findings.

在天然病毒颗粒中发现的新形式的HPV18 L1有助于病毒颗粒的稳定性和感染性

New form of HPV18 L1 found in native virions contributes to virion stability and infectivity
作者： Eric J. Ryndock, Jennifer Biryukov, Kumari Asha, Sarah Brendle, Neil D. Christensen, Craig Meyers
摘要中文翻译摘要 当前的教条指出,乳头瘤病毒病毒体仅由 L1 主要衣壳蛋白的一种形式组成。人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 已使用重组颗粒进行了研究,从而绕过了在分化上皮模型中培养病毒的需要。表达 媒介 被设计用于利用许多 HPV 类型中发现的共有蛋氨酸产生 L1。然而,HPV18 有两个额外的框内蛋氨酸残基,位于共有蛋氨酸上游 61 和 26 个氨基酸处。这些甲硫氨酸残基被排除在用于创建重组病毒的 L1 表达 媒介 之外。另一方面,由器官筏培养产生的 HPV 允许病毒粒子在其天然启动子和完整 基因组 存在的情况下在分化的上皮细胞中组装。在这里,我们利用这个系统来证明,在更自然的环境中产生的野生型 HPV18,可以从上游甲硫氨酸(共有甲硫氨酸上游的 61 个氨基酸残基)翻译出较大形式的 L1,以及从共有甲硫氨酸中翻译出较小的形式,两种尺寸都在新形成的病毒体中组装。61位上游甲硫氨酸残基的消融改变了病毒粒子衣壳构象,并且还降低了病毒粒子稳定性和感染性。重要性 本研究调查乳头瘤病毒病毒颗粒是否含有不止一种 L1 主要衣壳蛋白亚型。通过使用模仿自然分化上皮的器官型筏培养物,我们发现野生型 HPV18 表达两种不同的 L1 亚型并将其整合到病毒粒子中,包括从上游甲硫氨酸翻译而来的以前未被识别的较长形式。我们的研究进一步表明,上游蛋氨酸的破坏会损害病毒粒子的稳定性和感染性,证明较长的 L1 亚型有助于适当的衣壳结构和病毒功能。这些发现提供了对 HPV 衣壳生物学的新认识,并对 HPV 疫苗、诊断和 抗病毒药物 疗法的未来发展具有重要意义。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查乳头瘤病毒病毒粒子是否含有不止一种 L1 主要衣壳蛋白亚型,挑战了当前病毒粒子仅由一种形式的 L1 组成的教条。
设计与对象
该研究利用器官型筏培养物来模拟自然分化的上皮,其中使用其天然启动子和完整的 基因组 产生野生型 HPV18。
核心方法
研究人员采用器官型筏培养物产生 HPV18 病毒颗粒,并分析了两种不同 L1 亚型的表达和掺入,包括从上游甲硫氨酸翻译而来的较长形式。
主要结果
61位上游甲硫氨酸残基的消融改变了病毒体衣壳构象,降低了病毒体稳定性和感染性,并揭示了野生型HPV18表达两种不同的L1亚型并将其整合到病毒体中。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,较长的 L1 亚型有助于正确的衣壳结构和病毒功能,为 HPV 衣壳生物学提供了新的认识。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现对 HPV 疫苗、诊断和 抗病毒药物 疗法的未来发展具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,结果来自单一 HPV 类型,这可能会限制对其他 HPV 类型的普遍适用性。

New form of HPV18 L1 found in native virions contributes to virion stability and infectivity

Authors: Eric J. Ryndock, Jennifer Biryukov, Kumari Asha, Sarah Brendle, Neil D. Christensen, Craig Meyers
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Current dogma states that papillomavirus virions consist of only one form of the L1 major capsid protein. Human papillomaviruses (HPVs) have been studied using recombinant particles, thereby bypassing the need to grow the virus in a model of differentiating epithelium. Expression vectors engineered to produce L1 utilize a consensus methionine found in many HPV types. However, HPV18 has two additional in-frame methionine residues located 61 and 26 amino acids upstream of the consensus methionine. These methionine residues are excluded from the L1 expression vector used to create the recombinant virus. On the other hand, HPV produced from organotypic raft culture allows virions to be assembled in a differentiating epithelium in the presence of its native promoters and complete genome. Here, we have utilized this system to show that the wild-type HPV18, produced in a more natural setting, translates a larger form of L1 from the upstream methionine, which is 61 amino acid residues upstream from the consensus methionine, and a smaller form from the consensus methionine, with both sizes assembled in the newly formed virion. Ablation of the upstream methionine residue at position 61 altered the virion capsid conformation, and also decreased virion stability and infectivity. IMPORTANCE The present study investigates whether the papillomavirus virions contain more than one isoform of the L1 major capsid protein. By using organotypic raft cultures that mimic the naturally differentiating epithelium, we revealed that wild-type HPV18 expresses and incorporates two distinct L1 isoforms into the virion, including a previously unrecognized longer form translated from an upstream methionine. Our study has further shown that disruption of the upstream methionine impairs virion stability and infectivity, demonstrating that the longer L1 isoform contributes to proper capsid architecture and viral function. These findings offer a new understanding of HPV capsid biology and have significant implications for the future development of HPV vaccines, diagnostics, and antiviral therapies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether papillomavirus virions contain more than one isoform of the L1 major capsid protein, challenging the current dogma that virions consist of only one form of L1.
Design and population
The study utilized organotypic raft cultures to mimic the naturally differentiating epithelium, where wild-type HPV18 was produced using its native promoters and complete genome.
Core methods
The researchers employed organotypic raft cultures to produce HPV18 virions and analyzed the expression and incorporation of two distinct L1 isoforms, including a longer form translated from an upstream methionine.
Main results
Ablation of the upstream methionine residue at position 61 altered the virion capsid conformation, decreased virion stability and infectivity, and revealed that wild-type HPV18 expresses and incorporates two distinct L1 isoforms into the virion.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that the longer L1 isoform contributes to proper capsid architecture and viral function, offering a new understanding of HPV capsid biology.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings have significant implications for the future development of HPV vaccines, diagnostics, and antiviral therapies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, and the results are derived from a single HPV type, which may limit generalizability to other HPV types.

基于荧光的报告系统,用于监测角质形成细胞中 人乳头瘤病毒 E2 依赖性报告激活

A fluorescence-based reporter system to monitor human papillomavirus E2-dependent reporter activation in keratinocytes
作者： Sara Ecke, Burkhard Summer, Markus Reinholz, Lars Einar French, Benjamin Maximilian Clanner-Engelshofen
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 基因调控与上皮生物学密切相关,但用于可视化角质形成细胞中功能性病毒转录活性的简单实验系统仍然有限。本研究提出了一种基于荧光的报告系统,旨在监测 HaCaT 角质形成细胞中 HPV E2 依赖性报告基因的激活。该系统使用由 E2 响应启动子盒控制的增强型绿色荧光蛋白 (GFP) 报告基因,能够对报告基因激活进行直接显微可视化和基于图像的半定量分析。与仅报告基因对照相比,HPV16 早期区域质粒的共转染增加了标准化 GFP 信号,支持使用该平台作为可重复的原理验证测定。该系统并非旨在用作诊断测试或感染性测定,而是为体外研究 HPV 相关转录调控提供技术简单的实验工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决缺乏简单的实验系统来可视化角质形成细胞中功能性病毒转录活性的问题,这对于理解与上皮生物学相关的 HPV 基因调控至关重要。
设计与对象
本研究提出了一种基于荧光的报告系统,旨在监测 HaCaT 角质形成细胞中 HPV E2 依赖性报告基因的激活,使用由 E2 响应启动子盒控制的增强型绿色荧光蛋白 (GFP) 报告基因。
核心方法
该系统通过与 HPV16 早期区域质粒共转染,能够对报告基因激活进行直接显微可视化和基于图像的半定量分析,与仅报告基因对照相比,这增加了标准化 GFP 信号。
主要结果
与仅报告基因对照相比,HPV16 早期区域质粒的共转染增加了标准化 GFP 信号,支持使用该平台作为可重复的原理验证测定。
解释与创新
该系统为体外研究HPV相关转录调控提供了一种技术简单的实验工具,为监测E2依赖性激活提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为体外研究 HPV 相关转录调控提供了一种新的实验工具,可以为未来病毒基因调控及其对 HPV 相关疾病的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该系统并非旨在用作诊断测试或感染性测定,并且证据仅限于体外条件,这可能会影响其在体内环境中的普遍性。

A fluorescence-based reporter system to monitor human papillomavirus E2-dependent reporter activation in keratinocytes

Authors: Sara Ecke, Burkhard Summer, Markus Reinholz, Lars Einar French, Benjamin Maximilian Clanner-Engelshofen
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) gene regulation is closely linked to epithelial biology, but simple experimental systems for visualizing functional viral transcriptional activity in keratinocytes remain limited. This study presents a fluorescence-based reporter system designed to monitor HPV E2-dependent reporter activation in HaCaT keratinocytes. Using an enhanced green fluorescent protein (GFP) reporter controlled by an E2-responsive promoter cassette, the system enables direct microscopic visualization and semiquantitative image-based analysis of reporter activation. Cotransfection with an HPV16 early-region plasmid increased normalized GFP signal compared with reporter-only controls, supporting the use of this platform as a reproducible proof-of-principle assay. The system is not intended as a diagnostic test or infectivity assay, but provides a technically simple experimental tool for studying HPV-related transcriptional regulation in vitro.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of simple experimental systems for visualizing functional viral transcriptional activity in keratinocytes, which is crucial for understanding HPV gene regulation linked to epithelial biology.
Design and population
This study presents a fluorescence-based reporter system designed to monitor HPV E2-dependent reporter activation in HaCaT keratinocytes, using an enhanced green fluorescent protein (GFP) reporter controlled by an E2-responsive promoter cassette.
Core methods
The system enables direct microscopic visualization and semiquantitative image-based analysis of reporter activation through cotransfection with an HPV16 early-region plasmid, which increased normalized GFP signal compared with reporter-only controls.
Main results
Cotransfection with an HPV16 early-region plasmid increased normalized GFP signal compared with reporter-only controls, supporting the use of this platform as a reproducible proof-of-principle assay.
Interpretation and novelty
The system provides a technically simple experimental tool for studying HPV-related transcriptional regulation in vitro, offering a new method to monitor E2-dependent activation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a new experimental tool for studying HPV-related transcriptional regulation in vitro, which can inform future research on viral gene regulation and its implications for HPV-associated diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The system is not intended as a diagnostic test or infectivity assay, and the evidence is limited to in vitro conditions, which may affect its generalizability to in vivo settings.

使用扩展 人乳头瘤病毒 基因分型的当代高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 基因型分布和相关细胞学结果

Contemporary High-risk Human Papillomavirus Genotype Distribution and Associated Cytologic Findings Using Extended Human Papillomavirus Genotyping
作者： Kendall R. Wilson, Daisy Maharjan, Ben L. Shuler, Melissa L. Randolph, Joshua M. Howell, Barbara M. Frain, Sheila E. Segura
摘要中文翻译目标:2024 年 1 月,我们的实验室实施了 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 检测,可检测 14 种 高危型HPV (hrHPV) 基因型——6 种单独检测,8 种分为 3 个混合组。我们评估了一年多的基因型特异性感染和细胞学结果,以评估 HPV 相关病理学的趋势。方法:在机构审查委员会豁免后,对 2024 年 1 月至 12 月的 hrHPV 检测和相应的 hrHPV 检测阳性的巴氏细胞学诊断进行回顾性审查。结果:在 43,017 项 HPV 检测中,3,771 项 (8.8%) 为 hrHPV 阳性。在 3,015 例具有相应细胞学诊断的 hrHPV 阳性病例中,分别有 2,374 例(78.7%)和 641 例(21.3%)表现出单一 hrHPV 检测和多基因型感染。在多基因型组中,129 例病例中检测到 HPV16(其中 19 例患有 HSIL),68 例病例中检测到 HPV18(8 例患有 HSIL)。在 328 例病例中分别检测到了混合基因型 56/59/66 和 35/39/68,总共 6 例患有 HSIL。单基因型感染也表现出类似的趋势。尽管多基因型感染显示高级别病变的频率较高,但这些病例主要与 HPV16 和/或 HPV18 的存在相关。结论:扩展基因分型表明,合并的非 16/18 基因型组包括近一半的 hrHPV 阳性感染,并且主要与上皮内病变或恶性肿瘤阴性、意义未明的非典型鳞状细胞以及细胞学上的低度鳞状上皮内病变相关。尽管 HPV16 和 18 仍然是与高度细胞学异常最密切相关的基因型,但非 16/18 基因型的高 监测 凸显了基因型 监测 在疫苗接种后时代的重要性。这些研究结果支持了扩展基因分型对于不断演变的 HPV 流行病学 的风险分层和人群水平监测的价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估特定基因型的感染及细胞学结果在1年内的变化,以评估与HPV相关的病理趋势,应对疫苗接种时代更新监测的需求。
设计与对象
对 2024 年 1 月至 12 月的 hrHPV 检测和相应的巴氏细胞学诊断进行了回顾性审查,重点关注大量 43,017 例 HPV 检测中的 hrHPV 阳性病例。
核心方法
该研究利用 HPV 检测方法检测 14 种 高危型HPV 基因型,包括 6 种单独基因型和 3 个混合组中的 8 种基因型,并分析了每个 hrHPV 阳性病例的相应细胞学诊断。
主要结果
在 43,017 项 HPV 检测中,3,771 项 (8.8%) 为 hrHPV 阳性。其中,2,374 例(78.7%)为单基因型感染,641 例(21.3%)为多基因型感染。
解释与创新
扩展基因分型显示,合并的非 16/18 基因型组包含近一半的 hrHPV 阳性感染,并且主要与低度病变或良性发现相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了 监测 基因型在疫苗接种后时代的重要性,因为非 16/18 基因型很普遍,可能会影响风险分层和人群水平监测。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于一年的数据,并依赖于回顾性分析,这可能会引入偏差。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Contemporary High-risk Human Papillomavirus Genotype Distribution and Associated Cytologic Findings Using Extended Human Papillomavirus Genotyping

Authors: Kendall R. Wilson, Daisy Maharjan, Ben L. Shuler, Melissa L. Randolph, Joshua M. Howell, Barbara M. Frain, Sheila E. Segura
Original abstract (English)
Objectives: In January 2024, our laboratory implemented a human papillomavirus (HPV) assay that detects 14 high-risk HPV (hrHPV) genotypes—6 individually and 8 in 3 pooled groups. We evaluated genotype-specific infections and cytologic outcomes over 1 year to assess trends in HPV-related pathology. Methods: After institutional review board exemption, a retrospective review of hrHPV tests and corresponding Papanicolaou cytology diagnoses of positive hrHPV tests from January to December 2024 was performed. Results: Of 43,017 HPV tests, 3,771 (8.8%) were hrHPV-positive. Among 3,015 hrHPV-positive cases with corresponding cytologic diagnoses, 2,374 (78.7%) and 641 (21.3%) exhibited single hrHPV detections and multiple-genotype infections, respectively. Within the multiple-genotype group, HPV16 was detected in 129 cases—19 with HSIL—and HPV18 was present in 68 cases—8 with HSIL. Pooled genotypes 56/59/66 and 35/39/68 were each detected in 328 cases—6 total with HSIL. Single-genotype infections exhibited similar trends. Although multiple-genotype infections showed a higher frequency of high-grade lesions, these cases were predominantly associated with the presence of HPV16 and/or HPV18. Conclusions: Extended genotyping demonstrated that pooled non-16/18 genotype groups comprised nearly half of hrHPV-positive infections and were predominantly associated with negative for intraepithelial lesion or malignancy, atypical squamous cells of undetermined significance, and low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions on cytology. Although HPV16 and 18 remained the genotypes most strongly associated with high-grade cytologic abnormalities, the high prevalence of non-16/18 genotypes highlights the importance of genotype surveillance in the postvaccination era. These findings support the value of extended genotyping for risk stratification and population-level monitoring of evolving HPV epidemiology.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate genotype-specific infections and cytologic outcomes over 1 year to assess trends in HPV-related pathology, addressing the need for updated surveillance in the postvaccination era.
Design and population
A retrospective review of hrHPV tests and corresponding Papanicolaou cytology diagnoses from January to December 2024 was conducted, focusing on hrHPV-positive cases across a large population of 43,017 HPV tests.
Core methods
The study utilized an HPV assay that detects 14 high-risk HPV genotypes, including 6 individually and 8 in 3 pooled groups, and analyzed corresponding cytologic diagnoses for each hrHPV-positive case.
Main results
Of 43,017 HPV tests, 3,771 (8.8%) were hrHPV-positive. Among these, 2,374 (78.7%) had single-genotype infections, while 641 (21.3%) had multiple-genotype infections.
Interpretation and novelty
Extended genotyping revealed that pooled non-16/18 genotype groups comprised nearly half of hrHPV-positive infections and were predominantly associated with low-grade lesions or benign findings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings emphasize the importance of genotype surveillance in the postvaccination era, as non-16/18 genotypes are prevalent and may influence risk stratification and population-level monitoring.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single year of data and relies on retrospective analysis, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these constraints.

宫颈低度鳞状上皮内病变女性持续 高危型HPV 感染和阴道感染复发负担的危险因素:一项回顾性 队列研究 研究

Risk factors for persistent high-risk HPV infection and recurrence burden of vaginal infections in women with cervical low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions: a retrospective cohort study
作者： Ru Bai, Zishan Tang, Cunwei Ji, Lingling Tang, Yan Liu, Mingyong Luo
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定持续高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 感染的独立危险因素,并评估患有宫颈低度鳞状上皮内病变 (LSIL) 的女性阴道感染的复发负担。持续的 HR-HPV 感染会导致宫颈癌发生,但决定该人群持续感染的因素仍未完全确定。
设计与对象
队列研究 回顾性研究纳入了 3,563 名患有宫颈 LSIL 的女性,她们于 2021 年 1 月至 2025 年 8 月在广东妇女儿童医院接受了同步阴道微生态检测、HPV 基因分型和宫颈细胞学检查。患者被分为持续性感染组 (n=919) 或短暂性感染组 (n=2,644)。
核心方法
多变量逻辑回归确定了与持续性相关的因素,而负二项回归评估了阴道感染的复发负担,表示为每 100 人年的 发病率 密度。
主要结果
Age (per 5 years: OR = 1.13, 95% CI: 1.09–1.17, P<0.001), HPV-52/58 (OR = 1.65, 95% CI: 1.38–1.98, P<0.001), and multiple HR-HPV infections (OR = 1.50, 95% CI: 1.24–1.81, P<0.001) independently predicted persistence. Bacterial vaginosis (OR = 0.69, 95% CI: 0.55–0.88, P = 0.002) and intermediate bacterial vaginosis (OR = 0.66, 95% CI: 0.52–0.84, P<0.001) showed inverse associations. Vulvovaginal candidiasis (OR = 0.75, 95% CI: 0.58–0.96, P = 0.024) also showed an inverse association. Among persistently infected women, aerobic vaginitis had the highest recurrence burden (35.77 per 100 person-years, 95% CI: 33.17–38.53).
解释与创新
HPV-52/58、多重 HR-HPV 感染和年龄增长显着增加 LSIL 患者的持续风险。细菌性阴道病和外阴阴道念珠菌病与持续性的负相关可能是非因果关系,应谨慎解释。需氧性阴道炎的高复发负担强调需要综合管理来解决病毒持续存在和阴道菌群失调问题。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持基于基因型和微生物群的风险分层,并强调了在患有宫颈 LSIL 的女性中管理病毒持久性和阴道生态失调的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计而受到限制,可能会引入偏差。证据强度中等。

Risk factors for persistent high-risk HPV infection and recurrence burden of vaginal infections in women with cervical low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions: a retrospective cohort study

Authors: Ru Bai, Zishan Tang, Cunwei Ji, Lingling Tang, Yan Liu, Mingyong Luo
Original abstract (English)
Background Persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection drives cervical carcinogenesis, yet factors determining persistence in women with low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions (LSIL) remain incompletely characterized. The longitudinal recurrence burden of vaginal infections including aerobic vaginitis, bacterial vaginosis, and vulvovaginal candidiasis in this population has not been systematically quantified. Objective To identify independent risk factors for persistent HR-HPV infection and evaluate the recurrence burden of aerobic vaginitis, bacterial vaginosis, and vulvovaginal candidiasis in women with cervical LSIL. Study design This retrospective cohort study included 3,563 women with cervical LSIL who underwent concurrent vaginal microecology testing, HPV genotyping, and cervical cytology at Guangdong Women and Children Hospital (January 2021–August 2025). Patients were classified as persistent (n=919; ≥365 days with the same HR-HPV subtype) or transient (n=2,644; >30 days and <365 days). Multivariate logistic regression identified factors associated with persistence. Negative binomial regression assessed recurrence burden, expressed as incidence density per 100 person-years. Results After adjustment, age (per 5 years: OR = 1.13, 95% CI: 1.09–1.17, P<0.001), HPV-52/58 (OR = 1.65, 95% CI: 1.38–1.98, P<0.001), and multiple HR-HPV infections (OR = 1.50, 95% CI: 1.24–1.81, P<0.001) independently predicted persistence. Bacterial vaginosis (OR = 0.69, 95% CI: 0.55–0.88, P = 0.002) and intermediate bacterial vaginosis (OR = 0.66, 95% CI: 0.52–0.84, P<0.001), and vulvovaginal candidiasis (OR = 0.75, 95% CI: 0.58–0.96, P = 0.024) showed inverse associations. Among persistently infected women, aerobic vaginitis had the highest recurrence burden (35.77 per 100 person-years, 95% CI: 33.17–38.53), exceeding bacterial vaginosis and vulvovaginal candidiasis. Persistent infection was associated with TCT abnormalities ≥ASC-US (OR = 1.63, 95% CI: 1.40–1.91, P<0.001) and LSIL+ lesions (OR = 1.48, 95% CI: 1.25–1.75, P<0.001). Conclusion HPV-52/58, multiple HR-HPV infections, and advancing age significantly increase persistence risk in LSIL patients. The inverse associations of bacterial vaginosis and vulvovaginal candidiasis with persistence are likely non-causal and should be interpreted with caution. The high aerobic vaginitis recurrence burden underscores the need for integrated management addressing both viral persistence and vaginal dysbiosis. These findings support genotype- and microbiota-informed risk stratification.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify independent risk factors for persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection and evaluate the recurrence burden of vaginal infections in women with cervical low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions (LSIL). Persistent HR-HPV infection drives cervical carcinogenesis, yet factors determining persistence in this population remain incompletely characterized.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study included 3,563 women with cervical LSIL who underwent concurrent vaginal microecology testing, HPV genotyping, and cervical cytology at Guangdong Women and Children Hospital from January 2021 to August 2025. Patients were classified into persistent (n=919) or transient (n=2,644) infection groups.
Core methods
Multivariate logistic regression identified factors associated with persistence, while negative binomial regression assessed the recurrence burden of vaginal infections, expressed as incidence density per 100 person-years.
Main results
Age (per 5 years: OR = 1.13, 95% CI: 1.09–1.17, P<0.001), HPV-52/58 (OR = 1.65, 95% CI: 1.38–1.98, P<0.001), and multiple HR-HPV infections (OR = 1.50, 95% CI: 1.24–1.81, P<0.001) independently predicted persistence. Bacterial vaginosis (OR = 0.69, 95% CI: 0.55–0.88, P = 0.002) and intermediate bacterial vaginosis (OR = 0.66, 95% CI: 0.52–0.84, P<0.001) showed inverse associations. Vulvovaginal candidiasis (OR = 0.75, 95% CI: 0.58–0.96, P = 0.024) also showed an inverse association. Among persistently infected women, aerobic vaginitis had the highest recurrence burden (35.77 per 100 person-years, 95% CI: 33.17–38.53).
Interpretation and novelty
HPV-52/58, multiple HR-HPV infections, and advancing age significantly increase persistence risk in LSIL patients. The inverse associations of bacterial vaginosis and vulvovaginal candidiasis with persistence are likely non-causal and should be interpreted with caution. The high aerobic vaginitis recurrence burden underscores the need for integrated management addressing both viral persistence and vaginal dysbiosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support genotype- and microbiota-informed risk stratification and highlight the importance of managing both viral persistence and vaginal dysbiosis in women with cervical LSIL.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design, which may introduce bias. The evidence strength is moderate.

人乳头瘤病毒 环境采样和分子检测标准化程序的开发和实验室评估

Development and Laboratory Evaluation of a Standardized Procedure for Environmental Sampling and Molecular Detection of Human Papillomavirus
作者： Xiuzhi Duan, Meng Yang, Yiyi Xie, Xuchu Wang, Liqing Xu, Zhihua Tao
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 传染性病原体的环境监测对于疾病预防和控制越来越重要。然而,采样、保存和运输程序的异质性会严重影响分子检测结果。方法以人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)为无包膜DNA病毒模型,评价拭子类型、表面材料、采样间隔、拭子预湿、环境温度和保存介质等6个表面采样性能决定因素。通过汇集大约 150 个匿名男性尿道拭子样本,然后进行盐水洗脱、基因型确认和定量检测,制备 HPV 阳性接种物。通过实时定量 PCR (qPCR) 对 HPV DNA 进行定量。结果是阳性检出率和预先指定的对数规模恢复指数。结果聚酯拭子和海绵拭子的阳性检出率最高(均为98.04%±3.40%),而聚酯拭子的平均对数标度回收指数最高。与木材或机织织物相比,从光滑、无孔、非吸收性表面(瓷砖和聚丙烯)更容易回收 HPV DNA。尽管对数标度回收指数没有显着差异,但干聚酯拭子的阳性检出率高于 PBS 预湿拭子的阳性检出率(88.75% ± 2.80% vs 80.00% ± 5.23%;P = 0.032)。接种后 1 至 24 小时内采样并未显着影响任一结果。与 15°C 和 25°C 相比,暴露在 35°C 下会降低阳性检出率和对数标度回收指数。专用病毒运输介质在 2–8°C 储存的 28 天中保持 HPV DNA 的可检测性。PBS 在第 7 天首先降至预设的 70% 检测阈值以下,而生理盐水在 3 小时内保持在或高于阈值,并在 6 小时时降至阈值以下。结论 在测试的实验室条件下,干燥聚酯拭子与专用病毒运输介质相结合,为从环境表面回收 HPV 分子提供了最实用的整体方法。表面特性和环境温度是 HPV DNA 可检测性的主要决定因素。这些发现支持方案标准化,但并未确定病毒传染性或污染物 传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
传染性病原体的环境监测对于疾病预防和控制越来越重要;然而,采样、保存和运输程序的异质性会严重影响分子检测结果。
设计与对象
使用人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)作为无包膜DNA病毒模型来评估表面采样性能的六个决定因素:拭子类型、表面材料、采样间隔、拭子预湿、环境温度和保存介质。
核心方法
通过汇集大约 150 个匿名男性尿道拭子样本,然后进行盐水洗脱、基因型确认和定量检测,制备 HPV 阳性接种物。通过实时定量 PCR (qPCR) 对 HPV DNA 进行定量。
主要结果
聚酯拭子和海绵拭子的阳性检出率最高(均为 98.04% ± 3.40%),而聚酯拭子的平均对数标度回收指数最高。与木材或机织织物相比,从光滑、无孔、非吸收性表面(瓷砖和聚丙烯)更容易回收 HPV DNA。与 15°C 和 25°C 相比,暴露在 35°C 下会降低阳性检出率和对数标度回收指数。
解释与创新
在经过测试的实验室条件下,干燥聚酯拭子与专用病毒运输介质相结合,为从环境表面回收 HPV 分子提供了最实用的整体方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持方案标准化,但并未确定病毒传染性或污染物 传播。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究结果对现实世界环境的普遍性或混杂因素的存在。

Development and Laboratory Evaluation of a Standardized Procedure for Environmental Sampling and Molecular Detection of Human Papillomavirus

Authors: Xiuzhi Duan, Meng Yang, Yiyi Xie, Xuchu Wang, Liqing Xu, Zhihua Tao
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Environmental surveillance of infectious pathogens is increasingly important for disease prevention and control; however, heterogeneity in sampling, preservation, and transport procedures can substantially affect molecular detection results. Methods Human papillomavirus (HPV) was used as a non-enveloped DNA virus model to evaluate six determinants of surface sampling performance: swab type, surface material, sampling interval, swab pre-moistening, ambient temperature, and preservation medium. An HPV-positive inoculum was prepared by pooling approximately 150 anonymized male urethral swab specimens, followed by saline elution, genotype confirmation, and quantitative testing. HPV DNA was quantified by real-time quantitative PCR (qPCR). Outcomes were the positive detection rate and a prespecified log-scale recovery index. Results Polyester and sponge swabs achieved the highest positive detection rates (both 98.04% ± 3.40%), whereas polyester swabs had the highest mean log-scale recovery index. HPV DNA was more readily recovered from smooth, non-porous, non-absorbent surfaces (ceramic tile and polypropylene) than from wood or woven fabric. Dry polyester swabbing yielded a higher positive detection rate than PBS-pre-moistened swabbing (88.75% ± 2.80% vs 80.00% ± 5.23%; P = 0.032), although the log-scale recovery index did not differ significantly. Sampling from 1 to 24 h after inoculation did not significantly affect either outcome. Exposure to 35°C reduced both the positive detection rate and the log-scale recovery index compared with 15°C and 25°C. Dedicated viral transport media maintained HPV DNA detectability throughout 28 days of storage at 2–8°C. PBS first fell below the prespecified 70% detection threshold on day 7, whereas normal saline remained at or above the threshold through 3 h and fell below it at 6 h. Conclusions Under the tested laboratory conditions, dry polyester swabs combined with dedicated viral transport media provided the most practical overall approach for molecular recovery of HPV from environmental surfaces. Surface properties and ambient temperature were the principal determinants of HPV DNA detectability. These findings support protocol standardization but do not establish viral infectivity or fomite transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Environmental surveillance of infectious pathogens is increasingly important for disease prevention and control; however, heterogeneity in sampling, preservation, and transport procedures can substantially affect molecular detection results.
Design and population
Human papillomavirus (HPV) was used as a non-enveloped DNA virus model to evaluate six determinants of surface sampling performance: swab type, surface material, sampling interval, swab pre-moistening, ambient temperature, and preservation medium.
Core methods
An HPV-positive inoculum was prepared by pooling approximately 150 anonymized male urethral swab specimens, followed by saline elution, genotype confirmation, and quantitative testing. HPV DNA was quantified by real-time quantitative PCR (qPCR).
Main results
Polyester and sponge swabs achieved the highest positive detection rates (both 98.04% ± 3.40%), whereas polyester swabs had the highest mean log-scale recovery index. HPV DNA was more readily recovered from smooth, non-porous, non-absorbent surfaces (ceramic tile and polypropylene) than from wood or woven fabric. Exposure to 35°C reduced both the positive detection rate and the log-scale recovery index compared with 15°C and 25°C.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support protocol standardization but do not establish viral infectivity or fomite transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the generalisability of the findings to real-world settings or the presence of confounding factors.

了解使用自采样技术和护理点诊断在马拉维筛查 人乳头瘤病毒 的 人乳头瘤病毒 测试的实施和扩大

Understanding the implementation and scale up of human papillomavirus testing using self-sampling techniques and point-of-care diagnostics for the screening of cervical cancer in Malawi
作者： Hussein Twabi
摘要中文翻译背景:尽管 发病率 是全球最可预防的恶性肿瘤之一,但马拉维仍然是全球 发病率 和 发病率 发病率最高的五个国家之一。该国的筛查计划主要基于筛查和治疗策略,使用醋酸(VIA)进行目视检查,然后进行冷冻疗法或热消融。尽管在全国范围内进行了推广,但筛查覆盖率仍然很低且不公平。世界卫生组织 (WHO) 现在推荐高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) DNA 检测(与 VIA 相比,该检测具有更高的诊断准确性)作为首选筛查方法。新兴的护理点 (POC) HPV 检测和自我采样方式已显示出改变马拉维等资源匮乏地区筛查可及性的潜力。本论文探讨了将 POC HPV DNA 检测和自我采样纳入马拉维 宫颈癌 筛查计划的可行性。目标:这项工作有五个主要目标:(1) 描述马拉维 宫颈癌 筛查的采用情况;(2) 量化和描述飓风弗雷迪对孕产妇和生殖健康服务利用的短期影响;(3) 在筛查和治疗框架内评估近 POC HR-HPV 检测的诊断性能;(4)探讨女性宫颈自我取样的接受程度;(5) 评估在资源匮乏的环境中将 POC HPV 检测与自我采样相结合的可行性。调查结果:马拉维的 宫颈癌 筛查覆盖率仍然较低且不均衡,尤其有利于艾滋病毒呈阳性的城市妇女。包括飓风弗雷迪在内的气候冲击扰乱了人们获得基本生殖健康服务的机会。在马拉维南部农村接受筛查的女性中,HR-HPV 患病率 较高,VIA 和 HPV DNA 检测之间存在显着的诊断不一致。自我采样非常容易被接受,女性认为隐私、舒适和自主是主要优势,并且 HPV 快速检测被证明是可行的。意义:这些发现凸显了基于 VIA 筛查的局限性,并支持 WHO 关于过渡到初级 HPV 检测的建议。将 POC HPV 检测与自我采样相结合可以提高覆盖率、增强公平性并改善早期检测,特别是在农村和气候脆弱地区。结果直接为 2024 年马拉维国家癌症 治疗 指南提供信息,并为政策、培训和扩大战略提供了可操作的证据。未来的研究应评估纵向结果、成本效益和实施途径,以确保马拉维实现可持续、有弹性和以妇女为中心的 宫颈癌 预防。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 仍然是马拉维的一个重大公共卫生问题,尽管现有筛查计划,但 发病率 和 死亡率 率仍很高。该研究旨在探讨将即时 HPV DNA 检测和自我采样纳入马拉维 宫颈癌 筛查计划的可行性。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注马拉维的 宫颈癌 筛查计划,该计划主要使用醋酸 (VIA) 目视检查,然后进行冷冻疗法或热消融。它研究了飓风“弗雷迪”对孕产妇和生殖健康服务利用的影响,并评估了马拉维南部农村地区近 POC HR-HPV 检测的诊断性能。
核心方法
该研究描述了马拉维 宫颈癌 筛查的采用情况,量化了飓风 Freddy 对卫生服务利用的短期影响,并评估了筛查和治疗框架内近 POC HR-HPV 检测的诊断性能。它还探讨了女性自我采样的可接受性,并评估了在资源匮乏的环境中将 POC HPV 检测与自我采样相结合的可行性。
主要结果
马拉维的 宫颈癌 筛查覆盖率仍然较低且不均衡,尤其有利于艾滋病毒阳性者和城市妇女。包括飓风弗雷迪在内的气候冲击扰乱了人们获得基本生殖健康服务的机会。在马拉维南部农村接受筛查的女性中,HR-HPV 患病率 较高,VIA 和 HPV DNA 检测之间存在显着的诊断不一致。自我采样非常容易被接受,女性认为隐私、舒适和自主是主要优势,并且 HPV 快速检测被证明是可行的。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了基于 VIA 筛查的局限性,并支持 WHO 关于过渡到初级 HPV 检测的建议。该研究表明,将 POC HPV 检测与自我采样相结合可以提高覆盖率、增强公平性并改善早期检测,特别是在农村和气候脆弱地区。
科研与公卫意义
结果直接为 2024 年马拉维国家癌症 治疗 指南提供信息,并为政策、培训和扩大战略提供了可操作的证据。未来的研究应评估纵向结果、成本效益和实施途径,以确保马拉维实现可持续、有弹性和以妇女为中心的 宫颈癌 预防。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于仅抽象的分析,这可能会限制理解的深度。然而,证据足以支持在资源匮乏的环境中以中等置信度将 POC HPV 检测与自我采样相结合的可行性。

Understanding the implementation and scale up of human papillomavirus testing using self-sampling techniques and point-of-care diagnostics for the screening of cervical cancer in Malawi

Authors: Hussein Twabi
Original abstract (English)
Background: Although cervical cancer is one of the most preventable malignancies worldwide, Malawi remains among the top five countries globally with the highest incidence and mortality rates from cervical cancer. The country’s screening programme is largely based on a screen-and-treat strategy using visual inspection with acetic acid (VIA) followed by cryotherapy or thermoablation. Despite nationwide roll-out, screening coverage remains low and inequitable. The World Health Organization (WHO) now recommends high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) DNA testing – which demonstrates superior diagnostic accuracy as opposed to VIA – as the preferred screening method. Emerging point-of-care (POC) HPV tests and self-sampling modalities have shown potential to transform screening accessibility in low-resource settings such as Malawi. This thesis explores the feasibility of integrating POC HPV DNA testing and self-sampling into the Malawian cervical cancer screening programme. Objectives: This work had five main aims: (1) to describe the uptake of cervical cancer screening in Malawi; (2) to quantify and describe the short-term impact of Cyclone Freddy on maternal and reproductive health service utilisation; (3) to evaluate the diagnostic performance of near-POC HR-HPV testing within a screen-and-treat framework; (4) to explore the acceptability of cervical self-sampling among women; and (5) to assess the feasibility of integrating POC HPV testing with self-sampling in a low-resource setting. Findings: Cervical cancer screening coverage in Malawi remains low and uneven, disproportionately favouring HIV-positive and urban women. Climatic shocks, including Cyclone Freddy, disrupted access to essential reproductive health services. Among women screened in rural southern Malawi, HR-HPV prevalence was high and substantial diagnostic discordance existed between VIA and HPV DNA testing. Self-sampling was highly acceptable, with women citing privacy, comfort, and autonomy as key advantages, and HPV rapid testing was demonstrated to be operationally feasible. Implications: These findings highlight the limitations of VIA-based screening and support WHO recommendations for transitioning to primary HPV testing. Integrating POC HPV testing with self-sampling could increase coverage, enhance equity, and improve early detection, particularly in rural and climate-vulnerable settings. The results have directly informed the 2024 Malawi National Cancer Treatment Guidelines and provide actionable evidence for policy, training, and scale-up strategies. Future research should evaluate longitudinal outcomes, cost-effectiveness, and implementation pathways to ensure sustainable, resilient, and woman-centred cervical cancer prevention in Malawi.

Question and background

The study aimed to explore the feasibility of integrating point-of-care HPV DNA testing and self-sampling into the Malawian cervical cancer screening program.
