中国西北地区 HPV 感染的流行病学概况、与宫颈病变和阴道微生态紊乱的关系以及九价 疫苗 覆盖率:一项针对 57,035 名女性的 6 年回顾性研究

Epidemiological profile of HPV infection, associations with cervical lesions and vaginal microecological disorders, and nine-valent vaccine coverage in Northwest China: a 6-year retrospective study of 57,035 women
作者： Xin-Tong Li, Rui-Na Li, Xu-Yan Jia, Lin-Na Wei, Zhao Yang, Ting-Shuo Zhang, Jiao Li, Qian Zhang, Hui-Hui Feng, Fang Tan
摘要中文翻译宫颈癌 是全球女性癌症 死亡率 的主要原因,在中国,这一负担不断增加。持续的高危型 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 感染是宫颈癌发生的既定驱动因素,但中国西北地区的区域流行病学数据以及 HPV 与阴道微生态紊乱之间的关联仍知之甚少。我们对 2016 年至 2021 年陕西省一家三级医院的 57,035 名女性门诊患者进行了回顾性研究。进行 HPV 基因分型以评估持续感染模式、三个宫颈病变风险层的 9 价 疫苗 覆盖率,以及与细菌性阴道病 (BV)、需氧性阴道炎 (AV)、滴虫性阴道炎 (TV) 的相关性。和外阴阴道念珠菌病(VVC)。在这项对 57,035 名女性门诊患者进行 HPV 基因分型的研究中,总体 HPV 阳性率为 28.77%,感染以孤立的 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 和单基因型模式为主。HPV16、52、58、53 和 51 是最常见的 HR-HPV 基因型,HPV 患病率 显示双峰年龄分布,峰值在 26-30 岁,次峰值在 ≥ 61 岁。在3542名随访患者中,总体持续感染率为33.75%,HR/LR-HPV混合感染率最高。HPV 阳性与 TCT 异常率升高相关,九种 HR-HPV 基因型与细胞学异常显着相关;HPV16 占 宫颈癌 病例的 69.7%。9 价 疫苗 基因型的覆盖范围随着疾病严重程度而增加。HPV感染与细菌性阴道病、滴虫病和混合性阴道炎呈正相关,但与外阴阴道念珠菌病呈负相关,且具有亚型特异性关联模式。这项研究提供了全面的区域 HPV 流行病学证据,并揭示了亚型特异性 HPV 与阴道微生态的相互作用,支持中国西北地区量身定制的 宫颈癌 预防策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于持续性 HR-HPV 感染导致 宫颈癌 负担不断增加,该研究旨在了解中国西北地区的区域流行病学数据以及 HPV 与阴道微生态紊乱之间的关联。
设计与对象
对2016年至2021年陕西省某三级医院57035名女性门诊患者进行回顾性研究。
核心方法
进行 HPV 基因分型以评估持续感染模式、三个宫颈病变危险层的 9 价 疫苗 覆盖率,以及与细菌性阴道病、需氧性阴道炎、滴虫性阴道炎和外阴阴道念珠菌病的相关性。
主要结果
HPV总体阳性率为28.77%,其中HPV16、52、58、53和51是最常见的基因型。HPV 阳性与 TCT 异常率升高相关,九种 HR-HPV 基因型与细胞学异常显着相关。9 价 疫苗 基因型的覆盖范围随着疾病严重程度而增加。HPV感染与细菌性阴道病、滴虫病和混合性阴道炎呈正相关,但与外阴阴道念珠菌病呈负相关。
解释与创新
这项研究提供了全面的区域 HPV 流行病学证据,并揭示了亚型特异性 HPV 与阴道微生态的相互作用,支持中国西北地区量身定制的 宫颈癌 预防策略。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以告知监测、诊断和治疗中国西北地区的HPV感染情况和宫颈癌,并强调在HPV相关疾病管理中考虑阴道微生态的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和缺乏纵向随访而受到限制,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。总体持续感染率为33.75%,HR/LR-HPV混合感染率最高。

Epidemiological profile of HPV infection, associations with cervical lesions and vaginal microecological disorders, and nine-valent vaccine coverage in Northwest China: a 6-year retrospective study of 57,035 women

Authors: Xin-Tong Li, Rui-Na Li, Xu-Yan Jia, Lin-Na Wei, Zhao Yang, Ting-Shuo Zhang, Jiao Li, Qian Zhang, Hui-Hui Feng, Fang Tan
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer is a leading cause of female cancer mortality globally, with a rising burden in China. Persistent high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection is the established driver of cervical carcinogenesis, but regional epidemiological data and associations between HPV and vaginal microecological disorders remain poorly characterized in Northwest China. We conducted a retrospective study of 57,035 female outpatients at a tertiary hospital in Shaanxi Province from 2016 to 2021. HPV genotyping was performed to assess persistent infection patterns, 9-valent vaccine coverage across three cervical lesion risk strata, and correlations with bacterial vaginosis (BV), aerobic vaginitis (AV), trichomonal vaginitis (TV), and vulvovaginal candidiasis (VVC). In this study of 57,035 female outpatients undergoing HPV genotyping, the overall HPV positivity rate was 28.77%, with infections dominated by isolated high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) and single-genotype patterns. HPV16, 52, 58, 53 and 51 were the most prevalent HR-HPV genotypes, and HPV prevalence showed a bimodal age distribution peaking at 26–30 years with a secondary peak at ≥ 61 years. Among 3,542 patients with follow-up, the overall persistent infection rate was 33.75%, highest in mixed HR/LR-HPV infections. HPV positivity correlated with elevated abnormal TCT rates, and nine HR-HPV genotypes were significantly associated with cytological abnormalities; HPV16 accounted for 69.7% of cervical cancer cases. The coverage of 9-valent vaccine genotypes increased with disease severity. HPV infection was positively associated with bacterial vaginosis, trichomoniasis and mixed vaginitis, but inversely linked to vulvovaginal candidiasis, with subtype-specific association patterns. This study provides comprehensive regional HPV epidemiological evidence and reveals subtype-specific HPV-vaginal microecology interactions, supporting tailored cervical cancer prevention strategies in Northwest China.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the regional epidemiological data and associations between HPV and vaginal microecological disorders in Northwest China, given the rising burden of cervical cancer due to persistent HR-HPV infection.
Design and population
A retrospective study of 57,035 female outpatients at a tertiary hospital in Shaanxi Province from 2016 to 2021 was conducted.
Core methods
HPV genotyping was performed to assess persistent infection patterns, 9-valent vaccine coverage across three cervical lesion risk strata, and correlations with bacterial vaginosis, aerobic vaginitis, trichomonal vaginitis, and vulvovaginal candidiasis.
Main results
The overall HPV positivity rate was 28.77%, with HPV16, 52, 58, 53, and 51 being the most prevalent genotypes. HPV positivity correlated with elevated abnormal TCT rates, and nine HR-HPV genotypes were significantly associated with cytological abnormalities. The coverage of 9-valent vaccine genotypes increased with disease severity. HPV infection was positively associated with bacterial vaginosis, trichomoniasis, and mixed vaginitis, but inversely linked to vulvovaginal candidiasis.
Interpretation and novelty
This study provides comprehensive regional HPV epidemiological evidence and reveals subtype-specific HPV-vaginal microecology interactions, supporting tailored cervical cancer prevention strategies in Northwest China.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of HPV infection and cervical cancer in Northwest China, and highlight the importance of considering vaginal microecology in HPV-related disease management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the lack of longitudinal follow-up, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The overall persistent infection rate was 33.75%, highest in mixed HR/LR-HPV infections.

HPV16 CircE7 RNA 的调控和功能

Regulation and function of the HPV16 CircE7 RNA
作者： Eunice E. Lee, Yihui Huang, Eleanor Dowell, Kaitlyn Walsh, Calvin Hosler, Austin Witt, Odera Ifeacho, Doreen Palsgrove, Andrew Day, Claire James
摘要中文翻译摘要 高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV),包括 HPV16,产生包含 E7 癌基因 (circE7) 的环状 RNA。CircE7 可在 HPV16 阳性细胞和肿瘤中检测到,优先定位于细胞质,经过 N 6 -甲基腺苷 (m 6 A) 修饰,可翻译为 E7 癌蛋白。在这里,我们探讨了circE7的调控和功能。后剪接点两侧的 m 6 A 基序的 突变 揭示了单个 m 6 A 基序对于 circE7 的形成至关重要。该 m 6 A 基序的 突变 促进了 E6*I 剪接位点 (226^409) 的线性剪接,表明线性和环状 E7 剪接是反向调节的。此外,circE7 中 IRES 样基序的 突变 显着降低了 E7 蛋白表达,但对 circE7 RNA 水平没有显着影响。YTHDC1 的敲低(而非其他 m 6 A 结合蛋白)降低了 circE7 RNA 和蛋白质的表达。BaseScope ISH用于确认头颈鳞状细胞癌细胞系和肿瘤中circE7的表达。使用 qRT-PCR 和 BaseScope ISH,我们发现血清和氨基酸饥饿显着增加了 circE7。最后,我们在 circE7 m 6 A 基序 (Mut2) 中生成了带有 突变 的 HPV16 基因组。用 Mut2 对原代角质形成细胞进行稳定转导,证实了 circE7 的缺失和 E6*I 表达的增加。Mut2 HPV16 基因组 表现出病毒复制显着降低,但转化原代角质形成细胞的能力增强。我们的研究表明,m 6 A 对 circE7 和 E6*I 的精确调控对于 HPV16 感染和转化角质形成细胞的能力至关重要。重要性 高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV),例如 HPV16,必须仔细控制其产生的 E6 和 E7 蛋白数量。这项研究表明,HPV16 会切换病毒 RNA 上的单个化学标签(m6A 标记)来控制早期区域 RNA 的产生,包括称为 circE7 的环状 RNA。同一位点协调调节 E6*I 同工型的剪接。CircE7 使用 m6A 结合蛋白来控制其产生,并使用特定序列来促进其翻译。它存在于 HPV 阳性癌症中,可以对营养缺乏做出反应。通过该 m 6 A 位点对 circE7 的调节也影响病毒复制和转化能力,表明这种调节机制对于 HPV 生物学至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在研究 HPV16 产生的 circE7 RNA 的调控和功能,因为 高危型HPV(包括 HPV16)必须仔细控制其产生 E6 和 E7 蛋白的量。该研究解决了有关 circE7 在 HPV 生物学中的作用的知识空白。
设计与对象
该研究利用体外和体内模型探索了 circE7 RNA 在 HPV16 阳性细胞和肿瘤中的调节和功能。它涉及原代角质形成细胞和头颈鳞状细胞癌细胞系。
核心方法
研究人员使用 qRT-PCR 和 BaseScope ISH 检测 circE7 RNA 及其表达。他们还对 circE7 中的 m6A 基序和 IRES 样基序进行了 突变 分析,并使用修饰的 HPV16 基因组 进行了稳定转导实验。
主要结果
发现背剪接点侧翼的 m6A 基序的 突变 对于 circE7 的形成至关重要。此外,circE7 中 IRES 样基序的 突变 显着降低了 E7 蛋白表达,而不影响 circE7 RNA 水平。YTHDC1 的敲低(而非其他 m6A 结合蛋白)降低了 circE7 RNA 和蛋白质的表达。
解释与创新
作者解释说,m6A 对 circE7 和 E6*I 的调节对于 HPV16 感染和转化角质形成细胞的能力至关重要,强调了一种影响复制和致癌潜力的病毒 RNA 调节新机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对 HPV16 感染和转化的分子机制的见解,这可以为未来有关 HPV 相关癌症的研究和靶向治疗的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于体外和体内实验,可能无法完全代表人类HPV感染的复杂性。证据是中等的,因为结果来自受控实验室设置而不是临床试验。

Regulation and function of the HPV16 CircE7 RNA

Authors: Eunice E. Lee, Yihui Huang, Eleanor Dowell, Kaitlyn Walsh, Calvin Hosler, Austin Witt, Odera Ifeacho, Doreen Palsgrove, Andrew Day, Claire James
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT High-risk human papillomaviruses (HPV), including HPV16, produce circular RNA that encompasses the E7 oncogene (circE7). CircE7 can be detected in HPV16-positive cells and tumors, is preferentially localized to the cytoplasm, is N 6 -methyladenosine (m 6 A)-modified, and can be translated into the E7 oncoprotein. Here, we explored the regulation and function of circE7. Mutation of m 6 A motifs flanking the backsplice junction revealed a single m 6 A motif to be essential for circE7 formation. Mutation of this m 6 A motif promoted linear splicing of the E6*I splice site (226^409), suggesting that linear and circular E7 splicing are inversely regulated. Additionally, mutation of an IRES-like motif in circE7 significantly decreased E7 protein expression, without having significant effects on circE7 RNA levels. Knockdown of YTHDC1, but not other m 6 A-binding proteins, decreased both circE7 RNA and protein expression. BaseScope ISH was used to confirm the expression of circE7 in head and neck squamous cell carcinoma cell lines and tumors. Using both qRT-PCR and BaseScope ISH, we found that serum and amino acid starvation significantly increased circE7. Finally, we generated an HPV16 genome with mutations in the circE7 m 6 A motif (Mut2). Stable transduction of primary keratinocytes with Mut2 confirmed the loss of circE7 and increased expression of E6*I. The Mut2 HPV16 genome exhibited significantly decreased viral replication but an increased ability to transform primary keratinocytes. Our studies reveal that the precise regulation of circE7 and E6*I by m 6 A is critical for the ability of HPV16 to infect and transform keratinocytes. IMPORTANCE High-risk human papillomaviruses (HPVs), such as HPV16, must carefully control how much E6 and E7 proteins they make. This study shows that HPV16 toggles a single chemical tag on the viral RNA (an m6A mark) to control the production of early region RNAs, including a circular RNA called circE7. The same site coordinately regulates splicing of the E6*I isoform. CircE7 uses m6A-binding proteins to control its production and a specific sequence to promote its translation. It is present in HPV-positive cancers and can respond to nutrient starvation. Regulation of circE7 through this m 6 A site also impacted viral replication and transformation capacity, indicating that this regulatory mechanism is critical for HPV biology.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the regulation and function of circE7 RNA produced by HPV16, as high-risk HPVs, including HPV16, must carefully control how much E6 and E7 proteins they make. The research addresses a knowledge gap regarding the role of circE7 in HPV biology.
Design and population
The study explored the regulation and function of circE7 RNA in HPV16-positive cells and tumors, using both in vitro and in vivo models. It involved primary keratinocytes and head and neck squamous cell carcinoma cell lines.
Core methods
The researchers used qRT-PCR and BaseScope ISH to detect circE7 RNA and its expression. They also performed mutation analyses of m6A motifs and an IRES-like motif in circE7, and conducted stable transduction experiments with a modified HPV16 genome.
Main results
Mutation of the m6A motif flanking the backsplice junction was found to be essential for circE7 formation. Additionally, mutation of an IRES-like motif in circE7 significantly decreased E7 protein expression, without affecting circE7 RNA levels. Knockdown of YTHDC1, but not other m6A-binding proteins, decreased both circE7 RNA and protein expression.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the regulation of circE7 and E6*I by m6A is critical for HPV16's ability to infect and transform keratinocytes, highlighting a novel mechanism of viral RNA regulation that impacts both replication and oncogenic potential.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide insights into the molecular mechanisms of HPV16 infection and transformation, which could inform future research on HPV-associated cancers and the development of targeted therapies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on in vitro and in vivo experiments, which may not fully represent the complexity of human HPV infection. The evidence is moderate, as the results are derived from controlled laboratory settings and not from clinical trials.

自我采集阴道样本用于人乳头瘤病毒 DNA检测的可接受性和偏好性与医生采集的巴氏涂片比较

Acceptability and preference of self-collected vaginal samples for human papillomavirus DNA testing compared with physician-collected Pap smear.
作者： Ourlad Alzeus G Tantengco, Mikayla Francesca N de Guzman, Amina C Kunting, Sheriah Laine M de Paz-Silava, Michele H Diwa, Ian Kim B Tabios
摘要中文翻译背景:自我采集阴道样本用于人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)DNA检测为医生采集的巴氏涂片提供了一种有前景的替代方案,特别是在资源匮乏的环境中。然而,关于菲律宾女性对其可接受性和偏好的数据仍然有限。本研究旨在评估女性对自我采集阴道样本进行HPV DNA检测与巴氏涂片的感知、体验、意愿和偏好。方法:在592名女性中进行了一项横断面研究。参与者接受了医生采集的巴氏涂片和自我采集的阴道样本用于HPV DNA检测。使用五点李克特量表问卷在自我采集前后测量感知。采用描述性统计分析,并使用配对和独立Wilcoxon检验比较李克特量表回答。使用Cochran's Q和McNemar检验分析接受筛查的意愿。参与者偏好的筛查方法的原因被主题分类,并以堆叠条形图显示。结果:自我采集通常被认为无痛、不不适、不尴尬且容易和方便。参与者进行自我采集后,可接受性显著提高(疼痛和不适的P < 0.001)。与巴氏涂片相比,自我采集被认为更不尴尬(P < 0.001),但被认为更不便于操作(P = 0.01)和更不方便(P = 0.004)。大多数参与者由于对医疗专业知识的信任以及对程序准确性、安全性和清洁度的信心,更倾向于医生进行的筛查(84.8%)。结论:自我采集阴道样本具有高度可接受性,但医生采集的筛查仍然是首选方法。通过改善自我采集的教育、演示和支持,可能提高HPV DNA检测的参与率,并扩大菲律宾宫颈癌筛查的可及性。科学与公共卫生意义:这些发现可以为提高HPV DNA检测参与率和扩大菲律宾宫颈癌筛查的可及性提供策略依据,强调了对自我采集方法进行教育和支持的必要性。局限性与证据强度:本研究的局限性在于其横断面设计和自我报告数据,这可能引入偏倚。证据强度中等,因为它提供了关于可接受性和偏好的见解,但并未建立因果关系。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
自我采集阴道样本用于人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)DNA检测为医生采集的巴氏涂片提供了一种有前景的替代方案,特别是在资源匮乏的环境中。然而,关于菲律宾女性对其可接受性和偏好的数据仍然有限。
设计与对象
在592名女性中进行了一项横断面研究。参与者接受了医生采集的巴氏涂片和自我采集的阴道样本用于HPV DNA检测。
核心方法
感知通过五点李克特量表问卷在自我采样前和后进行测量。使用描述性统计,并采用配对和独立样本威尔科克森检验来比较李克特量表的回答。接受筛查的意愿通过科克兰Q检验和麦克纳马检验进行分析。
主要结果
自我采集通常被认为无痛、不不适、不尴尬且容易和方便。参与者进行自我采集后,可接受性显著提高(疼痛和不适的P < 0.001)。与巴氏涂片相比,自我采集被认为更不尴尬(P < 0.001),但被认为更不便于操作(P = 0.01)和更不方便(P = 0.004)。大多数参与者由于对医疗专业知识的信任以及对程序准确性、安全性和清洁度的信心,更倾向于医生进行的筛查(84.8%)。
解释与创新
自我采集阴道样本具有高度可接受性,但医生采集的筛查仍然是首选方法。通过改善自我采集的教育、演示和支持,可能提高HPV DNA检测的参与率,并扩大菲律宾宫颈癌筛查的可及性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可能通过强调自我采样方法的教育和支持需求,为提高菲律宾HPV DNA检测的接受度并扩大筛查可及性提供策略依据。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于菲律宾的单一人群,并未考虑长期行为变化。证据是温和的,因为它是基于自我报告的看法和经验。

Acceptability and preference of self-collected vaginal samples for human papillomavirus DNA testing compared with physician-collected Pap smear.

Authors: Ourlad Alzeus G Tantengco, Mikayla Francesca N de Guzman, Amina C Kunting, Sheriah Laine M de Paz-Silava, Michele H Diwa, Ian Kim B Tabios
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Self-collected vaginal sampling for human papillomavirus (HPV) DNA testing provides a promising alternative to physician-collected Pap smears, especially in low-resource settings. However, data on its acceptability and preferences among Filipino women remain limited. This study aimed to assess women's perceptions, experiences, willingness and preferences regarding self-collected vaginal sampling for HPV DNA testing compared with Pap smears. METHODS: A cross-sectional study was conducted among 592 women. Participants underwent both physician-collected Pap smears and self-collected vaginal sampling for HPV DNA testing. Perceptions were measured using five-point Likert scale questionnaires before and after self-collection. Descriptive statistics were used, and paired and independent Wilcoxon tests were used to compare Likert responses. Willingness to undergo screening was analyzed with Cochran's Q and McNemar tests. Participants' reasons for their preferred screening method were categorized thematically and displayed in stacked bar charts. RESULTS: Self-collection was generally viewed as painless, not uncomfortable, non-embarrassing, and easy and convenient. Acceptability significantly increased after participants performed self-collection (P < 0.001 for pain and discomfort). Compared with Pap smears, self-collection was seen as less embarrassing (P < 0.001), but rated as less easy (P = 0.01) and less convenient (P = 0.004). Most participants preferred physician-administered screening (84.8%) due to trust in medical expertise, and confidence in the procedure's accuracy, safety and cleanliness. CONCLUSIONS: Self-collected vaginal sampling was highly acceptable, yet physician-collected screening remained the preferred method. Improving education, demonstrations, and support for self-collection may enhance the uptake of HPV DNA testing and broaden access to cervical cancer screening in the Philippines.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Self-collected vaginal sampling for human papillomavirus (HPV) DNA testing provides a promising alternative to physician-collected Pap smears, especially in low-resource settings. However, data on its acceptability and preferences among Filipino women remain limited.

A cross-sectional study was conducted among 592 women. Participants underwent both physician-collected Pap smears and self-collected vaginal sampling for HPV DNA testing.

Descriptive statistics were used, and paired and independent Wilcoxon tests were used to compare Likert responses. Willingness to undergo screening was analyzed with Cochran's Q and McNemar tests.

Compared with Pap smears, self-collection was seen as less embarrassing (P < 0.001), but rated as less easy (P = 0.01) and less convenient (P = 0.004). Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform strategies to improve the uptake of HPV DNA testing and broaden access to cervical cancer screening in the Philippines by highlighting the need for education and support for self-collection methods.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single population in the Philippines and does not account for long-term behavioral changes. The evidence is moderate as it is based on self-reported perceptions and experiences.

增强型纸基 DNA 吸附和高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 16 和 18 的即时检测。

Enhanced Paper-Based DNA Adsorption and Point-of-Care Detection of High-Risk Human Papillomaviruses 16 and 18.
作者： Neha Rani Das, Mohamed Asif Shajahan, Sivie Kurian, Veeramani Velayutham, Saranya Gopalakrishnan, Shivam Kumar Jha, Sumathi Chandran, Kasthuri Vasu, Vijay Sathish Kumar Irulappan, Vidhya Amirthalingam
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在资源匮乏的环境中进行 宫颈癌 筛查需要将快速 DNA 提取与灵敏、低成本 DNA 扩增和检测相结合的分子诊断。
设计与对象
在这里,我们提出了一个基于纸质的 DNA 检测 (PDD) 平台,该平台引入了分散式 HPV 诊断的两项机制进步。
核心方法
首先,我们建立了一种基于乙醇辅助将 DNA 捕获到纤维素纸上的免离心 DNA 分离策略,以提供高产率 DNA。其次,我们引入了一种基于咖啡环的荧光传感机制,可将 PicoGreen 标记的扩增产物的空间重新分布转换为定量读数。
主要结果
我们首次报道,相对于未经处理的纸,经过酸预处理的纤维素 (APC) 纸可以更有效地捕获 DNA,从而确定底物 pH 值是纸基 DNA 提取的关键因素。
解释与创新
FTIR 分析和 zeta 电位测量表明,APC 纸分别主要通过氢键和静电相互作用有效保留 DNA。
科研与公卫意义
通过将机械引导的 DNA 分离与基于物理的空间 DNA 检测相结合,这项工作使基于纸质的分子诊断超越了传统的基于强度的检测,并为 HR HPV 的即时检测提供了一种简单、廉价且可现场部署的策略。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Enhanced Paper-Based DNA Adsorption and Point-of-Care Detection of High-Risk Human Papillomaviruses 16 and 18.

Authors: Neha Rani Das, Mohamed Asif Shajahan, Sivie Kurian, Veeramani Velayutham, Saranya Gopalakrishnan, Shivam Kumar Jha, Sumathi Chandran, Kasthuri Vasu, Vijay Sathish Kumar Irulappan, Vidhya Amirthalingam
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer screening in low-resource settings requires molecular diagnostics that integrate rapid DNA extraction with sensitive, low-cost DNA amplification and detection. Here, we present a paper-based DNA detection (PDD) platform that introduces two mechanistic advances for decentralized HPV diagnostics. First, we establish a centrifuge-free DNA isolation strategy based on ethanol-assisted capture of DNA onto cellulose paper to provide high-yield DNA. Second, we introduce a coffee-ring-based fluorescence-sensing mechanism that converts the spatial redistribution of PicoGreen-labeled amplified products into a quantitative readout. We report, for the first time, that acid-pretreated cellulose (APC) paper captures DNA efficiently relative to untreated paper, identifying substrate pH as a critical factor in paper-based DNA extraction. FTIR analysis and zeta potential measurements revealed that APC paper retained DNA efficiently primarily through hydrogen-bonding and electrostatic interactions, respectively. Moreover, the mechanism behind the coffee ring-based amplified DNA detection was attributed to the chromatographic and evaporation effects. DNA is quantified using peripheral fluorescence intensity (PFI), enabling robust discrimination between positive and negative reactions. The integrated platform detected HPV16 and HPV18 with a limit of detection of 10 copies μL−1. Clinical evaluation of 52 cervical samples demonstrated 100% specificity for both HPV16 and HPV18, 100% sensitivity for HPV18, and 85.7% sensitivity for HPV16 compared with an IVD-approved real-time PCR assay. By combining mechanistically guided DNA isolation with physics-based spatial DNA detection, this work advances paper-based molecular diagnostics beyond conventional intensity-based assays and provides a simple, inexpensive, and field-deployable strategy for point-of-care detection of HR HPVs.

高危型HPV 感染期间的宫颈阴道脂质介质谱与随后的病毒清除或持续性相关:一项探索性试点研究

Cervicovaginal lipid mediator profiles during high-risk HPV infection associated with subsequent viral clearance or persistence: an exploratory pilot study
作者： Matteo Mucci, Antonio Recchiuti, Anna-Barbara Moscicki, Domenico Mattoscio
摘要中文翻译背景 高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是 宫颈癌 的主要原因,而大多数感染会自行清除。尽管已经研究了与 HPV 结果相关的局部免疫和微生物因素,但宫颈阴道微环境中脂质介质的作用仍知之甚少。方法 采用靶向液相色谱-串联质谱法对前瞻性纵向 HPV 队列研究 中 30 名参与者的宫颈阴道冲洗样本进行分析。样本取自 HPV 阴性女性、在随后就诊时相关 HPV 类型呈阴性的 HPV 阳性女性(后续清除组)以及在随后就诊时仍可检测到相同 HPV 类型的 HPV 阳性女性。结果在宫颈阴道冲洗样品中检测到六种脂质介质。在 HPV 感染期间,随后清除的女性比 HPV 阴性女性测得的总 12-HETE 和总 14-HDHA 浓度更高。在基于百分比的二次分析中,后续清除组中 PGE 2 的相对贡献低于 HPV 阴性组和后续持续组,而后续持续组中总 14-HDHA 的相对贡献高于 HPV 阴性女性。相关系数的直接比较确定了随后的清除率和持久性中相反的 TxB 2 –14-HDHA 关系。结论 HPV 感染期间宫颈阴道总 12-HETE 和总 14-HDHA 浓度较高与后续就诊时相关 HPV 类型的清除相关。在随后的清除和持久性中观察到的相反的 TxB 2 – 总 14-HDHA 关联进一步表明脂质介质协调中与结果相关的差异。这些探索性发现将脂质介质确定为 HPV 结果的候选相关因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是 宫颈癌 的主要原因,而大多数感染会自行清除。该研究旨在探讨宫颈阴道脂质介质在 HPV 感染结果中的作用。
设计与对象
这项探索性试点研究分析了前瞻性纵向 HPV 队列研究 中 30 名参与者的宫颈阴道清洗样本。该研究包括 HPV 阴性女性、在随后就诊时清除感染的 HPV 阳性女性以及持续感染的 HPV 阳性女性。
核心方法
使用靶向液相色谱-串联质谱法分析宫颈阴道冲洗样品。该研究的重点是测量脂质介质浓度并比较不同 HPV 感染结果。
主要结果
在宫颈阴道冲洗样品中检测到六种脂质介质。随后清除的女性比 HPV 阴性女性测得的总 12-HETE 和总 14-HDHA 浓度更高。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,HPV 感染期间的脂质介质谱可能与病毒清除或持续存在相关。清除组和持久性组中相反的 TxB2-14-HDHA 关系进一步表明与结果相关的脂质介质协调的差异。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果凸显了脂质介质作为预测 HPV 感染结果的生物标志物的潜力,这可以为未来关于 监测、诊断 和 治疗 策略的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是探索性的,样本量较小,限制了研究结果的普遍性。由于缺乏大规模验证和研究的观察性质,证据是中等的。

Cervicovaginal lipid mediator profiles during high-risk HPV infection associated with subsequent viral clearance or persistence: an exploratory pilot study

Authors: Matteo Mucci, Antonio Recchiuti, Anna-Barbara Moscicki, Domenico Mattoscio
Original abstract (English)
Background High-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is the primary cause of cervical cancer, whereas most infections are spontaneously cleared. Although local immune and microbial factors associated with HPV outcomes have been investigated, the contribution of lipid mediators within the cervicovaginal microenvironment remains poorly characterized. Methods Cervicovaginal wash samples from 30 participants in a prospective longitudinal HPV cohort were analyzed by targeted liquid chromatography–tandem mass spectrometry. Samples were obtained from HPV-negative women, HPV-positive women who were negative for the relevant HPV type at the subsequent visit (subsequent-clearance group), and HPV-positive women in whom the same HPV type remained detectable at the subsequent visit. Results Six lipid mediators were detected in cervicovaginal wash samples. During HPV infection, women with subsequent clearance had higher measured concentrations of total 12-HETE and total 14-HDHA than HPV-negative women. In the secondary percentage-based analysis, the relative contribution of PGE 2 was lower in the subsequent-clearance group than in both the HPV-negative and subsequent-persistence groups, while the relative contribution of total 14-HDHA was higher in the subsequent-persistence group than in HPV-negative women. Direct comparison of correlation coefficients identified an opposite TxB 2 –14-HDHA relationship in subsequent clearance and persistence. Conclusions Higher cervicovaginal concentrations of total 12-HETE and total 14-HDHA during HPV infection were associated with clearance of the relevant HPV type at the subsequent visit. The opposite TxB 2 –total 14-HDHA associations observed in subsequent clearance and persistence further suggest outcome-associated differences in lipid-mediator coordination. These exploratory findings identify lipid mediators as candidate correlates of HPV outcomes.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
High-risk human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is the primary cause of cervical cancer, whereas most infections are spontaneously cleared. The study aimed to explore the role of cervicovaginal lipid mediators in HPV infection outcomes.
Design and population
This exploratory pilot study analyzed cervicovaginal wash samples from 30 participants in a prospective longitudinal HPV cohort. The study included HPV-negative women, HPV-positive women who cleared the infection at the subsequent visit, and HPV-positive women who had persistent infection.
Core methods
Cervicovaginal wash samples were analyzed using targeted liquid chromatography–tandem mass spectrometry. The study focused on measuring lipid mediator concentrations and comparing them across different HPV infection outcomes.
Main results
Six lipid mediators were detected in cervicovaginal wash samples. Women with subsequent clearance had higher measured concentrations of total 12-HETE and total 14-HDHA than HPV-negative women.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that lipid mediator profiles during HPV infection may be associated with viral clearance or persistence. The opposite TxB2–14-HDHA relationships in clearance and persistence groups further indicate differences in lipid-mediator coordination related to outcomes.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results highlight the potential of lipid mediators as biomarkers for predicting HPV infection outcomes, which could inform future studies on surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is exploratory and based on a small sample size, limiting the generalizability of the findings. The evidence is moderate due to the lack of large-scale validation and the observational nature of the study.

5-氨基乙酰丙酸光动力疗法治疗 1 级宫颈上皮内瘤变患者的真实临床结果

Real-world clinical outcomes of 5-aminolevulinic acid photodynamic therapy in patients with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 1
作者： Tian-Ci Fan, Li-Na Geng, Guan-Wen Gao, Zi-Xun Long, De-Gao Liu, Yun-Fei Wang, Chang-Zhong Li
摘要中文翻译背景 1 级宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN1) 通常通过观察进行治疗,因为自发消退很常见,并且进展为高级别疾病的情况并不常见。尽管如此,一些持续感染HPV或反复出现低度异常的患者在适当的咨询后可能会选择积极的治疗。本研究旨在描述接受主动 治疗 的 CIN1 患者接受 5-氨基乙酰丙酸光动力疗法 (ALA-PDT) 的临床结果和安全性。方法 这项回顾性队列研究研究纳入了2021年7月至2025年7月期间在北京大学深圳医院接受ALA-PDT的269例CIN1患者。所有患者均接受了6次ALA-PDT,每次间隔7-14天。在 治疗 治疗前和 治疗 治疗后 3 个月(± 1 个月)随访期间评估 HPV 状态和 ThinPrep 细胞学检测(TCT)结果,部分患者在 治疗 治疗后 6 个月(± 1 个月)评估 HPV 状态和 ThinPrep 细胞学检测 (TCT) 结果。分析了 HPV 清除、细胞学改善和 治疗 相关不良事件。结果 266 例 HPV 阳性患者中,155 例(58.27%)在预定的 3 个月随访时 HPV 结果为阴性。3 个月时,247 名进行细胞学评估的患者中,有 205 名 (83.00%) 进行了 NILM 细胞学检查。治疗 或随访期间未观察到严重不良事件。结论 在这项回顾性 队列研究 研究中,ALA-PDT 后的随访期间观察到 HPV 阴性和细胞学结果改善,以及可接受的安全性且没有严重不良事件。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景 1 级宫颈上皮内瘤变 (CIN1) 通常通过观察进行治疗,因为自发消退很常见,并且进展为高级别疾病的情况并不常见。

这项回顾性队列研究研究纳入了2021年7月至2025年7月期间在北京大学深圳医院接受5-氨基乙酰丙酸光动力疗法(ALA-PDT)的269例CIN1患者。
核心方法

在 266 名 HPV 阳性患者中,有 155 名患者(58.27%)在预定的 3 个月随访中 HPV 结果呈阴性。3 个月时,247 名进行细胞学评估的患者中,有 205 名 (83.00%) 进行了 NILM 细胞学检查。

在这项回顾性 队列研究 研究中,在 ALA-PDT 后的随访期间观察到 HPV 阴性和细胞学结果改善,以及可接受的安全性且没有严重不良事件。
科研与公卫意义
这些研究结果表明,对于持续感染 HPV 或反复出现低度异常的 CIN1 患者,ALA-PDT 可能是一种可行的 治疗 选择,在 HPV 清除和细胞学改善方面提供潜在益处。
局限与证据强度
该研究是一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,可能会引入偏差并限制建立因果关系的能力。证据是中等的,因为它是基于观察数据而不是随机对照试验。

Real-world clinical outcomes of 5-aminolevulinic acid photodynamic therapy in patients with cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 1

Authors: Tian-Ci Fan, Li-Na Geng, Guan-Wen Gao, Zi-Xun Long, De-Gao Liu, Yun-Fei Wang, Chang-Zhong Li
Original abstract (English)
Background Cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 1 (CIN1) is generally managed by observation because spontaneous regression is common and progression to high-grade disease is uncommon. Nevertheless, some patients with persistent HPV infection or repeated low-grade abnormalities may elect active treatment after appropriate counseling. This study aimed to describe the clinical outcomes and safety of 5-aminolevulinic acid photodynamic therapy (ALA-PDT) in patients with CIN1 who underwent active treatment. Methods This retrospective cohort study included 269 patients with CIN1 who underwent ALA-PDT at Peking University Shenzhen Hospital between July 2021 and July 2025. All patients received six sessions of ALA-PDT at intervals of 7–14 days. HPV status and ThinPrep cytologic test (TCT) results were assessed before treatment and during follow-up at 3 months (± 1 month) and, in some patients, at 6 months (± 1 month) after treatment. HPV clearance, cytological improvement, and treatment-related adverse events were analyzed. Results Among the 266 HPV-positive patients, 155 patients (58.27%) had a negative HPV result at the predefined 3-month follow-up. At 3 months, 205 of 247 patients (83.00%) with available cytological assessment had NILM cytology. No serious adverse events were observed during treatment or follow-up. Conclusions In this retrospective cohort study, HPV negativity and improved cytological findings were observed during follow-up after ALA-PDT, together with an acceptable safety profile and no serious adverse events.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Background Cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 1 (CIN1) is generally managed by observation because spontaneous regression is common and progression to high-grade disease is uncommon.
Design and population
This retrospective cohort study included 269 patients with CIN1 who underwent 5-aminolevulinic acid photodynamic therapy (ALA-PDT) at Peking University Shenzhen Hospital between July 2021 and July 2025.
Core methods
HPV status and ThinPrep cytologic test (TCT) results were assessed before treatment and during follow-up at 3 months (± 1 month) and, in some patients, at 6 months (± 1 month) after treatment. All patients received six sessions of ALA-PDT at intervals of 7–14 days.
Main results
Among the 266 HPV-positive patients, 155 patients (58.27%) had a negative HPV result at the predefined 3-month follow-up. At 3 months, 205 of 247 patients (83.00%) with available cytological assessment had NILM cytology.
Interpretation and novelty
In this retrospective cohort study, HPV negativity and improved cytological findings were observed during follow-up after ALA-PDT, together with an acceptable safety profile and no serious adverse events.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that ALA-PDT may be a viable treatment option for patients with CIN1 who have persistent HPV infection or repeated low-grade abnormalities, offering potential benefits in terms of HPV clearance and cytological improvement.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a retrospective cohort study, which may introduce bias and limit the ability to establish causality. The evidence is moderate as it is based on observational data rather than randomized controlled trials.

奥约中学女青少年对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 作为 宫颈癌 潜在因素的了解和基线意识

owledge and Baseline Awareness of Human Papillomavirus (HPV) as a Potential Factor in Cervical Cancer among Female Adolescents in Secondary Schools in Oyo
作者： Olusola Olayiwola, Oyindamola Ayegbusi, Favour Olanrewaju, Ademola Alaba, Adeola-Kehinde Olaleye
摘要中文翻译宫颈癌 仍然是尼日利亚女性癌症相关 死亡率 的主要原因。尽管将 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 纳入国家免疫计划以针对女性青少年,但由于基线知识匮乏和认识欠佳而导致的 疫苗 犹豫不决给根除工作带来了重大瓶颈。这项以学校为基础的横断面研究旨在评估奥约州 245 名中学女生对 HPV 的基线认识和健康态度。使用经过验证的、自我管理的结构化问卷收集有关社会人口特征、基线研究以及对 HPV 和 宫颈癌 之间联系的认识的数据。使用描述性统计分析数据。参与者的平均年龄主要在 15-17 岁范围内(62.8%,n=155),参与者主要是约鲁巴人(87.4%,n=216)。对HPV的基本认知度较低:只有43.3%(n=107)从未听说过该病毒,56.7%(n=140)以前没有接触过该病毒。互联网和社交媒体是主要的信息渠道(26.7%,n=66),表现优于医护人员(17.0%,n=42)。引人注目的是,68.0% (n=168) 的人完全不知道 HPV 症状,58.7% (n=145) 承认完全不了解具体的临床表现。此外,虽然 44.9% (n=111) 的受访者认识到 HPV 会导致 宫颈癌,但只有 34.8% (n=84) 的受访者了解预防 HPV 感染可直接降低 宫颈癌 风险。Oyo 的女性青少年对 HPV 及其与 宫颈癌 的致癌联系缺乏公共卫生知识和认识。数字景观提供了最可行的干预窗口。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 仍然是尼日利亚女性癌症相关 死亡率 的主要原因。尽管将 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 纳入国家免疫计划以针对女性青少年,但由于基线知识匮乏和认识欠佳而导致的 疫苗 犹豫不决给根除工作带来了重大瓶颈。
设计与对象
这项以学校为基础的横断面研究旨在评估奥约州 245 名中学女生对 HPV 的基线认识和健康态度。参与者的平均年龄主要在 15-17 岁范围内(62.8%,n=155),参与者主要是约鲁巴人(87.4%,n=216)。
核心方法
使用经过验证的、自我管理的结构化问卷收集有关社会人口特征、基线研究以及对 HPV 和 宫颈癌 之间联系的认识的数据。使用描述性统计分析数据。
主要结果
对HPV的基本认知度较低:只有43.3%(n=107)从未听说过该病毒,56.7%(n=140)以前没有接触过该病毒。引人注目的是,68.0% (n=168) 的人完全不知道 HPV 症状,58.7% (n=145) 承认完全不了解具体的临床表现。此外,虽然 44.9% (n=111) 的受访者认识到 HPV 会导致 宫颈癌,但只有 34.8% (n=84) 的受访者了解预防 HPV 感染可直接降低 宫颈癌 风险。
解释与创新
Oyo 的女性青少年对 HPV 及其与 宫颈癌 的致癌联系缺乏公共卫生知识和认识。数字景观提供了最可行的干预窗口。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,迫切需要采取有针对性的教育干预措施,以提高奥约州女性青少年对 HPV 的认识,这可以提高 疫苗 的吸收率并减少 宫颈癌 发病率。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横截面设计的限制,无法建立因果关系,而且依赖自我报告的数据,可能会引入偏差。证据是中等的,因为它提供了意识的快照,但缺乏纵向或干预数据。

owledge and Baseline Awareness of Human Papillomavirus (HPV) as a Potential Factor in Cervical Cancer among Female Adolescents in Secondary Schools in Oyo

Authors: Olusola Olayiwola, Oyindamola Ayegbusi, Favour Olanrewaju, Ademola Alaba, Adeola-Kehinde Olaleye
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer remains a leading cause of cancer-related mortality among women in Nigeria. Despite the integration of the Human Papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine into the national immunization schedule to target female adolescents, vaccine hesitancy driven by poor baseline knowledge and suboptimal awareness presents a significant bottleneck to eradication efforts. This cross-sectional school-based study was carried out to assess the baseline awareness and health attitudes regarding HPV among 245 female secondary school adolescents in Oyo State. Data on socio-demographic characteristics, baseline study, and awareness of the link between HPV and cervical cancer were collected using validated, self-administered structured questionnaires. Data were analyzed using descriptive statistics. The mean age of the participants fell predominantly within the 15–17 years range (62.8%, n=155), and participants were primarily Yoruba (87.4%, n=216). Basic awareness of HPV was low: only 43.3% (n=107) had never heard of the virus, and 56.7% (n=140) had no prior exposure. The internet and social media were the leading information channels (26.7%, n=66), outperforming healthcare workers (17.0%, n=42). Strikingly, 68.0% (n=168) were completely unaware of HPV symptoms, and 58.7% (n=145) confessed total ignorance regarding specific clinical manifestations. Furthermore, while 44.9% (n=111) recognized that HPV causes cervical cancer, only 34.8% (n=84) understood that preventing HPV infection directly reduces cervical cancer risks. Female adolescents in Oyo present deficient public health knowledge and low awareness regarding HPV and its oncogenic links to cervical cancer. Digital landscapes offer the most viable window for intervention.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer remains a leading cause of cancer-related mortality among women in Nigeria. Despite the integration of the Human Papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine into the national immunization schedule to target female adolescents, vaccine hesitancy driven by poor baseline knowledge and suboptimal awareness presents a significant bottleneck to eradication efforts.
Design and population
This cross-sectional school-based study was carried out to assess the baseline awareness and health attitudes regarding HPV among 245 female secondary school adolescents in Oyo State. The mean age of the participants fell predominantly within the 15–17 years range (62.8%, n=155), and participants were primarily Yoruba (87.4%, n=216).
Core methods
Data on socio-demographic characteristics, baseline study, and awareness of the link between HPV and cervical cancer were collected using validated, self-administered structured questionnaires. Data were analyzed using descriptive statistics.
Main results
Basic awareness of HPV was low: only 43.3% (n=107) had never heard of the virus, and 56.7% (n=140) had no prior exposure. Strikingly, 68.0% (n=168) were completely unaware of HPV symptoms, and 58.7% (n=145) confessed total ignorance regarding specific clinical manifestations. Furthermore, while 44.9% (n=111) recognized that HPV causes cervical cancer, only 34.8% (n=84) understood that preventing HPV infection directly reduces cervical cancer risks.
Interpretation and novelty
Female adolescents in Oyo present deficient public health knowledge and low awareness regarding HPV and its oncogenic links to cervical cancer. Digital landscapes offer the most viable window for intervention.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the urgent need for targeted educational interventions to improve HPV awareness among female adolescents in Oyo State, which could enhance vaccine uptake and reduce cervical cancer incidence.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality, and its reliance on self-reported data, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate as it provides a snapshot of awareness but lacks longitudinal or interventional data.

与接受保守治疗的育龄妇女宫颈上皮内瘤变2持续存在和进展相关的高危因素:回顾性分析

High-risk factors associated with persistence and progression of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 2 in women of reproductive age undergoing conservative management: a retrospective analysis
作者： Mei-Lian Chen, Gang Xiao, Hong-Mei Xiao, Ying-Xue Bai, Yi-Xue Zhou, Xue-Lian Tan, Li-Mei Wu, Hai-Xia Jiang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定接受保守治疗的育龄妇女中与宫颈上皮内瘤变 2 (CIN2) 持续或进展为 CIN3 相关的高危因素,以指导 治疗 保留生育能力的策略。

这项回顾性分析包括 226 名接受 CIN2 保守治疗的患者,并随访评估病变消退、持续性或进展为 CIN3。

High-risk factors associated with persistence and progression of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 2 in women of reproductive age undergoing conservative management: a retrospective analysis

Authors: Mei-Lian Chen, Gang Xiao, Hong-Mei Xiao, Ying-Xue Bai, Yi-Xue Zhou, Xue-Lian Tan, Li-Mei Wu, Hai-Xia Jiang
Original abstract (English)
Objective To investigate the high-risk factors associated with the persistence or progression of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 2 (CIN2) to cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 3 (CIN3) in women of childbearing age undergoing conservative management, thereby providing guidance for selecting appropriate treatment strategies for women with CIN2 who wish to preserve their fertility. Methods Univariate and multivariate logistic regression analyses were performed using the clinical and pathological characteristics of the included patients to identify high-risk factors associated with the persistence or progression of CIN2. Results In total, 226 patients were included in this study. Of these, 142 (62.8%) experienced lesion regression, 58 (25.7%) had persistent CIN2, and 26 (11.5%) progressed to CIN3. Univariate analysis indicated that age, high- squamous intraepithelial lesion (HSIL), human papillomavirus type 16/18 (HPV16/18) positivity, and P16/Ki-67 positivity were associated with persistence of CIN2 or progression to CIN3. Multivariate analysis revealed that age of >35 years (OR 2.732, 95% CI 1.350–5.529), HPV16/18 positivity (OR 1.506, 95% CI 1.129–17.611), HSIL (OR 4.693, 95% CI 3.554–19.211), and P16/Ki-67 positivity (OR 4.007, 95% CI 2.819–6.014) were associated with persistence or progression of CIN2. Conclusion Age of >35 years, HPV16/18 positivity, HSIL, and P16/Ki-67 positivity are associated with persistence and progression of CIN2. Conservative management with outpatient follow-up is an appropriate treatment option for carefully selected patients without these high-risk factors.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify high-risk factors associated with the persistence or progression of cervical intraepithelial neoplasia 2 (CIN2) to CIN3 in women of reproductive age undergoing conservative management, to guide treatment strategies that preserve fertility.
Design and population
This retrospective analysis included 226 patients undergoing conservative management for CIN2, with follow-up to assess lesion regression, persistence, or progression to CIN3.
Core methods
Univariate and multivariate logistic regression analyses were conducted using clinical and pathological characteristics to identify high-risk factors for CIN2 persistence or progression.
Main results
Of the 226 patients, 142 (62.8%) experienced lesion regression, 58 (25.7%) had persistent CIN2, and 26 (11.5%) progressed to CIN3.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified age >35 years, HPV16/18 positivity, HSIL, and P16/Ki-67 positivity as significant predictors of CIN2 persistence or progression.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform targeted surveillance and management strategies for women with CIN2, particularly those at higher risk of progression.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential for selection bias, but the evidence is strong due to the use of multivariate analysis to control for confounding factors.

Long-Term Outcomes of De-Escalated 30-Gy Elective Irradiation in HPV-Associated Oropharyngeal Cancer

Authors: Amir H. Safavi, E. Christopher Dee, Chiaojung Jillian Tsai, Yingzhi Wu, Sean M. McBride, Daphna Y. Gelblum, Yao Yu, Linda C. Chen, Kaveh Zakeri, Achraf A. Shamseddine
Original abstract (English)
This cohort study describes 5-year outcomes following a de-escalated 30-Gy elective nodal irradiation regimen during definitive concurrent chemoradiotherapy among patients with human papillomavirus–associated oropharyngeal carcinoma.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the long-term outcomes of a de-escalated 30-Gy elective nodal irradiation regimen in patients with HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer, addressing the need for understanding its efficacy and safety in this specific context.
Design and population
A cohort study was conducted among patients with human papillomavirus–associated oropharyngeal carcinoma who received a de-escalated 30-Gy elective nodal irradiation regimen during definitive concurrent chemoradiotherapy.
Core methods
The study involved a cohort of patients with HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer who underwent a de-escalated 30-Gy elective nodal irradiation regimen as part of their definitive concurrent chemoradiotherapy.
Main results
The study describes 5-year outcomes following the de-escalated 30-Gy elective nodal irradiation regimen.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide insights into the long-term outcomes of a de-escalated irradiation regimen in HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer, contributing to the understanding of treatment strategies in this specific context.

Limitations and evidence strength

HPV阳性鼻咽癌患者循环肿瘤HPV-DNA检测

Detection of circulating tumor HPV-DNA in patients with HPV-positive sinonasal and nasopharyngeal carcinoma
作者： Jakob Myllerup Jensen, Simone Kloch Bendtsen, Kathrine Kronberg Jakobsen, Katalin Kiss, Maria Rossing, Christian Grønhøj, Christian Von Buchwald
摘要中文翻译背景:大约 30% 的鼻窦癌和鼻咽癌病例与 HPV 相关,但调查这些患者中循环肿瘤 HPV-DNA (ctHPV-DNA) 的研究很少。本研究的目的是探讨 HPV 阳性 (HPV+) 鼻窦癌和鼻咽癌患者治疗前血液样本中 ctHPV-DNA 的敏感性。方法:本研究纳入 2017 年至 2025 年期间在丹麦哥本哈根 Rigshospitalet 耳鼻喉科、头颈外科和听力科接受治疗的确诊 HPV+ 鼻窦癌或鼻咽癌的患者。在 治疗 启动之前,在 Streck 管中抽取血样,并通过微滴数字 PCR (ddPCR) 分析 ctHPV-DNA 的存在。结果:纳入了 15 名原发性 HPV+ 鼻窦癌或鼻咽癌患者。除2例HPV相关多表型癌外,其余均为鳞状细胞癌。HPV16 最为常见,在 6 名患者 (40%) 中发现。还包括 HPV18 (n = 2)、HPV33 (n = 1)、HPV35 (n = 3)、HPV45 (n = 1) 和 HPV56 (n = 2)。在纳入患者的治疗前血液样本中检测 ctHPV-DNA 的敏感性为 80%。ctHPV-DNA 水平随着 T 类的增加而显着增加。结论:80% HPV+ 鼻窦癌和鼻咽癌患者的治疗前血液样本中检测到 ctHPV-DNA。这支持进一步研究 ctHPV-DNA 作为这些患者 治疗 监测 后的生物标志物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨 HPV 阳性鼻窦癌和鼻咽癌患者治疗前血液样本中循环肿瘤 HPV-DNA (ctHPV-DNA) 的敏感性,解决该领域研究的匮乏问题。
设计与对象
该研究纳入了 2017 年至 2025 年在丹麦哥本哈根 Rigshospitalet 耳鼻喉科、头颈外科和听力科接受治疗的 15 名原发性 HPV+ 鼻窦癌或鼻咽癌患者。
核心方法
在 治疗 启动前抽取血样,并通过微滴数字 PCR (ddPCR) 分析 ctHPV-DNA 的存在。
主要结果
80% 的纳入患者中检测到 ctHPV-DNA,其中 HPV16 是最常见的类型,在 40% 的病例中检测到。
解释与创新
该研究支持进一步研究 ctHPV-DNA 作为 HPV+ 鼻窦癌和鼻咽癌患者 治疗 监测 后的生物标志物。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,ctHPV-DNA 检测可用于监测 治疗 后的这些患者,可能改善 监测 和管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量较小以及仅包括来自单个中心的患者这一事实的限制,这可能会影响普遍性。

Detection of circulating tumor HPV-DNA in patients with HPV-positive sinonasal and nasopharyngeal carcinoma

Authors: Jakob Myllerup Jensen, Simone Kloch Bendtsen, Kathrine Kronberg Jakobsen, Katalin Kiss, Maria Rossing, Christian Grønhøj, Christian Von Buchwald
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Approximately 30% of sinonasal and nasopharyngeal carcinoma cases are HPV-associated, yet studies investigating circulating tumor HPV-DNA (ctHPV-DNA) among these patients are scarce. The aim of this study was to investigate the sensitivity of ctHPV-DNA in pretreatment blood samples from patients with HPV-positive (HPV+) sinonasal and nasopharyngeal carcinoma. METHODS: Patients with a confirmed HPV+ sinonasal or nasopharyngeal carcinoma treated at the Department of Otorhinolaryngology, Head and Neck Surgery and Audiology at Rigshospitalet, Copenhagen, Denmark in the period 2017-2025 were included in this study. Blood samples were drawn in Streck tubes before treatment initiation and analysed for the presence of ctHPV-DNA by Droplet digital PCR (ddPCR). RESULTS: Fifteen patients with primary HPV+ sinonasal or nasopharyngeal carcinoma were included. Except for two cases of HPV-related multiphenotypic carcinoma, all cases were squamous cell carcinoma. HPV16 was most prevalent and found in 6 patients (40%). HPV18 (n = 2), HPV33 (n = 1), HPV35 (n = 3), HPV45 (n = 1), and HPV56 (n = 2) were also represented. The sensitivity of detecting ctHPV-DNA in the pretreatment blood samples of the included patients was 80%. The level of ctHPV-DNA increased significantly with increasing T-category. CONCLUSION: ctHPV-DNA was detected in pretreatment blood samples in 80% of patients with HPV+ sinonasal and nasopharyngeal carcinoma. This supports further investigation of ctHPV-DNA as a biomarker for post-treatment surveillance in these patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the sensitivity of circulating tumor HPV-DNA (ctHPV-DNA) in pretreatment blood samples from patients with HPV-positive sinonasal and nasopharyngeal carcinoma, addressing the scarcity of studies in this area.
Design and population
The study included 15 patients with primary HPV+ sinonasal or nasopharyngeal carcinoma treated at the Department of Otorhinolaryngology, Head and Neck Surgery and Audiology at Rigshospitalet, Copenhagen, Denmark from 2017 to 2025.
Core methods
Blood samples were drawn before treatment initiation and analysed for the presence of ctHPV-DNA by Droplet digital PCR (ddPCR).
Main results
ctHPV-DNA was detected in 80% of the included patients, with HPV16 being the most prevalent type, found in 40% of the cases.
Interpretation and novelty
The study supports further investigation of ctHPV-DNA as a biomarker for post-treatment surveillance in HPV+ sinonasal and nasopharyngeal carcinoma patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that ctHPV-DNA detection could be useful for monitoring these patients post-treatment, potentially improving surveillance and management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and the fact that it only included patients from a single centre, which may affect generalisability.

人类肿瘤病毒的系统药理学:针对病毒驱动的癌症的基于网络的药物发现。

Systems Pharmacology of Human Oncoviruses: Network-Based Drug Discovery for Virus-Driven Cancers.
作者： Jingyi Sun, Changning Song, Yining Hua, Jiayin Pang, Shuaiting Sun
摘要中文翻译人类肿瘤病毒,包括 高危型HPV、EBV、HBV、HCV、MCPyV、KSHV 和 HTLV-1,约占全球人类恶性肿瘤的 15%-20%。病毒癌蛋白不是通过孤立的途径发挥作用,而是系统地重新连接 宿主 调控网络,使传统的单靶点肿瘤学范例容易受到快速耐药性和临床失败的影响。系统药理学提供了一个变革性的、以网络为中心的框架,将病毒驱动的癌症视为 宿主 蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用网络的系统性扰动。这篇综述综合了主要人类肿瘤病毒的计算方法、网络拓扑和药物发现策略。我们研究图中心性算法、定量网络邻近度指标(Z 分数)和互补暴露协同模型(SAB)如何发现隐藏的网络漏洞并指导靶向药物的重新利用。尽管具有不同的基因组结构和组织向性,致癌病毒聚集在共享的 宿主 相互作用组中心,特别是 TP53、AKT1、BCL2、MYC、STAT3 和 VEGFA,以驱动细胞周期失调、凋亡抑制和微环境重塑。我们评估重新定位的合成药物(例如激酶抑制剂和 宿主 离子通道阻滞剂)和多靶点生物活性制剂,详细说明其针对病毒 宿主 依赖性的机制。最后,我们解决了关键的翻译瓶颈,包括计算机结合预测和体内药代动力学之间的差异、广泛的血浆蛋白结合、肿瘤渗透障碍和病毒潜伏期 储存宿主。克服这些障碍需要整合深度图学习、单细胞空间多组学和患者衍生的类器官模型,以加速病毒肿瘤学网络靶向治疗的临床实现。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本综述提出了更全面地了解人类肿瘤病毒(特别是 高危型HPV)如何导致全球恶性肿瘤的需要,以及传统单靶点肿瘤学方法在治疗病毒驱动的癌症方面的局限性。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了主要人类肿瘤病毒(包括 高危型HPV)及其与 宿主 网络的相互作用的计算方法和药物发现策略。
核心方法
该综述采用图中心性算法、定量网络邻近度指标(Z 分数)和互补暴露协同模型(SAB)来识别网络漏洞并指导针对病毒驱动的癌症的靶向药物重新利用。
主要结果
该综述强调,包括 高危型HPV 在内的致癌病毒聚集在共享的 宿主 相互作用组中心,如 TP53、AKT1、BCL2、MYC、STAT3 和 VEGFA,这些对细胞周期失调、细胞凋亡抑制和微环境重塑至关重要。
解释与创新
作者解释说,致癌病毒在共享的 宿主 中心上的聚集表明了一个共同的治疗目标,并且他们提出了一个新的药物发现框架,该框架整合了系统药理学和基于网络的方法。
科研与公卫意义
通过识别 宿主 相互作用组中的共同脆弱性,这些发现可以为 HPV 相关癌症(例如宫颈上皮内瘤变和 HPV 相关 宫颈癌)的网络靶向治疗的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于对现有方法的回顾,并且不提供原始实验数据,但它提供了当前方法的高水平综合,并对它们对 HPV 驱动的癌症的适用性有一定的信心。

Systems Pharmacology of Human Oncoviruses: Network-Based Drug Discovery for Virus-Driven Cancers.

Authors: Jingyi Sun, Changning Song, Yining Hua, Jiayin Pang, Shuaiting Sun
Original abstract (English)
Human oncoviruses, including high-risk HPV, EBV, HBV, HCV, MCPyV, KSHV, and HTLV-1, account for approximately 15%-20% of global human malignancies. Rather than acting through isolated pathways, viral oncoproteins systematically rewire host regulatory networks, rendering conventional single-target oncology paradigms vulnerable to rapid resistance and clinical failure. Systems pharmacology offers a transformative, network-centric framework that views virus-driven cancers as systemic perturbations of host protein-protein interaction networks. This review synthesises computational methodology, network topology, and drug discovery strategies across major human oncoviruses. We examine how graph centrality algorithms, quantitative network proximity metrics (Z-score), and complementary exposure synergy modelling (S AB ) uncover hidden network vulnerabilities and guide targeted drug repurposing. Despite distinct genomic structures and tissue tropisms, oncogenic viruses converge on shared host interactome hubs, notably TP53, AKT1, BCL2, MYC, STAT3, and VEGFA, to drive cell cycle dysregulation, apoptotic suppression, and microenvironmental remodelling. We evaluate repositioned synthetic pharmaceuticals (such as kinase inhibitors and host ion channel blockers) and multi-target bioactive formulations, detailing their mechanisms in targeting viral host dependencies. Finally, we address key translational bottlenecks, including disparities between in silico binding predictions and in vivo pharmacokinetics, extensive plasma protein binding, tumour penetration barriers, and viral latency reservoirs. Overcoming these hurdles will require integrating deep graph learning, single-cell spatial multi-omics, and patient-derived organoid models to accelerate the clinical realization of network-targeted therapies in viral oncology.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This review addresses the need for a more comprehensive understanding of how human oncoviruses, particularly high-risk HPV, contribute to global malignancies and the limitations of conventional single-target oncology approaches in treating virus-driven cancers.
Design and population
The study is a review of computational methodologies and drug discovery strategies focused on major human oncoviruses, including high-risk HPV, and their interactions with host networks.
Core methods
The review employs graph centrality algorithms, quantitative network proximity metrics (Z-score), and complementary exposure synergy modelling (S AB ) to identify network vulnerabilities and guide targeted drug repurposing for virus-driven cancers.
Main results
The review highlights that oncogenic viruses, including high-risk HPV, converge on shared host interactome hubs such as TP53, AKT1, BCL2, MYC, STAT3, and VEGFA, which are critical for cell cycle dysregulation, apoptotic suppression, and microenvironmental remodelling.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the convergence of oncogenic viruses on shared host hubs suggests a common therapeutic target, and they present a novel framework for drug discovery that integrates systems pharmacology and network-based approaches.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of network-targeted therapies for HPV-associated cancers, such as cervical intraepithelial neoplasia and HPV-associated cervical cancer, by identifying shared vulnerabilities in host interactomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a review of existing methodologies and does not present original experimental data, but it provides a high-level synthesis of current approaches with a moderate confidence in their applicability to HPV-driven cancers.

印度对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 的需求不断增长:家长和社区接受度的行为和社会驱动因素回顾

Building Demand for Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Vaccination in India: A Review of the Behavioural and Social Drivers of Parental and Community Acceptance
作者： Akash P. Zanwar, Priya Sahni, Arti Arti, Jeevan Garg
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述的重点是了解印度家长和社区接受 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 的行为和社会驱动因素,特别是在新发起的全国 HPV 疫苗接种 运动的背景下。核心问题是这些驱动因素如何影响 14 岁女孩对 疫苗接种 的接受,重点是从基于成本的计划向免费、国家认可的单剂量计划的转变。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对印度父母和社区接受 HPV 疫苗接种 证据的叙述性综合,这些证据是通过结构化 PubMed 搜索确定的。证据是使用世界卫生组织 (WHO) 行为和社会驱动因素 (BeSD) 框架进行组织的。该综述没有报告荟萃分析或汇总估计,而是综合了数量有限的研究结果,这些研究主要集中在南部地区。
共识与结论
印度的研究一致报告称,人们的意识较低,对安全和不孕不育的担忧,以及对 疫苗 可能鼓励早期性活动的担忧。决定受到更广泛的家庭的影响,父亲和长辈往往拥有最终决定权,并受到医生和一线工作人员建议的影响。全国运动消除了成本障碍,但没有解决基于信仰和社会的障碍,这些障碍仍然很重要。
争议与缺口
大多数研究衡量的是陈述的意愿而不是实际的 疫苗接种,而且证据集中在南部的几个地区,限制了普遍性。各州的接受程度存在很大差异,审查指出,向免费计划的转变改变了早期发现的相关性。证据并未涉及特定 HPV 类型的作用或其与宫颈上皮内瘤变或 HPV 相关 宫颈癌 等疾病的关联。
科研与实践启示
需求生成应优先考虑让值得信赖的卫生工作者和教师推荐 疫苗接种,让大家庭和社区领袖参与进来,预防错误信息,并让同意和获取变得容易。Standardised measurement of behavioural drivers,using tools such as the WHO BeSD surveys,should be built into campaign monitoring.

Building Demand for Human Papillomavirus (HPV) Vaccination in India: A Review of the Behavioural and Social Drivers of Parental and Community Acceptance

Authors: Akash P. Zanwar, Priya Sahni, Arti Arti, Jeevan Garg
Original abstract (English)
India launched its National HPV Vaccination Campaign in February 2026, offering free, voluntary, singledose vaccination to 14-year-old girls with parental consent.By September 2026, more than 80 lakh doses had been administered against an estimated annual cohort of about 1.2 crore girls, with wide variation across states.Because uptake depends on parental decisions, understanding what drives acceptance is now a programme priority rather than an academic question.This narrative review synthesises Indian evidence on parental and community acceptance of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination, identified through a structured PubMed search and organised by the World Health Organization (WHO) Behavioural and Social Drivers (BeSD) framework, and considers how the shift to a free, nationally endorsed, single-dose programme changes the relevance of earlier findings.Indian studies consistently report low awareness, fears about safety and infertility, and concerns that the vaccine may encourage early sexual activity.Decisions are shaped by the wider family, with fathers and elders often having the final say, and by the recommendations of doctors and frontline workers.Most studies measure stated willingness rather than actual vaccination, and the evidence is concentrated in a few southern districts.Before 2026, cost was a dominant practical barrier; with free provision, the remaining practical barriers are mostly access, consent processes, and reaching girls outside school.The national campaign removes the cost barrier but not the belief-based and social barriers.Demand generation should prioritise equipping trusted health workers and teachers to recommend vaccination, engaging extended families and community leaders, pre-empting misinformation, and making consent and access easy.Standardised measurement of behavioural drivers, using tools such as the WHO BeSD surveys, should be built into campaign monitoring.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on understanding the behavioural and social drivers of parental and community acceptance of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination in India, particularly in the context of the newly launched National HPV Vaccination Campaign. The central question is how these drivers influence vaccination uptake among 14-year-old girls, with an emphasis on the shift from a cost-based to a free, nationally endorsed, single-dose programme.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of Indian evidence on parental and community acceptance of HPV vaccination, identified through a structured PubMed search. The evidence is organised using the World Health Organization (WHO) Behavioural and Social Drivers (BeSD) framework. The review does not report on meta-analysis or pooled estimates but synthesises findings from a limited number of studies, primarily concentrated in southern districts.
Consensus and conclusions
Indian studies consistently report low awareness, fears about safety and infertility, and concerns that the vaccine may encourage early sexual activity. Decisions are influenced by the wider family, with fathers and elders often having the final say, and by the recommendations of doctors and frontline workers. The national campaign removes the cost barrier but does not address belief-based and social barriers, which remain significant.
Controversies and gaps
Most studies measure stated willingness rather than actual vaccination, and the evidence is concentrated in a few southern districts, limiting generalisability. There is wide variation in uptake across states, and the review notes that the shift to a free programme changes the relevance of earlier findings. The evidence does not address the role of specific HPV types or their association with diseases such as cervical intraepithelial neoplasia or HPV-associated cervical cancer.
Research and practice implications
Demand generation should prioritise equipping trusted health workers and teachers to recommend vaccination, engaging extended families and community leaders, pre-empting misinformation, and making consent and access easy. Standardised measurement of behavioural drivers, using tools such as the WHO BeSD surveys, should be built into campaign monitoring.

Journal: Journal of Epidemiology and Global Health  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-22  |  DOI: 10.1007/s44197-026-00655-3  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

马拉维姆祖祖市女性 患病率、HPV 类型类别以及高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 感染的相关因素

Prevalence, HPV Type Categories, and Associated Factors of High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Infection among Women in Mzuzu City, Malawi
作者： Cynthia Sifenjane Phiri, Pizga Kumwenda
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是 宫颈癌 的主要原因,这仍然是撒哈拉以南非洲地区,特别是马拉维的一个重大公共卫生挑战。了解 HPV 类型的本地分布及其相关危险因素对于优化筛查和 疫苗接种 策略至关重要。该研究旨在确定马拉维姆祖祖女性中流行的 HPV 类型的分布情况。这是一项横断面回顾性研究,纳入了 2021 年 5 月至 2022 年 12 月期间在马拉维姆祖祖城市卫生中心接受妇科服务的 385 名女性。对临床记录的年龄、居住地点、艾滋病毒状况和 HPV 结果进行了审查。使用 Xpert HPV 检测进行 HPV DNA 检测和基因分型,该检测可识别 HPV 16、HPV 18/45 以及其他 11 种 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 基因型的汇总组。使用描述性统计来评估 高危型HPV 的 患病率 及其类型分布。HR-HPV 感染的总体 患病率 为 42.9%。主要类型是其他 HR-HPV 类型 (34%),其次是 HPV 18/45 (11.7%) 和 HPV 16 (9.1%)。26.7% 的阳性病例发生多重感染,最常见的组合是 HPV 18/45 和其他 HR-HPV 类型。26 岁以下女性的感染率最高(55.6%),而南姆祖祖的 HPV 阳性病例数量最多(49.2%)。HIV 阳性女性 (51.6%) 的 HPV 感染率高于 HIV 阴性女性 (40%)。该研究表明,姆祖祖市女性的 HR-HPV 类型类别具有较高的 患病率 和多样性,其中非 疫苗 HR-HPV 类型占主导地位,并且与 HIV 感染存在显着关联。这些发现强调了对持续分子 监测 的需求以及对更广谱 HPV 疫苗 的考虑。将 HPV 筛查纳入艾滋病毒护理并优先考虑对年轻女性进行预防性干预可能会大大减轻马拉维的 宫颈癌 负担。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染是 宫颈癌 的主要原因,这仍然是撒哈拉以南非洲地区,特别是马拉维的一个重大公共卫生挑战。该研究旨在确定马拉维姆祖祖女性中流行的 HPV 类型的分布情况。
证据基础与方法
这是一项横断面回顾性研究,纳入了 2021 年 5 月至 2022 年 12 月期间在马拉维姆祖祖城市卫生中心接受妇科服务的 385 名女性。对临床记录的年龄、居住地点、艾滋病毒状况和 HPV 结果进行了审查。使用 Xpert HPV 检测进行 HPV DNA 检测和基因分型,该检测可识别 HPV 16、HPV 18/45 以及其他 11 种 高危型HPV (HR-HPV) 基因型的汇总组。使用描述性统计来评估 高危型HPV 的 患病率 及其类型分布。
共识与结论
HR-HPV感染的总体患病率为42.9%。主要类型是其他 HR-HPV 类型 (34%),其次是 HPV 18/45 (11.7%) 和 HPV 16 (9.1%)。26.7% 的阳性病例发生多重感染,最常见的组合是 HPV 18/45 和其他 HR-HPV 类型。该研究表明,姆祖祖市女性的 HR-HPV 类型类别具有较高的 患病率 和多样性,其中非 疫苗 HR-HPV 类型占主导地位,并且与 HIV 感染存在显着关联。
争议与缺口
该研究没有报告 HPV 感染或其进展为 宫颈癌 的具体机制。它还没有解决非 疫苗 HR-HPV 类型对疾病结果的潜在影响或当前针对这些类型的筛查方法的有效性。
科研与实践启示
这些发现强调了对持续分子 监测 的需求以及对更广谱 HPV 疫苗 的考虑。将 HPV 筛查纳入艾滋病毒护理并优先考虑对年轻女性进行预防性干预可能会大大减轻马拉维的 宫颈癌 负担。

Prevalence, HPV Type Categories, and Associated Factors of High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Infection among Women in Mzuzu City, Malawi

Authors: Cynthia Sifenjane Phiri, Pizga Kumwenda
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is the primary cause of cervical cancer, which remains a major public health challenge in sub-Saharan Africa, particularly in Malawi. Understanding the local distribution of HPV types and their associated risk factors is essential for optimising screening and vaccination strategies. The study aimed to establish the distribution of HPV types circulating among women in Mzuzu, Malawi. This was a cross-sectional retrospective study which included 385 women attending gynecological services at Mzuzu Urban Health Centre, Malawi, between May 2021 and December 2022. Clinical records were reviewed for age, residential location, HIV status, and HPV results. HPV DNA detection and genotyping were performed using the Xpert HPV assay, which identifies HPV 16, HPV 18/45, and a pooled group of 11 other high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) genotypes. Descriptive statistics were used to assess the prevalence of high-risk HPV and its type distribution. The overall prevalence of HR- HPV infection was 42.9%. The predominant types were among the other HR-HPV types (34%), followed by HPV 18/45 (11.7%) and HPV 16 (9.1%). Multiple infections occurred in 26.7% of positive cases, with the most common combination being HPV 18/45 and other HR-HPV types. The highest infection rate was observed among women less than 26 years (55.6%), while Mzuzu South recorded the largest number of HPV-positive cases (49.2%). HPV infection was more frequent among HIV-positive women (51.6%) than HIV-negative women (40%). The study demonstrates a high prevalence and diversity of HR-HPV type categories among women in Mzuzu City, with non-vaccine HR-HPV types predominating and a notable association with HIV infection. These findings emphasise the need for ongoing molecular surveillance and the consideration of broader-spectrum HPV vaccines. Integrating HPV screening into HIV care and prioritising preventive interventions for younger women may substantially reduce the cervical cancer burden in Malawi.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is the primary cause of cervical cancer, which remains a major public health challenge in sub-Saharan Africa, particularly in Malawi. The study aimed to establish the distribution of HPV types circulating among women in Mzuzu, Malawi.
Evidence base and method
This was a cross-sectional retrospective study which included 385 women attending gynecological services at Mzuzu Urban Health Centre, Malawi, between May 2021 and December 2022. Clinical records were reviewed for age, residential location, HIV status, and HPV results. HPV DNA detection and genotyping were performed using the Xpert HPV assay, which identifies HPV 16, HPV 18/45, and a pooled group of 11 other high-risk HPV (HR-HPV) genotypes. Descriptive statistics were used to assess the prevalence of high-risk HPV and its type distribution.
Consensus and conclusions
The overall prevalence of HR-HPV infection was 42.9%. The predominant types were among the other HR-HPV types (34%), followed by HPV 18/45 (11.7%) and HPV 16 (9.1%). Multiple infections occurred in 26.7% of positive cases, with the most common combination being HPV 18/45 and other HR-HPV types. The study demonstrates a high prevalence and diversity of HR-HPV type categories among women in Mzuzu City, with non-vaccine HR-HPV types predominating and a notable association with HIV infection.
Controversies and gaps
The study does not report on the specific mechanisms of HPV infection or its progression to cervical cancer. It also does not address the potential impact of non-vaccine HR-HPV types on disease outcomes or the effectiveness of current screening methods against these types.
Research and practice implications
These findings emphasise the need for ongoing molecular surveillance and the consideration of broader-spectrum HPV vaccines. Integrating HPV screening into HIV care and prioritising preventive interventions for younger women may substantially reduce the cervical cancer burden in Malawi.

简短通讯:澳大利亚大都市艾滋病毒服务中的乙型肝炎、人乳头瘤病毒、肺炎球菌和流感 疫苗接种

Brief communication: hepatitis B, human papillomavirus, pneumococcal and influenza vaccination in an Australian metropolitan HIV service
作者： Sophie Sparkes, Nicholas Leedman, Michael Taggart
摘要中文翻译HIV 感染者承受着不成比例的 疫苗 可预防感染的负担。这项回顾性审计评估了澳大利亚 HIV 服务机构中具有足够的 疫苗接种 抗乙型肝炎、人乳头瘤病毒、肺炎球菌和流感的患者比例。与肺炎球菌结合物 (19.0%)、肺炎球菌多糖 (6.6%) 和 人乳头瘤病毒 (7.4%) 疫苗接种 相比,足够的乙型肝炎 疫苗接种 (47.9%) 的比例显着更高 (p < 0.001)。流感 疫苗接种 发病率 (30.6%) 与西澳大利亚一般人群发病率相似。需要进行充分的 疫苗接种 审查,以预防乙型肝炎获得和 HPV 相关肿瘤,特别是在不含替诺福韦的抗逆转录病毒治疗方案和常规肛门癌筛查的背景下。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究重点关注 HIV 感染者针对乙型肝炎、人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV)、肺炎球菌和流感的 疫苗接种 状态。核心问题是这些患者是否有足够的 疫苗接种 保险来对抗这些疾病,特别是在抗逆转录病毒治疗和癌症筛查的背景下。
证据基础与方法
该审查是在澳大利亚艾滋病毒服务机构内进行的回顾性审计。它评估了具有足够的 疫苗接种 抗乙型肝炎、人乳头瘤病毒、肺炎球菌和流感的患者比例。研究设计涉及分析 疫苗接种 状态的现有记录,但没有明确报告荟萃分析或共识过程。
共识与结论
研究发现,与肺炎球菌结合物(19.0%)、肺炎球菌多糖(6.6%)和人乳头瘤病毒(7.4%)疫苗接种(7.4%)相比,乙型肝炎疫苗接种(47.9%)充足的患者比例明显更高。这些发现凸显了 HIV 人群中这些疾病的 疫苗接种 覆盖率差异。
争议与缺口
研究指出,流感 疫苗接种 发病率 (30.6%) 与西澳大利亚一般人群发病率相似,这可能表明该地区没有显着差异。然而,证据并未说明 HPV 疫苗接种 感染率低的原因或 疫苗接种 在预防 HPV 相关疾病方面的有效性。
科研与实践启示
该研究表明,需要对针对 HPV 的足够 疫苗接种 进行审查,以预防 HPV 相关肿瘤,特别是在不含替诺福韦的抗逆转录病毒治疗方案和常规肛门癌筛查的背景下。这表明需要针对 HIV 阳性个体进行针对性干预并改进 HPV 监测 中的 监测。

Brief communication: hepatitis B, human papillomavirus, pneumococcal and influenza vaccination in an Australian metropolitan HIV service

Authors: Sophie Sparkes, Nicholas Leedman, Michael Taggart
Original abstract (English)
People living with HIV experience a disproportionate burden of vaccine-preventable infections. This retrospective audit assessed the proportion of patients with adequate vaccination against hepatitis B, human papillomavirus, pneumococcus and influenza within an Australian HIV service. There was a significantly higher proportion ( p < 0.001) with adequate hepatitis B vaccination (47.9%), compared to pneumococcal conjugate (19.0%), pneumococcal polysaccharide (6.6%), and human papillomavirus (7.4%) vaccination. Influenza vaccination rates (30.6%) were similar to general population rates in Western Australia. Adequate vaccination requires review to prevent hepatitis B acquisition and HPV-associated neoplasia, particularly in the context of tenofovir-free antiretroviral regimens and routine anal cancer screening.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study focuses on the vaccination status of people living with HIV against hepatitis B, human papillomavirus (HPV), pneumococcus, and influenza. The central question is whether these patients have adequate vaccination coverage against these diseases, particularly in the context of antiretroviral regimens and cancer screening.
Evidence base and method
The review is a retrospective audit conducted within an Australian HIV service. It assessed the proportion of patients with adequate vaccination against hepatitis B, human papillomavirus, pneumococcus, and influenza. The study design involved analyzing existing records of vaccination status without explicitly reporting on meta-analysis or consensus processes.
Consensus and conclusions
The study found that there was a significantly higher proportion of patients with adequate hepatitis B vaccination (47.9%) compared to pneumococcal conjugate (19.0%), pneumococcal polysaccharide (6.6%), and human papillomavirus (7.4%) vaccination. These findings highlight disparities in vaccination coverage among these diseases in the HIV population.
Controversies and gaps
The study notes that influenza vaccination rates (30.6%) were similar to general population rates in Western Australia, which may suggest that there is no significant disparity in this area. However, the evidence does not address the reasons behind the low rates of HPV vaccination or the effectiveness of vaccination in preventing HPV-associated diseases.
Research and practice implications
The study implies that adequate vaccination against HPV requires review to prevent HPV-associated neoplasia, especially in the context of tenofovir-free antiretroviral regimens and routine anal cancer screening. This suggests a need for targeted interventions and improved surveillance in HPV vaccination for HIV-positive individuals.

人乳头瘤病毒 相关癌症和局部细菌群的潜在作用

Human Papillomavirus-Associated Cancers and the Potential Role of Local Bacterial Populations
作者： Amitabha Ray, Daniel M. Borsch, Rajashree Pandit
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是美国乃至全球最常见的性传播传染病。本综述旨在检查与 HPV 相关的肿瘤疾病,特别关注宫颈癌和口咽癌。在 高危型HPV 组(即可引起异常细胞变化并导致恶性肿瘤的 HPV 类型)中,HPV 基因型 16 和 18 在 宫颈癌 中常见,而 HPV-16 是口咽癌中检测最多的 HPV。据了解,HPV 基因组包含三个主要功能区:早期区,编码E6、E7等蛋白;长控制区;和晚期区域,编码结构衣壳蛋白 L1 和 L2。E6 和 E7 蛋白对于致癌作用至关重要。E6 促进肿瘤抑制因子 p53 的降解,而 E7 则使视网膜母细胞瘤蛋白失活,严重破坏细胞周期调节并导致增殖不受控制。这些破坏促进了 HPV 感染细胞的存活——这是病毒生命周期的要求。病毒 L1 是主要衣壳成分,用于分类、PCR 检测、抗原测试和预防性 疫苗 制备。然而,将 HPV 的作用与不同的危险因素联系起来非常重要,特别是考虑到不同国家和地理区域的 宫颈癌 发病率 比率存在很大差异时。与宫颈癌相比,HPV阳性口咽癌危险因素被认为主要与性行为有关。总体而言,宫颈阴道区域和口腔的局部微生物环境可能在癌症的发展中发挥重要作用。例如,某些细菌在疾病过程中有所增加,例如口咽癌中的梭杆菌或细菌性阴道病中的普雷沃氏菌和加德纳氏菌。另一方面,宫颈阴道环境中有益细菌的消耗可能会导致宫颈肿瘤的发生。就 宫颈癌 而言,所有危险因素都可能对宫颈阴道微生物菌群产生不利改变。厌氧菌占优势,可引起病理变化。据报道,与疾病进展相关的是,乳酸菌消耗水平不断增加。同样,在不同的头颈癌中,研究人员记录了 HPV 阳性和 HPV 阴性病例之间细菌属的差异。此外,一些研究人员认为某些细菌可以作为口腔区域的癌症生物标志物。各种细菌存在于整个胃肠道中,特别是在大肠(结肠)中,它们在那里形成最大的种群,并且影响肠道内外的多种病理生理过程。肠道微生物群是其变化如何与包括癌症发展在内的病理过程相关的典型例子。关于它们对其他器官的影响,已经提出了不同的双向通信网络。一个相关的例子是肠道-阴道轴,它连接这两个器官的微生物群落。目前,越来越多的研究正在尝试操纵该轴并以有利的方式重建宫颈阴道微生物菌群。因此,适当改变局部微生物环境可以以经济的方式预防病理过程。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述旨在检查与 HPV 相关的肿瘤疾病,特别关注宫颈癌和口咽癌。
证据基础与方法
该综述讨论了 HPV 在癌症发展中的作用,包括 HPV 基因组 的结构及其蛋白质的功能。它还提到了病毒 L1 蛋白在分类、PCR 检测、抗原测试以及预防性 疫苗 制备中的用途。
共识与结论
在高危型HPV组中,HPV基因型16和18在宫颈癌中常见,而HPV-16是口咽癌中检测到最多的HPV。E6和E7蛋白对于癌变至关重要,E6促进肿瘤抑制因子p53的降解,而E7使视网膜母细胞瘤蛋白失活,破坏细胞周期调节并导致不受控制的增殖。这些破坏促进了 HPV 感染细胞的存活,这是病毒生命周期的要求。
争议与缺口
将 HPV 的作用与不同的风险因素关联起来非常重要,特别是考虑到不同国家和地理区域的 宫颈癌 发病率 比率存在很大差异时。厌氧菌的优势可能会带来病理变化,据报道,乳酸菌消耗水平的增加与疾病进展有关。此外,不同头颈癌中 HPV 阳性和 HPV 阴性病例之间的细菌属存在差异。
科研与实践启示
适当改变当地的微生物环境可以以经济的方式预防病理过程。目前,越来越多的研究正在尝试操纵肠道-阴道轴并以有利的方式重建宫颈阴道微生物菌群。

Human Papillomavirus-Associated Cancers and the Potential Role of Local Bacterial Populations

Authors: Amitabha Ray, Daniel M. Borsch, Rajashree Pandit
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the most common sexually transmitted infectious agent in the United States as well as globally. This review aims to examine neoplastic diseases associated with HPV with special emphasis on cancers of the uterine cervix and oropharynx. Among the group of high-risk HPVs, i.e., types of HPV that can cause abnormal cellular changes leading to malignancies, HPV genotypes 16 and 18 are commonly found in cervical cancer, whereas HPV-16 is the most detected HPV in oropharyngeal cancer. It is known that the HPV genome comprises three major functional regions: the early region, which encodes proteins such as E6 and E7; the long control region; and the late region, which encodes structural capsid proteins L1 and L2. The E6 and E7 proteins are pivotal to carcinogenesis. E6 promotes degradation of the tumor suppressor p53, while E7 inactivates the retinoblastoma protein, critically disrupting cell cycle regulation and enabling uncontrolled proliferation. These disruptions promote survival of HPV-infected cells–a requirement for the viral life cycle. The viral L1, the major capsid component, is used for classification, PCR detection, antigenic tests, and in prophylactic vaccine preparation. However, it is important to correlate the role of HPV with different risk factors, particularly when considering a wide variation in cervical cancer incidence rates in different nations and geographic regions. Compared to cervical cancer, HPV-positive oropharyngeal cancer risk factors are thought to be associated with sexual behavior primarily. Overall, the local microbial environment of both the cervicovaginal region and the oral cavity may play a significant role in cancer development. For example, an increase in certain bacteria has been documented in the course of disease, such as Fusobacterium in oropharyngeal cancer or Prevotella and Gardnerella in bacterial vaginosis. On the other hand, a depletion of beneficial bacteria in the cervicovaginal environment may contribute to the development of cervical neoplasia. In the case of cervical cancer, all risk factors possibly alter the cervicovaginal microbial flora adversely. The dominance of anaerobic bacteria could bring about pathological changes. In relation to the disease progression, an increasing level of Lactobacillus depletion has been reported. Similarly, in different head and neck cancers, investigators documented differences in bacterial genera between HPV-positive and HPV-negative cases. Furthermore, some investigators suggested that certain bacteria could serve as cancer biomarkers in oral regions. Various bacteria are present throughout the gastrointestinal tract, particularly in the large intestine (colon), where they form the largest populations, and they influence diverse pathophysiological processes both inside and outside the gut. The gut microbiota is a typical example of how alterations in it could be associated with pathological processes, including cancer development. Regarding their impact on other organs, different bidirectional communication networks have been proposed. One pertinent example is the gut–vaginal axis, which connects the microbial communities of these two organs. Currently, a growing number of studies are trying to manipulate this axis and reconstitute the cervicovaginal microbial flora in a favorable manner. Therefore, appropriate modifications of the local microbial environment could prevent pathological processes in an economical way.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review aims to examine neoplastic diseases associated with HPV with special emphasis on cancers of the uterine cervix and oropharynx.
Evidence base and method
The review discusses the role of HPV in cancer development, including the structure of the HPV genome and the function of its proteins. It also mentions the use of the viral L1 protein for classification, PCR detection, antigenic tests, and in prophylactic vaccine preparation.
Consensus and conclusions
Among the group of high-risk HPVs, HPV genotypes 16 and 18 are commonly found in cervical cancer, whereas HPV-16 is the most detected HPV in oropharyngeal cancer. The E6 and E7 proteins are pivotal to carcinogenesis, with E6 promoting degradation of the tumor suppressor p53 and E7 inactivating the retinoblastoma protein, disrupting cell cycle regulation and enabling uncontrolled proliferation. These disruptions promote survival of HPV-infected cells, a requirement for the viral life cycle.
Controversies and gaps
It is important to correlate the role of HPV with different risk factors, particularly when considering a wide variation in cervical cancer incidence rates in different nations and geographic regions. The dominance of anaerobic bacteria could bring about pathological changes, and an increasing level of Lactobacillus depletion has been reported in relation to disease progression. Additionally, differences in bacterial genera between HPV-positive and HPV-negative cases have been documented in different head and neck cancers.
Research and practice implications
Appropriate modifications of the local microbial environment could prevent pathological processes in an economical way. Currently, a growing number of studies are trying to manipulate the gut–vaginal axis and reconstitute the cervicovaginal microbial flora in a favorable manner.
